<script>
/* ============================================================================
   HELL FARMERS
   Farm hub -> portal -> Hall of Mirrors (5 rooms) -> Boss Gate -> home (Day+1)
   20 segments · 100 rooms · 21 bosses · mouse aim · dash charges
   ========================================================================= */

/* ==========================================================================
   THE SCREEN vs THE ARENA

   640x360 is the ONLY 640-wide base that fills a 16:9 monitor completely:
   it IS 16:9, so it lands on 1920x1080 at exactly 3x, 1440p at 4x and 4K at 6x
   with no letterboxing in either axis. 640x320 (2:1) filled the width but left
   11% of the HEIGHT black on every 16:9 screen there is.

   The catch: 360 / 16 = 22.5 TILES. There is no whole number of 16px rows in a
   360px-tall screen, so you cannot simply widen the arena to fit.

   So the screen and the arena are now two different things:

     THE ARENA  640x320  — 40x20 tiles. UNCHANGED. Every clamp, every spawn,
                           every boss pattern and the whole pathfinder still
                           speak in these coordinates, so none of them had to
                           move and none of them could break.
     THE HUD     640x40  — the 40px that 16:9 hands us for free, spent on a
                           dedicated band. The HUD used to be painted ON TOP of
                           the play area — hearts and text over the floor you
                           were fighting on. Now it has its own room.
     THE SCREEN 640x360  — exactly 16:9. Fills the glass.
   ========================================================================= */
const TILE=16,VIEW_W=640,VIEW_H=320,COLS=40,ROWS=20;   // VIEW_* is THE ARENA
const HUD_H=40;                                        // the band beneath it
const SCREEN_W=VIEW_W, SCREEN_H=VIEW_H+HUD_H;          // 640x360 — exact 16:9
const CHAR=32;

/* ==========================================================================
   CHARACTER SPRITES
   Hand-drawn art, embedded as a base64 sheet so the game stays ONE file — no
   loose PNGs to lose, no second fetch to fail.

   The source frames were 48x48 with real detail at that size (41% of their 2x2
   blocks were non-uniform, so they were NOT a doubled 24px image and could not
   be halved losslessly). They were resampled to 32 to match CHAR, then snapped
   back onto the original palette so they stay crisp pixel art instead of
   turning to mush.

   SHEET LAYOUT — 8 columns x 4 rows, 32px cells:
     col 0-1 : idle     (2 frames)
     col 2-5 : walk     (4 frames)
     col 6   : WIND-UP  (coiled away from the target)
     col 7   : STRIKE   (driving through it)
     row 0   : FRONT (facing the camera / down)
     row 1   : BACK  (away / up)
     row 2   : LEFT
     row 3   : RIGHT

   THE ATTACK FRAMES ARE DERIVED, NOT DRAWN.
   There were no attack poses in the art. Rather than leave everyone firing a
   weapon from a walk cycle, each attack frame is BENT out of the character's
   own most-planted walk frame: the feet stay on the floor and the torso leans —
   away to coil, then through to strike. Inverse-mapped, so the bend cannot tear
   holes in the body. It is the character's own pixels, own palette, own armour;
   only the posture is new.
   ========================================================================= */
const SPR_W=32, SPR_H=32, SPR_COLS=8;
const SPR_WINDUP=6, SPR_STRIKE=7;
const SPR_ROW={front:0, back:1, left:2, right:3};

const SHEETS={};                 // heroId -> {img, ok}
let spriteScratch=null;          // offscreen canvas, used for tinting

function loadSheet(id, data){
  const img=new Image();
  const rec={img, ok:false};
  img.onload =()=>{ rec.ok=true; };
  img.onerror=()=>{ rec.ok=false; };   // fall back to the procedural body
  img.src=data;
  SHEETS[id]=rec;
}

/* ---- THE SEVEN HEIRS ----
   One sheet each, 24 frames, embedded. Saved as PALETTE PNGs at 64 colours
   rather than RGBA: these are 32px sprites and they were carrying 650-730
   colours apiece, which the eye cannot use and the file has to pay for.
   Indexed, they are a QUARTER of the size and visually identical —
   75 KB for all seven, where RGBA wanted 269 KB. */
loadSheet('CAELUM', 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAQAAAACACAMAAADTa0c4AAAAwFBMVEUAAAC9wL6ZoZ+Nh4F8fXZ4c  …[TRUNCATED 11884 chars]
loadSheet('MARINA', 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAQAAAACACAMAAADTa0c4AAAAwFBMVEUAAADLyLqpo5Opk4SOj4Jyo  …[TRUNCATED 11860 chars]
loadSheet('MINERVA', 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAQAAAACACAMAAADTa0c4AAAAwFBMVEUAAADR0MaroZiSkIiCgXKC  …[TRUNCATED 11343 chars]
loadSheet('SOLON', 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAQAAAACACAMAAADTa0c4AAAAwFBMVEUAAADryWXZq1fNoFTHk0OzmV  …[TRUNCATED 11181 chars]
loadSheet('FREYA', 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAQAAAACACAMAAADTa0c4AAAAwFBMVEUAAADNrIuchmOadF6NZlOJXE  …[TRUNCATED 12220 chars]
loadSheet('MERCER', 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAQAAAACACAMAAADTa0c4AAAAwFBMVEUAAADPwZqymnqclnWZimuZg  …[TRUNCATED 11030 chars]
loadSheet('GORE', 'data:image/png;base64,iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAQAAAACACAMAAADTa0c4AAAAwFBMVEUAAAB/fHJZVklbUElSS0VVREB  …[TRUNCATED 12302 chars]

const hasSheet = id => SHEETS[id] && SHEETS[id].ok;

/* Which way is she facing? She shoots with the mouse, so she LOOKS where she
   shoots — that's what reads correctly when you're aiming across the room. */
function facingOf(ax, ay, px_, py_){
  const dx=ax-px_, dy=ay-(py_-8);
  if(Math.abs(dx) > Math.abs(dy)) return dx<0 ? 'left' : 'right';
  return dy<0 ? 'back' : 'front';
}

/* Draw one cell of a sheet, optionally tinted. Tinting goes through an
   offscreen canvas with source-atop, so the colour lands ONLY on the character
   and not on a 32x32 square of the room behind her. */
function drawSprite(id, col, row, dx, dy, tint, alpha){
  const rec=SHEETS[id];
  if(!rec||!rec.ok) return false;
  const sx=col*SPR_W, sy=row*SPR_H;
  if(alpha!==undefined) g.globalAlpha=alpha;
  if(tint){
    if(!spriteScratch){
      spriteScratch=document.createElement('canvas');
      spriteScratch.width=SPR_W; spriteScratch.height=SPR_H;
    }
    const s=spriteScratch.getContext('2d');
    s.clearRect(0,0,SPR_W,SPR_H);
    s.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    s.drawImage(rec.img, sx,sy,SPR_W,SPR_H, 0,0,SPR_W,SPR_H);
    s.globalCompositeOperation='source-atop';
    s.fillStyle=tint; s.fillRect(0,0,SPR_W,SPR_H);
    s.globalCompositeOperation='source-over';
    g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    g.drawImage(spriteScratch, 0,0,SPR_W,SPR_H, dx,dy,SPR_W,SPR_H);
  } else {
    g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    g.drawImage(rec.img, sx,sy,SPR_W,SPR_H, dx,dy,SPR_W,SPR_H);
  }
  g.globalAlpha=1;
  return true;
}
const SEGMENTS=20, ROOMS_PER_SEG=5, TOTAL_ROOMS=SEGMENTS*ROOMS_PER_SEG;

/* ==========================================================================
   FIVE TIERS

   There were four, and the top one was doing two jobs at once: it held both the
   solid end-game relics (Duplication Mirror, Holy Mantle) AND the handful of
   items that simply WIN the run on the spot (Phantom Twin, Omega Drone, Blood
   Covenant). Scored by raw effect, the 56 of them spanned a range of TWELVE TO
   ONE. Rolling a 10% "top tier" drop and getting a Shattered Hourglass instead
   of a Quasar Shot is not a jackpot, it is a coin flip wearing gold paint.

   So the apex is its own tier now. The 18 strongest were promoted to MYTHIC, and
   MYTHIC drops FIVE PERCENT OF THE TIME. One in twenty. You will finish most
   runs without seeing one, and that is exactly what makes the one you do see
   worth remembering.

   The odds still sum to 100 — the 5% came out of the UNCOMMON share, not out of
   thin air, so the total flow of loot is unchanged. What changed is the shape of
   the tail. */
const TIER_ODDS=[[5,5],[4,10],[3,25],[2,35],[1,25]];

/* ONE naming table, not two. TIER_NAME said the top tier was "LEGENDARY" and the
   Town's own TIER_N said it was a "RELIC" — the same item was two different
   things depending on which screen you read it on. */
const TIER_COL={1:'#8a8a96', 2:'#5ac86a', 3:'#4a9ae8', 4:'#e0a020', 5:'#ff5ad8'};
const TIER_NAME={1:'COMMON', 2:'UNCOMMON', 3:'RARE', 4:'LEGENDARY', 5:'MYTHIC'};

const itemName=it=>(USE_SAFE_NAMES && SAFE[it.n]) ? SAFE[it.n] : it.n;
const POOL={1:[],2:[],3:[],4:[],5:[]};
ITEMS.forEach(it=>POOL[it.t].push(it));

/* Roll a tier, then a uniform item within it. */
function rollItem(forceTier){
  let t=1;
  /* The Void Walker's promise is 'the best drops', and before the split that
     meant tier 4 because tier 4 was the top. It still means the top: it now rolls
     a MYTHIC one time in three, and a LEGENDARY otherwise. */
  if(forceTier===true) t = Math.random()<0.34 ? 5 : 4;
  else if(typeof forceTier==='number') t=forceTier;
  else{
    let r=Math.random()*100;
    for(const [tier,odds] of TIER_ODDS){ if(r<odds){ t=tier; break; } r-=odds; }
  }
  const p=POOL[t];
  return p[Math.floor(Math.random()*p.length)];
}

const rand=(a=1,b=0)=>b+Math.random()*(a-b);
const dst=(a,b)=>Math.hypot(a.x-b.x,a.y-b.y);
const ang=(a,b)=>Math.atan2(b.y-a.y,b.x-a.x);
const clamp=(v,a,b)=>v<a?a:v>b?b:v;
const mul=a=>()=>{a|=0;a=a+0x6D2B79F5|0;let t=Math.imul(a^a>>>15,1|a);
  t=t+Math.imul(t^t>>>7,61|t)^t;return((t^t>>>14)>>>0)/4294967296;};
let rng=mul(1);

/* ---- frame clock / scheduler / forces (shared with the boss module) ---- */
let FMS=16, sched=[];
const every=(b,key,per,fn)=>{ const k='_'+key;
  b.s[k]=(b.s[k]||0)-FMS; if(b.s[k]<=0){ b.s[k]=per; fn(); } };
const after=(ms,fn)=>sched.push({t:ms,fn,tag:boss});
/* COSMETIC callbacks that must survive the death of the thing that scheduled
   them. after() tags with the current boss and the scheduler drops any callback
   whose tag is no longer current — correct for attacks (a dead boss shouldn't
   keep firing) but fatal for a death animation, which is scheduled BY the boss
   and must run AFTER it is gone. Tag null: it always fires. */
const afterFx=(ms,fn)=>sched.push({t:ms,fn,tag:null});
const pushField=(x,y)=>{ P.forceX+=x; P.forceY+=y; };
const impulse =(x,y)=>{ P.kbX+=x;    P.kbY+=y;    };
const slowTo  =(v)=>{ P.slowReq=Math.min(P.slowReq,v); };

   written there. What the doc left as prose, the engine now enforces: every
   one of these seven plays differently in the hand, not just on the page.
   ========================================================================= */

/* ==========================================================================
   ITEM LEVELS — the Anvil's whole reason to exist.

   `inv` holds REFERENCES into the shared ITEMS table (it is even saved as a list
   of indices into it). So an item in your bag is the SAME OBJECT as the item
   definition every future run will roll. Writing a level onto it would upgrade
   that relic permanently, for every character, forever — a save-corrupting bug
   dressed up as a feature.
   So levels live HERE, in a plain array aligned index-for-index with `inv`.
   Nothing in the item table is ever mutated. */
let lvls=[];                                    // lvls[i] is the level of inv[i]
const MAX_LVL=4;
const lvlOf = i => lvls[i]||1;

/* An item at level L is worth this much more than it was at level 1. Linear, and
   deliberately gentle: a level-4 relic is twice the relic, not eight times it. */
const lvlScale = L => 1 + (L-1)/3;              // 1.00 / 1.33 / 1.67 / 2.00 exactly

/* ==========================================================================
   POTIONS
   A rare drop from ORDINARY enemies — not elites, not bosses. Elites already
   pay in shards and relics; the grub you almost ignored is what saves you.
   Carry up to 3. Press Q to drink: restores HALF your maximum health.
   It heals a FRACTION, not a fixed amount, so it stays relevant at 3 hearts
   and at 8.
   ========================================================================== */
const POTION_MAX=3;
const POTION_CHANCE=0.045;      // ~1 in 22 ordinary kills
let potions=0;

function drinkPotion(){
  if(potions<=0){ say('NO POTIONS'); return false; }
  if(P.hp>=stats.maxHp){ say('ALREADY AT FULL HEALTH'); return false; }
  potions--;
  RUN.potions++;
  const heal=Math.max(1, Math.ceil(stats.maxHp/2));      // half the bar, rounded up
  const before=P.hp;
  P.hp=Math.min(stats.maxHp, P.hp+heal);
  const got=P.hp-before;
  for(let i=0;i<10;i++) burst(P.x,P.y,1,C.crop);
  say(`POTION — +${(got/2).toFixed(1)} HEARTS  (${potions} left)`);
  return true;
}
let pausedFrom=S.FARM;

/* THE RUN SEED.
   Room generation used to be seeded from the room NUMBER alone, which meant
   every playthrough produced the identical 100 rooms — same layouts, same
   conditions, same biomes, same drops, forever. In a roguelite.
   Now each run rolls its own seed. Rooms are still deterministic WITHIN a run
   (so a save can resume mid-room and regenerate it exactly), but no two runs
   are the same. */
let runSeed = (Math.random()*0x7fffffff)|0;

/* Pause is only legal while a run is actually happening. */
const canPause=()=>mode===S.FARM||mode===S.HALL||mode===S.BOSS||mode===S.SKILL;

function pauseGame(){
  if(!canPause()) return;
  pausedFrom=mode; mode=S.PAUSE; menuSel=0;
  K.clear(); P.firing=false;          // don't hold a key/trigger through the pause
}
function resumeGame(){
  if(mode!==S.PAUSE) return;
  mode=pausedFrom;
  K.clear(); P.firing=false;
  /* Clear any hit-stop. If you paused during the freeze of a hit, you would
     otherwise come back to a game that is STILL frozen for a few frames — you
     press resume and nothing moves. A freeze is punctuation on a moment that
     has already passed; it should not survive the pause menu. */
  hitStop=0; slowMo=0;
  P.iframe=Math.max(P.iframe,400);    // a moment of grace on the way back in
}

/* ==========================================================================
   MENUS
   One system, used by the title, the pause screen, the death screen, the win
   screen and the skill web. Every one of them is fully mouse-driven: hover to
   highlight, click to pick. The keyboard still works — it just isn't required.
   ========================================================================= */
let menuSel=0;

/* DEAD/WIN sit at the BOTTOM now: the summary above them fills the screen. */
const MENU_Y={ [S.TITLE]:128, [S.PAUSE]:112, [S.DEAD]:250, [S.WIN]:250 };
const menuRow=(i,m)=>({ X:VIEW_W/2-124, Y:(MENU_Y[m===undefined?mode:m]||130)+i*26, W:248, H:22 });

const PAUSE_MENU=[
  {k:'CONTINUE',      d:'back to it',                     go:()=>resumeGame()},
  {k:'THE COMPENDIUM',d:'every item in the game, and where it comes from', go:()=>toCodex()},
  {k:'SAVE',          d:'write your progress, keep playing',
   go:async()=>{ await saveRun(); say('PROGRESS SAVED'); resumeGame(); }},
  {k:'SAVE AND EXIT', d:'save, then back to the title screen',
   go:async()=>{ await saveRun(); toTitle(); }},
  {k:'ABANDON RUN',   d:'quit WITHOUT saving — the run is lost', danger:1,
   go:()=>{ toTitle(); }},
];

const DEAD_MENU=[
  {k:'NEW RUN',       d:'roll the die again', go:()=>{ newGame(); }},
  {k:'TITLE SCREEN',  d:'back to the start',  go:()=>{ toTitle(); }},
];
const WIN_MENU=[
  {k:'NEW RUN',       d:'again, harder',      go:()=>{ newGame(); }},
  {k:'TITLE SCREEN',  d:'back to the start',  go:()=>{ toTitle(); }},
];


function titleMenu(){
  const m=[];
  if(hasSave) m.push({k:'CONTINUE RUN', d:'pick up where you left off', hot:1,
                      go:()=>Store.get(SAVE_KEY).then(raw=>{
                        try{ deserialise(raw); }catch(e){ say('SAVE CORRUPT'); newGame(); } })});
  m.push({k:'NEW RUN', d: hasSave?'this abandons the run you have saved':'descend into Hell',
          go:()=>{ newGame(); clearSave(); }});
  m.push({k:'THE COMPENDIUM', d:'every item in the game, and where it comes from',
          go:()=>toCodex()});
  m.push({k:'WATCH THE OPENING', d:'the betrayal, the prophecy, the reality',
          go:()=>startIntro()});
  if(hasSave) m.push({k:'DELETE SAVE', d:'erase it', danger:1, go:()=>{ clearSave(); menuSel=0; }});
  return m;
}

/* ==========================================================================
   THE COMPENDIUM — every item in the game, and where it actually comes from.

   A NOTE ON HONESTY: items are NOT tied to bosses. Nothing in this game says
   "the Minotaur drops the Titan's Muscle". Every drop rolls the same tier table,
   and where you are standing when it rolls does not change what comes out. So
   this screen does not invent a link that is not there. It tells you the truth,
   which is more useful anyway: WHAT ROLLS THE TABLE, and WHAT GUARANTEES A TIER.
   ========================================================================== */

/* Where loot actually comes from, read straight out of the code that drops it. */
const SOURCES=[
  {n:'EVERY BOSS KILL',  c:'#e0603c', d:'rolls the tier table. Duplication Mirror doubles it.'},
  {n:'ELITE KILL',       c:'#c04a8a', d:'35% chance to roll the table.'},
  {n:'THE GAUNTLET door',c:'#e0603c', d:'a GUARANTEED RARE. two elites are the price.'},
  {n:'THE CURSED door',  c:'#8a3ac8', d:'a GUARANTEED LEGENDARY. and a curse, forever.'},
  {n:'THE TRIAL door',   c:'#4a9ae8', d:'55% chance to roll the table, plus double shards.'},
  {n:'THE MARKET',       c:'#e0b93c', d:'buy outright. the apex slot is a MYTHIC one day in four.'},
  {n:'THE FORGE',        c:'#ff8a5a', d:'melt three of a tier into one of the next. up to MYTHIC.'},
  {n:'THE VOID WALKER',  c:'#ff5ad8', d:'a relic that forces your NEXT drop to the top of the table.'},
];

/* which of those can actually hand you an item of tier t */
function sourcesFor(t){
  const s=['EVERY BOSS KILL','ELITE KILL','THE TRIAL door','THE MARKET'];
  if(t===3) s.unshift('THE GAUNTLET door \u2014 GUARANTEED');
  if(t===4) s.unshift('THE CURSED door \u2014 GUARANTEED');
  if(t>=4)  s.push('THE VOID WALKER');
  if(t>1)   s.push('THE FORGE (three '+TIER_NAME[t-1]+')');
  return s;
}
const TIER_ODD = t => (TIER_ODDS.find(o=>o[0]===t)||[0,0])[1];

let codexTier=0;        // 0 = all, else 1-5
let codexSel=0;         // which item is highlighted
let codexScroll=0;
let codexFrom=S.TITLE;  // where to go back to

function codexList(){
  const l = codexTier ? ITEMS.filter(i=>i.t===codexTier) : ITEMS.slice();
  return l.sort((a,b)=> a.t-b.t || a.n.localeCompare(b.n));
}
function toCodex(){ codexFrom=mode; mode=S.CODEX; codexSel=0; codexScroll=0; }

const CODEX_ROWS=11;
const codexRow=i=>({X:12, Y:60+i*22, W:300, H:20});

function codexHit(mx,my){
  for(let t=0;t<=5;t++){
    const b={X:12+t*46, Y:32, W:42, H:18};
    if(mx>=b.X&&mx<b.X+b.W&&my>=b.Y&&my<b.Y+b.H) return {tier:t};
  }
  for(let i=0;i<CODEX_ROWS;i++){ const r=codexRow(i);
    if(mx>=r.X&&mx<r.X+r.W&&my>=r.Y&&my<r.Y+r.H) return {row:codexScroll+i}; }
  return null;
}

/* These effects are written as PERCENTAGES on the item card (+10% Speed) but
   stored as fractions on the stat sheet (spd 0.10). It lived inside
   recalcStats, which meant the Compendium had no way to know that "+10" meant
   ten PERCENT — so the ladder would have printed a bare "+10" next to a flat
   "+2 Armor" and quietly implied they were the same kind of number. */
const PCT={spd:100, crit:100, range:100, block:100, stun:100, freeze:100,
           insta:100, corrode:100, extra:100, healKill:100,
           shardGain:100, tradeVal:100, pChance:100};

/* ==========================================================================
   WHAT AN ITEM IS WORTH AT EACH LEVEL

   ONE function, used by BOTH the Anvil (which charges you) and the Compendium
   (which promises you). If these two ever disagreed, the shop would be lying —
   so they are not allowed to be two functions. */
/* WHICH DIRECTION HURTS?
   For almost every stat, a negative number is the bad news. fireCd is INVERTED:
   -8 means eight percent LESS cooldown (good), +35 means thirty-five percent MORE
   (bad). Keying the drawback rule on the SIGN alone got this exactly backwards —
   the Charging Piercing Blade's +35 cooldown scaled to +70 at level 4, and a
   MYTHIC became WORSE THAN CARRYING NOTHING the more you paid to sharpen it. */
const BAD_WHEN_POSITIVE = new Set(['fireCd']);
const isDrawback = (key, v) =>
  BAD_WHEN_POSITIVE.has(key) ? v > 0 : v < 0;

function effAt(it, key, L){
  const v=it.e[key];
  const S=lvlScale(L);
  if(key==='dyn')    return null;                    // a keyword, not a number
  if(key==='dmgMul') return 1 + (v-1)*S;             // the BONUS scales, not the whole
  if(key==='heal'||key==='shards') return v;         // paid once, at pickup. levels do nothing.

  /* ===== A DRAWBACK DOES NOT GROW =====
     Scaling EVERY number meant levelling an item made its downside worse too.
     The Blood Covenant (x3 damage, -6 max HP) became -12 max HP at level 4 — and
     since stats.maxHp is floored at 1, the game quietly applied -5 while the
     Compendium promised -12. You would have paid 1,314 shards to be left on half
     a heart.
     You paid to IMPROVE the relic. The upside scales. The price stays what it was
     when you signed. */
  if(isDrawback(key, v)) return v;

  return v*S;
}

/* does levelling change THIS effect on THIS item? (a drawback does not) */
function scalesFor(it, key){
  if(key==='dyn'||key==='heal'||key==='shards') return false;
  return !isDrawback(key, it.e[key]);
}
/* the key-only version, for the places that do not have the item to hand */
const scales = key => key!=='dyn' && key!=='heal' && key!=='shards';

/* pretty-print a value for a given effect */
function effStr(it, key, L){
  const v=effAt(it,key,L);
  if(v===null) return 'unique';
  if(key==='dmgMul') return '\u00d7'+ (Math.round(v*100)/100);
  const r = Math.round(v*10)/10;
  return (r>0?'+':'')+r+(PCT[key]?'%':'');   // a percent is not a flat number
}

/* the value an item gives, spelled out from its effect table */
const EFF_LABEL={
  dmg:'Damage', dmgMul:'Damage MULTIPLIER', maxHp:'Max HP (half-hearts)', armor:'Armor',
  spd:'Speed', rate:'Fire rate', shotSpd:'Shot speed', tearSpd:'Shot speed (flat)',
  range:'Range', crit:'Crit chance', critD:'Crit damage', pierce:'Pierce',
  wisp:'Familiars', homing:'Homing', extra:'Extra shots', block:'Block chance',
  insta:'Instant-kill chance', corrode:'Corrode', thorns:'Thorns', dot:'Poison damage',
  pDmg:'Poison damage', pChance:'Poison chance', stun:'Stun chance', freeze:'Freeze chance',
  knock:'Knockback', heal:'Heals on pickup', shards:'Shards on pickup',
  shardGain:'Shard gains', tradeVal:'Trade value', yield:'Crop yield', growth:'Crop growth',
  dashCh:'Dash charges', dashCd:'Dash cooldown', auto:'Auto-fire', revive:'Revives',
  fireCd:'Fire cooldown', healKill:'Heal on kill', luck:'Luck', dyn:'UNIQUE',
};

function drawCodex(){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'#0b080e');
  txt('THE COMPENDIUM', 12, 8, C.bone, 12);
  txt(ITEMS.length+' items \u00b7 five tiers \u00b7 \u2191\u2193 scroll \u00b7 0-5 filter \u00b7 ESC back',
      SCREEN_W-268, 12, '#4a3f4a', 7);

  /* the tier filter */
  const tabs=['ALL','I','II','III','IV','V'];
  for(let t=0;t<=5;t++){
    const b={X:12+t*46, Y:32, W:42, H:18}, on=t===codexTier;
    const col = t===0 ? C.bone : TIER_COL[t];
    px(b.X,b.Y,b.W,b.H, on?'#241c26':'#140f18');
    px(b.X,b.Y, on?3:1, b.H, col);
    txt(tabs[t], b.X+8, b.Y+4, on?'#fff':col, 8);
    if(t>0) txt(String(POOL[t].length), b.X+b.W-14, b.Y+5, '#4a3f4a', 6);
  }

  const list=codexList();
  if(codexSel>=list.length) codexSel=Math.max(0,list.length-1);
  /* keep the highlighted row on screen */
  if(codexSel<codexScroll) codexScroll=codexSel;
  if(codexSel>=codexScroll+CODEX_ROWS) codexScroll=codexSel-CODEX_ROWS+1;
  codexScroll=clamp(codexScroll, 0, Math.max(0,list.length-CODEX_ROWS));

  for(let i=0;i<CODEX_ROWS;i++){
    const k=codexScroll+i, it=list[k];
    if(!it) break;
    const r=codexRow(i), sel=k===codexSel;
    px(r.X,r.Y,r.W,r.H, sel?'#241c26':'#110d14');
    px(r.X,r.Y, sel?3:1, r.H, TIER_COL[it.t]);
    txt(it.n.slice(0,32), r.X+10, r.Y+5, sel?'#fff':'#b0a4b0', 8);
    txt(TIER_NAME[it.t].slice(0,3), r.X+r.W-30, r.Y+6, TIER_COL[it.t], 6);
  }

  /* the scrollbar — you should be able to see how deep the list goes */
  if(list.length>CODEX_ROWS){
    const trackH=CODEX_ROWS*22-2, barH=Math.max(14, trackH*CODEX_ROWS/list.length);
    const barY=60 + (trackH-barH)*(codexScroll/Math.max(1,list.length-CODEX_ROWS));
    px(316, 60, 3, trackH, '#1e1822');
    px(316, barY, 3, barH, '#5a4a5e');
  }
  txt((codexSel+1)+' / '+list.length, 12, SCREEN_H-16, '#4a3f4a', 7);

  /* ---------------- THE DETAIL PANEL ---------------- */
  const it=list[codexSel];
  if(!it) return;
  const X=330, W=SCREEN_W-X-12;
  px(X, 32, W, SCREEN_H-46, '#120e16');
  px(X, 32, 3, SCREEN_H-46, TIER_COL[it.t]);

  txt(it.n, X+12, 40, '#fff', 11);
  txt(TIER_NAME[it.t], X+12, 56, TIER_COL[it.t], 8);
  txt(TIER_ODD(it.t)+'% of every roll', X+12+TIER_NAME[it.t].length*5+12, 57, '#5a4a5e', 7);

  /* the flavour line the item already carries */
  let y=74;
  const words=it.d.split(' ');
  let line='';
  for(const w of words){
    if((line+' '+w).length>44){ txt(line, X+12, y, C.dim, 7); y+=10; line=w; }
    else line = line? line+' '+w : w;
  }
  if(line) txt(line, X+12, y, C.dim, 7);
  y+=16;

  /* ================= THE UPGRADE LADDER =================
     Every level of the item, and exactly what it is worth at each one. These are
     not decorative numbers — they are computed by effAt(), the SAME function
     recalcStats uses to actually apply them. What this panel promises is what the
     Anvil delivers. */
  txt('THE ANVIL \u2014 WHAT EACH LEVEL BUYS YOU', X+12, y, C.sulfur, 7); y+=13;

  const LX=[X+120, X+164, X+208, X+252];        // the four level columns
  for(let L=1;L<=MAX_LVL;L++)
    txt('LV'+L, LX[L-1], y, L===1?'#8a7a8a':'#9adcff', 7);
  y+=11;

  /* what it COSTS to stand in each column */
  txt('cost', X+16, y, '#5a4a5e', 6);
  for(let L=1;L<=MAX_LVL;L++){
    const c = L===1 ? '\u2014' : String(Math.round(ANVIL_BASE[L]*ANVIL_TIER[it.t]));
    txt(c, LX[L-1], y, L===1?'#4a3f4a':C.gold, 6);
  }
  y+=12;

  const keys=Object.keys(it.e);
  if(!keys.length){ txt('nothing. it is a memento.', X+16, y, '#4a3f4a', 7); y+=11; }

  for(const k of keys){
    if(y>SCREEN_H-58) { txt('...', X+16, y, '#4a3f4a', 7); y+=10; break; }
    const label=(EFF_LABEL[k]||k);
    const drawback = isDrawback(k, it.e[k]);
    txt(label.slice(0,17), X+16, y, drawback?'#c0304a':'#b0a4b0', 6);
    if(!scalesFor(it,k)){
      /* be honest about what levelling does NOT touch */
      const why = k==='dyn'   ? 'a rule of its own'
                : drawback    ? effStr(it,k,1)+'  \u2014 the price does not grow'
                :               'once, on pickup \u2014 levels do nothing';
      txt(why, LX[0], y, drawback?'#c0304a':'#5a4a5e', 6);
    } else {
      for(let L=1;L<=MAX_LVL;L++){
        const s=effStr(it,k,L);
        const neg = effAt(it,k,L) < (k==='dmgMul'?1:0);
        txt(s, LX[L-1], y, L===1 ? '#8a7a8a' : (neg?'#c0304a':C.crop), 6);
      }
    }
    y+=10;
  }
  y+=6;

  /* the bottom line: what the whole climb costs */
  const total=[2,3,4].reduce((a,L)=>a+Math.round(ANVIL_BASE[L]*ANVIL_TIER[it.t]),0);
  const anyScale = keys.some(k=>scalesFor(it,k));
  if(anyScale) txt('\u25c6 '+total+' shards takes it all the way to LV4 (a run pays ~1300)',
                   X+16, y, '#7a6a4a', 6);
  else         txt('this one gains NOTHING from the anvil. do not waste your shards.',
                   X+16, y, '#c0304a', 6);
  y+=14;

  /* WHERE IT COMES FROM — the honest answer */
  txt('WHERE IT COMES FROM', X+12, y, C.sulfur, 7); y+=11;
  for(const s of sourcesFor(it.t)){
    if(y>SCREEN_H-16) break;
    const guaranteed = s.includes('GUARANTEED');
    txt('\u00b7 '+s, X+16, y, guaranteed?C.gold:'#8a7a8a', 6);
    y+=9;
  }
}

function currentMenu(){
  if(mode===S.TITLE) return titleMenu();
  if(mode===S.PAUSE) return PAUSE_MENU;
  if(mode===S.DEAD)  return DEAD_MENU;
  if(mode===S.WIN)   return WIN_MENU;
  return null;
}

function toTitle(){ mode=S.TITLE; menuSel=0; }

/* ==========================================================================
   CHARACTER SELECT
   The first real decision of a run, and it happens before you take a step.
   Seven bloodlines, seven weapons, seven ways to hold the same Hell.
   ========================================================================= */
let selIdx=0;

function toSelect(){ mode=S.SELECT; selIdx=0; }

const heroCard=i=>({ X:14+i*88, Y:56, W:82, H:64 });

function selectHit(mx,my){
  for(let i=0;i<CHARS.length;i++){
    const r=heroCard(i);
    if(mx>=r.X&&mx<r.X+r.W&&my>=r.Y&&my<r.Y+r.H) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function pickHero(i){
  hero=CHARS[clamp(i,0,CHARS.length-1)];
  beginRun();
}

function drawSelect(){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'#0a070c');
  g.textAlign='center';
  txtC('CHOOSE YOUR BLOOD', VIEW_W/2, 14, C.bone, 12);
  txtC('seven families trapped the gods. you are an heir to one of them.',
       VIEW_W/2, 30, C.dim, 8);
  txtC('\u2190 \u2192 or CLICK  ·  ENTER to descend', VIEW_W/2, 40, '#5a4a5a', 7);
  g.textAlign='left';

  CHARS.forEach((h,i)=>{
    const r=heroCard(i), sel=i===selIdx;
    px(r.X,r.Y,r.W,r.H, sel?'#241c26':'#120e16');
    px(r.X,r.Y, sel?3:1, r.H, h.col);
    if(sel){ px(r.X,r.Y,r.W,2,h.col); px(r.X,r.Y+r.H-2,r.W,2,h.col); }

    /* If this bloodline has hand-drawn art, SHOW HER. A sigil is a placeholder
       for a face you don't have yet. */
    const cx=r.X+r.W/2, cy=r.Y+26;
    if(hasSheet(h.id)){
      const idle=Math.floor(now/420)%2;                       // she breathes
      g.globalAlpha=sel?1:.6;
      drawSprite(h.id, idle, SPR_ROW.front, Math.round(cx)-SPR_W/2, r.Y+6);
      g.globalAlpha=1;
      g.textAlign='center';
      txtC(h.fam, cx, r.Y+42, sel?h.col2:h.col, 9);
      txtC(h.god, cx, r.Y+53, sel?C.dim:'#4a3f4a', 7);
      g.textAlign='left';
      return;
    }
    g.globalAlpha=sel?1:.55;
    if(h.id==='CAELUM'){ px(cx-1,cy-10,3,9,h.col); px(cx-5,cy-2,10,3,h.col); px(cx-1,cy+1,3,9,h.col); }
    if(h.id==='MARINA'){ px(cx-7,cy-8,2,16,h.col); px(cx-1,cy-11,2,19,h.col); px(cx+5,cy-8,2,16,h.col); px(cx-7,cy+6,14,2,h.col); }
    if(h.id==='MINERVA'){ px(cx-8,cy-9,12,14,h.col); px(cx-6,cy-7,8,10,'#120e16'); px(cx+5,cy-11,2,20,h.col); }
    if(h.id==='SOLON'){ px(cx-7,cy-9,2,18,h.col); px(cx-5,cy-9,3,2,h.col); px(cx-5,cy+7,3,2,h.col); px(cx-2,cy-1,12,2,h.col); }
    if(h.id==='FREYA'){ px(cx-1,cy-2,3,11,h.col); px(cx-5,cy-10,10,8,h.col); px(cx-2,cy-8,4,4,'#120e16'); }
    if(h.id==='MERCER'){ px(cx-1,cy-10,3,19,h.col); px(cx-6,cy-8,5,2,h.col); px(cx+3,cy-8,5,2,h.col); px(cx-5,cy-4,11,2,h.col); }
    if(h.id==='GORE'){ px(cx-1,cy-11,3,16,h.col); px(cx-6,cy+3,13,3,h.col); px(cx-1,cy+6,3,5,h.col); }
    g.globalAlpha=1;

    g.textAlign='center';
    txtC(h.fam, cx, r.Y+42, sel?h.col2:h.col, 9);
    txtC(h.god, cx, r.Y+53, sel?C.dim:'#4a3f4a', 7);
    g.textAlign='left';
  });

  /* the detail panel for whoever's selected */
  const h=CHARS[selIdx];
  px(14,128,VIEW_W-28,VIEW_H-142,'#100c14');
  px(14,128,3,VIEW_H-142,h.col);
  txt(h.weapon, 26, 136, h.col2, 11);
  txt('TOOL: '+h.tool+'   ·   '+h.crop, 26, 152, C.dim, 7);

  const lines=h.bonus.split('\n');
  lines.forEach((ln,i)=> txt(ln.trim(), 26, 168+i*11, C.crop, 8));
  txt(h.play, 26, 168+lines.length*11+6, '#8a7a90', 8);

  /* the stat deltas, honestly */
  const parts=[];
  const NAMES={dmg:'DMG',rate:'RATE',spd:'SPEED',maxHp:'HEALTH',armor:'ARMOR',
    range:'RANGE',crit:'CRIT',pierce:'PIERCE',chain:'CHAIN',extra:'MULTI',
    knock:'KNOCK',magnet:'PICKUP',shotSpd:'SHOT SPD',critD:'CRIT DMG',
    shardGain:'SHARDS',dashCh:'DASHES'};
  for(const k in h.mod){
    const v=h.mod[k];
    if(!NAMES[k]) continue;
    const show = (k==='maxHp') ? (v>0?'+':'')+(v/2)+' HEART'+(Math.abs(v/2)===1?'':'S')
      : (k==='magnet') ? 'HUGE'
      : (v>0?'+':'')+ (Math.abs(v)<1 && k!=='armor' && k!=='pierce' && k!=='chain' && k!=='dashCh'
          ? Math.round(v*100)+'%' : v);
    parts.push({t:NAMES[k]+' '+show, good:v>0});
  }
  let X=26;
  const Y=VIEW_H-22;
  parts.forEach(pt=>{
    txt(pt.t, X, Y, pt.good?C.crop:'#c05a6a', 7);
    X += pt.t.length*4.6 + 12;
  });
}

/* Shared row renderer — every menu looks and behaves the same. */
function drawMenu(items){
  menuSel=clamp(menuSel,0,items.length-1);
  items.forEach((o,i)=>{
    const r=menuRow(i), sel=i===menuSel;
    px(r.X,r.Y,r.W,r.H, sel?'#241c26':'#15101a');
    px(r.X,r.Y, sel?3:1, r.H, o.danger?C.blood:(sel?C.sulfur:'#2a2030'));
    g.textAlign='left'; g.textBaseline='top';
    g.fillStyle = o.danger ? (sel?C.blood:'#7a3040')
                : o.hot    ? (sel?C.sulfur:'#8a7a3a')
                : (sel?C.bone:C.dim);
    g.font='10px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText(o.k, r.X+14, r.Y+4);
    g.fillStyle = sel ? C.dim : '#463c46';
    g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText(o.d, r.X+14, r.Y+14);
  });
  g.textAlign='left';
}

/* Mouse hit-testing, shared. Returns the index under the cursor, or -1. */
function menuHit(mx,my){
  const items=currentMenu();
  if(!items) return -1;
  for(let i=0;i<items.length;i++){
    const r=menuRow(i);
    if(mx>=r.X&&mx<r.X+r.W&&my>=r.Y&&my<r.Y+r.H) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

/* PLAYER — now with mouse aim + dash charges */
const P={
  x:0,y:0,r:6,hp:6,dx:0,dy:1,anim:0,tool:1,
  atkT:0, atkMax:0,          // the attack pose: how long is left, and how long it was
  steal:0,                   // GORE: the fraction of a half-heart the sword is still owed
  cool:0, iframe:0,
  slow:1,slowReq:1,slowT:0,slowV:1,invert:0,frozen:0,
  forceX:0,forceY:0,kbX:0,kbY:0,
  /* aim */
  aimX:0, aimY:0,           // cursor position, in world (640x320) space
  firing:false,
  /* dash */
  dashN:DASH_CHARGES,       // charges available right now
  dashQ:[],                 // ms remaining on each recharging charge
  dashT:0,                  // ms left in the current dash
  dashVX:0, dashVY:0,
  dashTrail:[],
};
/* THE FARM TOOLS.
   Slots 1-3 are the same work for everyone — you break ground, you sow, you
   water. Slot 4 is the HARVEST, and that is the one the doc gives each family
   its own instrument for: Gore reaps with a scythe, Mercer with a shepherd's
   crook, Caelum with a cattle prod. It was written on the select screen and
   then never appeared again. It appears now, every time you look at the HUD. */
const TOOLNAME={1:'HOE',2:'SEED',3:'CAN',4:'SCYTHE'};
const toolName = n => (n===4 && hero && hero.tool) ? hero.tool : TOOLNAME[n];

const FARM_W=14, FARM_H=8, FARM_X=13, FARM_Y=7;
let plots;

function dashMax(){ return DASH_CHARGES + stats.dashCh; }          // Phantom Step: +1 charge
function dashRecharge(){ return DASH_RECHARGE - stats.dashCd; }    // Slipstream: faster refill

/* Stats are ALWAYS rebuilt from base + skill tree + every held item.
   That's what makes dropping an item cleanly reverse its effect. */
function recalcStats(){
  Object.assign(stats,BASE);
  /* YOUR BLOOD, before anything else. Items and skills build on top of it. */
  for(const k in hero.mod) stats[k] = (stats[k]||0) + hero.mod[k];
  stats.maxHp += hpUpgrades*2;              // one heart per boss felled
  DYN.clear();
  /* CURSES bite here, before anything else builds on top. */
  if(curses.has('FRAIL'))   stats.maxHp   = Math.max(2, stats.maxHp-2);   // -1 heart
  if(curses.has('BRITTLE')) stats.armor   = stats.armor-2;
  if(curses.has('SLOTH'))   stats.rate    = stats.rate*0.85;
  if(curses.has('GREEDY'))  stats.shardGain = stats.shardGain-0.40;
  const T=TREES[tree];
  for(let r=0;r<5;r++)for(let c=0;c<3;c++){
    if(!spent[r][c])continue;
    const [,key,val]=T.rows[r][c];
    stats[key]+=val;
  }
  /* Items speak in POINTS and PERCENT; the engine speaks in fractions.
     This map is the translation layer — the tree and the items no longer
     fight over keys like `spd` and `crit`. */
  let mult=1;
  for(let i=0;i<inv.length;i++){
    const it=inv[i], L=lvlOf(i);
    for(const k in it.e){
      if(k==='heal'||k==='shards') continue;        // instant-only, applied at pickup
      if(k==='dyn'){ DYN.add(it.e[k]); continue; }  // a keyword is not a number; never scale it

      /* effAt() IS THE ONLY PLACE that knows what a level is worth. The
         Compendium's upgrade ladder calls this exact function to show you the
         numbers, so what the panel promises is by construction what the game
         applies. Two copies of this arithmetic would eventually drift, and the
         shop would start lying to you. */
      const v=effAt(it,k,L);

      if(k==='dmgMul'){ mult *= v; continue; }      // effAt already scaled the BONUS, not the whole
      if(k==='fireCd'){ stats.rate += -v/100; continue; }   // -8% cooldown = +8% rate
      if(k==='tearSpd'){ stats.tearSpd += v; continue; }     // flat px/s
      if(k==='pChance'){ stats.dotC += v/100; continue; }    // feeds the existing DoT system
      if(k==='pDmg'){ stats.dot += v; continue; }
      if(PCT[k]){ stats[k] += v/PCT[k]; continue; }
      stats[k]=(stats[k]||0)+v;
    }
  }
  stats.dmgMul=mult;

  /* THE ALCHEMY TABLE's work — everything the farm bought you. */
  stats.armor  += elix.armor;
  stats.spd    += elix.spd;
  stats.dmgMul *= (1 + elix.dmg);
  stats.maxHp  += elix.maxHp;

  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------- BALANCE
     Two systems used to have no ceiling: block chance summed past 100%
     (invincibility), and damage multipliers stacked multiplicatively forever
     (x16+ loadouts). These caps keep both rewarding without going degenerate.
     Tune the three numbers to taste.                                        */
  const BLOCK_CAP = 0.80;   // hardest you can ever be to hit (20% of shots always land)
  const DM_SOFT   = 4.0;    // damage multiplier is full value up to here...
  const DM_RATE   = 0.40;   // ...past it, every extra multiplier counts at 40%
  stats.block = Math.min(BLOCK_CAP, Math.max(0, stats.block));
  if(stats.dmgMul > DM_SOFT)
    stats.dmgMul = DM_SOFT + (stats.dmgMul - DM_SOFT) * DM_RATE;
  /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */

  stats.rate=Math.max(.25,stats.rate);
  stats.maxHp=Math.max(1,stats.maxHp);
  revives=Math.max(0, stats.revive - revivesUsed);   // spent revives stay spent
  P.hp=Math.min(P.hp,stats.maxHp);
  P.dashN=Math.min(P.dashN,dashMax());
  rebuildWisps();
}

function rebuildWisps(){
  const n=stats.wisp;
  wisps=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++) wisps.push({a:i/n*6.283, cd:0});
}

/* Effective damage, recomputed every shot — the dynamic items live here. */
function fireCool(){
  let c = 250/Math.max(.2, stats.rate);
  if(heroIs('GORE')) c *= 1.55;               // a broadsword is not a machine gun
  if(rageT>0)        c *= 0.5;                // RAGE: twice as fast, for 5 seconds
  return Math.max(60, c - stats.fireCd);
}

function effDmg(){
  let d=stats.dmg;
  if(DYN.has('adrenaline')) d += (1-P.hp/stats.maxHp)*5;
  if(DYN.has('greed'))      d += Math.min(4, shards/40);
  if(DYN.has('overclock'))  d += overclock*3;
  if(DYN.has('bloodletter')&&P.hp<=1) d += 2;
  let m=stats.dmgMul;
  if(spectralT>0) m*=1.25;
  return d*m;
}

function say(t){ msg=t; msgT=2400; }

/* ==========================================================================
   MUSIC — context-aware rotation with crossfade

   TWO MODES, and it picks automatically:

   1. YOUR TRACKS. Drop files next to this HTML and list them below. They
      rotate on a shuffle-bag (no repeats until the pool is exhausted) and
      crossfade into each other. Playlists are per-context, so the farm
      doesn't play boss music.

   2. NO FILES? A built-in generative chiptune composer takes over, so the
      game is never silent. Same rotation logic, same crossfades — it just
      synthesises the tracks instead of streaming them.

   Browsers block audio until the user interacts, so nothing starts until
   the first keypress or click. That's not a bug, it's the platform.
   ========================================================================= */
/* YOUR MUSIC PLAYS THROUGHOUT — title, farm, hall, boss.
   The ONLY exception is the opening cinematic, which is narration-driven: a
   metal track over the voiceover buries the words. That's why 'intro' is the
   one empty pool, and why it needed its own context to begin with.

   Each context still has its own weighting, so the boss gate leans heavy and
   the farm leans on the driving-but-not-frantic ones — but every track can
   play anywhere, and the shuffle bag means no repeats until the pool runs out. */

const T_RIP    = 'audio/rip_it.mp3';
const T_WIRE   = 'audio/live_wire.mp3';
const T_FIGHT  = 'audio/fighting_to_the_end.mp3';
const T_SCREAM = 'audio/scream_of_hell.mp3';
const T_KING   = 'audio/kings_return.mp3';       // The King's Return

const MUSIC_TRACKS={
  /* THE OPENING CINEMATIC — the one place your music does NOT play.
     It's narration-driven; a full track would bury the words. */
  intro:[],

  title:[ T_KING, T_WIRE, T_FIGHT, T_RIP, T_SCREAM ],
  farm :[ T_WIRE, T_KING, T_RIP, T_FIGHT, T_SCREAM ],
  hall :[ T_RIP, T_WIRE, T_FIGHT, T_SCREAM, T_KING ],
  boss :[ T_SCREAM, T_FIGHT, T_KING, T_RIP, T_WIRE ],
};

/* --- the built-in composer's songbook ---------------------------------- */
const SCALES={
  minor:      [0,2,3,5,7,8,10],
  phrygian:   [0,1,3,5,7,8,10],   // that flat-2 is the "hell" interval
  harmonic:   [0,2,3,5,7,8,11],
  dorian:     [0,2,3,5,7,9,10],
};
const SONGS=[
 {n:'ASH AND SEED',      ctx:'farm', bpm: 82, root:45, sc:'dorian',   prog:[0,5,3,4], drums:.35, lead:.30, bars:16},
 {n:'THE QUIET PLOT',    ctx:'farm', bpm: 74, root:43, sc:'minor',    prog:[0,3,4,3], drums:.15, lead:.22, bars:16},
 {n:'DAYSHIFT',          ctx:'farm', bpm: 90, root:48, sc:'dorian',   prog:[0,4,5,2], drums:.45, lead:.35, bars:16},
 {n:'HALL OF MIRRORS',   ctx:'hall', bpm:126, root:40, sc:'phrygian', drums:.85, lead:.55, prog:[0,1,0,6], bars:16},
 {n:'TILL THE ASH',      ctx:'hall', bpm:138, root:38, sc:'minor',    drums:.90, lead:.60, prog:[0,6,5,6], bars:16},
 {n:'NO MERCY',          ctx:'hall', bpm:144, root:41, sc:'phrygian', drums:.95, lead:.62, prog:[0,1,5,1], bars:16},
 {n:'THE GATE OPENS',    ctx:'boss', bpm:152, root:36, sc:'harmonic', drums:1.0, lead:.75, prog:[0,6,1,0], bars:16},
 {n:'CROWN OF THE DEEP', ctx:'boss', bpm:160, root:34, sc:'phrygian', drums:1.0, lead:.80, prog:[0,1,6,1], bars:16},
 {n:'TRUST NO ONE',      ctx:'title',bpm: 68, root:36, sc:'phrygian', drums:.10, lead:.28, prog:[0,1,0,6], bars:12},
 /* The cinematic's own score — sparse on purpose, so it sits UNDER the words. */
 {n:'THE BETRAYAL',      ctx:'intro',bpm: 60, root:33, sc:'phrygian', drums:.06, lead:.18, prog:[0,1,0,6], bars:16},
 {n:'WHAT THEY BURIED',  ctx:'intro',bpm: 66, root:36, sc:'minor',    drums:.08, lead:.20, prog:[0,5,3,4], bars:16},
];

const Music=(()=>{
  let ctxA=null, master=null, on=true, vol=.55, unlocked=false;
  let mode2='title', bag=[], cur=null, curName='—', useFiles=false;
  let audioA=null, audioB=null, which=0, filesBroken=false;
  let timer=null, nextNote=0, step=0, song=null, barsLeft=0;

  const listFor=c => MUSIC_TRACKS[c]||[];
  const hasFiles=c=>listFor(c).length>0;

  function init(){
    if(ctxA) return;
    try{
      ctxA=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
      master=ctxA.createGain();
      master.gain.value=on?vol:0;
      master.connect(ctxA.destination);
    }catch(e){ ctxA=null; }
  }

  /* ---- shuffle bag: no repeats until the pool is exhausted ---- */
  function refill(c){
    const pool = (hasFiles(c)&&!filesBroken) ? listFor(c).slice()
                                             : SONGS.filter(s=>s.ctx===c).map(s=>s);
    const fallback = pool.length ? pool : SONGS.filter(s=>s.ctx==='hall');
    bag=fallback.slice();
    for(let i=bag.length-1;i>0;i--){ const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1)); [bag[i],bag[j]]=[bag[j],bag[i]]; }
  }
  function draw(c){
    if(!bag.length) refill(c);
    return bag.pop();
  }

  /* ================= FILE PLAYBACK (your tracks) ================= */
  function playFile(src){
    const a=new Audio(src);
    a.volume=0; a.loop=false;
    a.addEventListener('ended',()=>next());
    /* If the mp3s aren't sitting beside the HTML (sandbox, moved file, typo),
       don't go silent — fall back to the synth for the rest of the session. */
    const bail=()=>{
      if(filesBroken) return;
      filesBroken=true; useFiles=false;
      bag=[];                        // <-- the bag is still full of FILE PATHS.
                                     //     Draw from it now and playSong() gets a
                                     //     string, and the synth throws forever.
      try{ a.pause(); }catch(e){}
      init(); if(ctxA) playSong(draw(mode2));
    };
    a.addEventListener('error',bail);
    const guard=setTimeout(()=>{ if(a.readyState===0) bail(); },4000);
    a.addEventListener('canplay',()=>clearTimeout(guard));
    a.play().catch(bail);
    const old = which?audioA:audioB;
    if(which) audioB=a; else audioA=a;
    which^=1;
    /* 1.2s crossfade */
    const t0=performance.now();
    const fade=()=>{
      const k=Math.min(1,(performance.now()-t0)/1200);
      a.volume=(on?vol:0)*k;
      if(old) old.volume=(on?vol:0)*(1-k);
      if(k<1) requestAnimationFrame(fade);
      else if(old){ old.pause(); old.src=''; }
    };
    fade();
    curName=src.split('/').pop().replace(/\.[^.]+$/,'').replace(/_/g,' ').toUpperCase();
  }

  /* ================= GENERATIVE COMPOSER ================= */
  const hz=n=>440*Math.pow(2,(n-69)/12);

  function voice(type,freq,t,dur,gain,detune){
    if(!ctxA) return;
    const o=ctxA.createOscillator(), g=ctxA.createGain();
    o.type=type; o.frequency.value=freq;
    if(detune) o.detune.value=detune;
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t);
    g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(gain,t+.012);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0008,t+dur);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(master);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t+dur+.02);
  }
  function kick(t,gain){
    if(!ctxA) return;
    const o=ctxA.createOscillator(), g=ctxA.createGain();
    o.type='sine';
    o.frequency.setValueAtTime(150,t);
    o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(42,t+.13);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(gain,t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.001,t+.19);
    o.connect(g); g.connect(master); o.start(t); o.stop(t+.2);
  }
  function noise(t,dur,gain,hp){
    if(!ctxA) return;
    const n=Math.floor(ctxA.sampleRate*dur);
    const buf=ctxA.createBuffer(1,n,ctxA.sampleRate);
    const d=buf.getChannelData(0);
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++) d[i]=(Math.random()*2-1)*(1-i/n);
    const src=ctxA.createBufferSource(); src.buffer=buf;
    const f=ctxA.createBiquadFilter(); f.type='highpass'; f.frequency.value=hp||1800;
    const g=ctxA.createGain(); g.gain.value=gain;
    src.connect(f); f.connect(g); g.connect(master);
    src.start(t);
  }

  const SEQ=16;                                   // 16th notes per bar
  function schedule(){
    if(!ctxA||!song) return;
    const spb=60/song.bpm/4;                      // seconds per 16th
    /* Alt-tab for a minute and the AudioContext clock runs on while this
       scheduler is throttled. On return, nextNote is far in the past and the
       loop would try to catch up by firing hundreds of notes at once. Snap
       forward instead of stampeding. */
    if(nextNote < ctxA.currentTime - .5) nextNote = ctxA.currentTime + .05;
    let guard=0;
    while(nextNote < ctxA.currentTime + .12){
      if(++guard>64) break;                       // hard ceiling per tick
      const t=nextNote, i=step%SEQ, bar=Math.floor(step/SEQ);
      const sc=SCALES[song.sc];
      const deg=song.prog[bar%song.prog.length];
      const rootN=song.root+sc[deg%7]+(deg>=7?12:0);

      /* BASS — the spine */
      if(i%4===0) voice('triangle',hz(rootN-12),t,spb*3.4,.30);
      if(i===10)  voice('triangle',hz(rootN-12+7),t,spb*1.4,.20);

      /* PAD — a slow chord, one per bar */
      if(i===0){
        [0,2,4].forEach((k,j)=>
          voice('sawtooth',hz(rootN+sc[(deg+k)%7]+(k?0:0)),t,spb*13,.055,j*6-6));
      }

      /* LEAD — a wandering arpeggio, seeded so a song sounds like itself */
      if(song.lead>0 && (i%2===0)){
        const r=Math.abs(Math.sin((step*12.9898 + song.bpm)*43758.5453))%1;
        if(r < song.lead){
          const oct=r>.72?12:0;
          const n=rootN+sc[Math.floor(r*7)]+12+oct;
          voice('square',hz(n),t,spb*1.5,.085);
        }
      }

      /* DRUMS */
      const d=song.drums;
      if(d>0){
        if(i===0||i===8) kick(t,.62*d);
        if(d>.5 && i===6) kick(t,.35*d);
        if(i===4||i===12) noise(t,.11,.22*d,1400);        // snare
        if(d>.3 && i%2===0) noise(t,.028,.055*d,6500);    // hat
        if(d>.85 && i===15) noise(t,.05,.10*d,3000);
      }

      nextNote+=spb; step++;
      if(step>=SEQ*song.bars){ next(); return; }
    }
  }

  function playSong(s){
    /* Defensive: never accept anything that isn't a real song object. */
    if(!s || typeof s!=='object' || !s.prog || !s.bpm){
      s = SONGS.find(x=>x.ctx===mode2) || SONGS[0];
    }
    song=s; step=0; barsLeft=s.bars;
    nextNote=ctxA.currentTime+.05;
    curName=s.n;
  }

  /* ================= PUBLIC ================= */
  function stopFiles(){
    [audioA,audioB].forEach(a=>{ if(a){ try{ a.pause(); a.src=''; }catch(e){} } });
    audioA=audioB=null;
  }
  function stopSynth(){ song=null; }

  function next(){
    if(!unlocked) return;
    const c=mode2;
    /* BUG THIS FIXES: walking from the Hall (mp3) to the Farm (synth) used to
       leave the mp3 playing UNDERNEATH the synth. Kill the other source. */
    if(hasFiles(c) && !filesBroken){
      stopSynth(); useFiles=true; playFile(draw(c));
    } else {
      stopFiles(); useFiles=false; init();
      if(ctxA) playSong(draw(c));
    }
  }

  function setContext(c){
    if(c===mode2) return;
    mode2=c; bag=[];
    if(!unlocked) return;
    next();                                        // crossfade into the new mood
  }

  function unlock(){
    if(unlocked) return;
    unlocked=true; init();
    if(ctxA && ctxA.state==='suspended') ctxA.resume();
    next();
    if(!timer) timer=setInterval(()=>{ try{ if(!useFiles) schedule(); }catch(e){ song=null; } },25);
  }

  return {
    unlock, setContext, next,
    toggle(){ on=!on; if(master) master.gain.value=on?vol:0;
      if(useFiles){ [audioA,audioB].forEach(a=>{ if(a) a.volume=on?vol:0; }); }
      return on; },
    louder(d){ vol=clamp(vol+d,0,1);
      if(master) master.gain.value=on?vol:0;
      if(useFiles) [audioA,audioB].forEach(a=>{ if(a) a.volume=on?vol:0; });
      return vol; },
    get on(){ return on; }, get vol(){ return vol; },
    get name(){ return unlocked?curName:'PRESS ANY KEY'; },
    get source(){ return useFiles?'FILE':'SYNTH'; },
  };
})();

/* ==========================================================================
   SAVE / LOAD
   Storage is environment-dependent, so this feature-detects rather than
   assuming. In order of preference:
     1. window.storage  — the sandboxed artifact host provides this
     2. localStorage    — a plain .html file opened locally, or a real build
     3. memory          — neither available; the run still works, it just
                          won't survive a reload (and we say so)
   ========================================================================== */
/* ==========================================================================
   THE THREE WAYS TO PLAY

   ONE LIFE      — hardcore. No coming back. The clock stops when Hades falls,
                   and that number is the whole point.
   THE LONG WAY  — die as often as you need to. You always wake on your farm.
                   The clock never stops, so it costs you the only thing that
                   matters in the other two modes: time.
   THE GAUNTLET  — all seven houses, in a random order, back to back. Beat Hades
                   with one and the next heir steps up with NOTHING: no relics,
                   no skills, no heart containers. One death anywhere ends all
                   of it. Unlocked by winning once.
   ========================================================================= */
const MODES=[
 {id:'HARDCORE', n:'ONE LIFE', c:'#c0304a',
  tag:'hardcore',
  d:['One life. One run. No return.',
     'Die and it is over — no matter how deep you were.',
     'The clock stops when Hades falls. That number is the point.'],
  need:()=>true, why:''},

 {id:'LONGWAY',  n:'THE LONG WAY', c:'#6ae0a8',
  tag:'forgiving',
  d:['Die as often as you need to.',
     'You always wake on your farm, at the head of the segment that killed you.',
     'It costs your shards and half your crops — and THE CLOCK NEVER STOPS.'],
  need:()=>META.unlockedReturn,
  why:'FELL A BOSS TO EARN THE RIGHT TO COME BACK'},

 {id:'GAUNTLET', n:'THE GAUNTLET', c:'#e0b93c',
  tag:'the real test',
  d:['All SEVEN houses. A random order. Back to back.',
     'Each heir starts with NOTHING — no relics, no skills, no hearts carry over.',
     'ONE DEATH, anywhere, ends all seven.'],
  need:()=>META.wins>0,
  why:'FELL HADES ONCE, AND THE GAUNTLET OPENS'},
];
let MODE='HARDCORE';
const modeIs = id => MODE===id;
const modeDef = () => MODES.find(m=>m.id===MODE) || MODES[0];

/* THE GAUNTLET's state: whose turn it is, and who is left. */
let gaunt=null;      // {order:[heroId...], idx:0}

function rollGauntlet(){
  const ids=CHARS.map(h=>h.id);
  for(let i=ids.length-1;i>0;i--){ const j=Math.floor(Math.random()*(i+1)); [ids[i],ids[j]]=[ids[j],ids[i]]; }
  gaunt={order:ids, idx:0};
}

/* ==========================================================================
   THE TOWN

   The run had three currencies and NOTHING TO SPEND THEM ON. Shards piled up
   past two thousand and bought nothing; crops were a number that went up. The
   farm was in the title of the game and was mechanically decoration.

   The Town is the sink. It sits at the west edge of your farm, so you walk into
   it between segments — the portal east takes you to Hell, the gate west takes
   you to town. Three trades:

     THE MARKET  (shards)  — a stock of four, refreshed every dawn. One slot is
                             ALWAYS a Tier 4 relic. It costs a fortune. It is
                             meant to.
     THE FORGE   (items)   — melt THREE relics of one tier, get ONE of the tier
                             above. The items are the price. This is how a bag
                             full of junk commons becomes something worth having.
     THE ANVIL   (crops)   — pour crops into a relic you already own and it gets
                             STRONGER. Four levels. This is what the farm is for.

   Shards buy breadth. The forge buys rarity. Crops buy depth. Three currencies,
   three answers.
   ========================================================================= */
/* the Town speaks the same language as the rest of the game now */
const TIER_N = TIER_NAME;
const TIER_C = TIER_COL;

/* ==========================================================================
   THE TWO ECONOMIES — and why they are separate.

   MEASURED, not guessed: a run pays about 64 SHARDS a segment, so roughly 1,300
   across a full descent. The farm pays about 8-16 CROPS a day, and ONLY if you
   actually work it — a plot returns one crop and one seed, so the seed bank
   holds steady and the crop pile grows slowly, by hand.

   So the currencies are not interchangeable, and the Town does not pretend they
   are:

     SHARDS are POWER. Torn out of Hell, spent on Hell. They buy relics and they
     forge and sharpen them. You get them by killing things.

     CROPS are SURVIVAL. Grown at home, in the quiet, between the killing. They
     become potions and elixirs — the things that keep you breathing.

   A whole run's shards (~1,300) buys you ONE Tier-4 relic and ONE relic taken to
   maximum. Not both comfortably. Not everything. That is the point: the Town is
   a place where you choose what you cannot afford to do.

   And crops are scarcer still, so an elixir is a genuine sacrifice of farm time
   you could have spent descending. The farm finally COSTS something, which is
   the only way it can be worth something.
   ========================================================================= */

/* THE MARKET (shards). A LEGENDARY is 500 — you can afford two if you buy
   nothing else all run, and buying nothing else all run means no forging and no
   sharpening.
   A MYTHIC is 1,100: measured against a 1,339-shard run, that is EVERYTHING. The
   shop will sell you the best item in the game, and the price is your entire
   descent. Most days you will look at it and walk away. Good. */
const PRICE   = {1:35, 2:80, 3:170, 4:500, 5:1100};

/* ==========================================================================
   THE ANVIL (shards)

   MEASURED: a full descent pays 1,339 shards on average (906 on a bad run,
   1,854 on a good one). The target is that a run can take about TWO things to
   the top — and that doing it costs you nearly everything else.

   The climb is deliberately back-loaded. Levels 2 and 3 are affordable, so
   sharpening is something you do casually with spare change. LEVEL 4 IS THE
   WALL: it costs more than the two below it put together. Getting one relic to
   the top is a decision; getting two is the whole run.

   And the anvil CARES WHAT IT IS SHARPENING. Putting an edge on a Relic is
   harder work than putting one on a Cardboard Box, and the payoff is bigger too
   — a level-4 Relic is twice a Relic; a level-4 Common is twice a Common, which
   is still a Common. So the fee scales with rarity:

     max a COMMON     438 shards   (cheap, and the reward is small)
     max an UNCOMMON  584
     max a RARE       730          <- two of these IS your run
     max a RELIC      949          <- the most expensive thing in the game

   Two Rares to level 4 is 1,460 against a 1,339 average: you need a GOOD run,
   and you buy nothing else at all. That is the ceiling the balance is aiming at.
   ========================================================================= */
const ANVIL_BASE = {2:80, 3:200, 4:450};             // 730 to take one Rare to the top
const ANVIL_TIER = {1:0.6, 2:0.8, 3:1.0, 4:1.3, 5:1.8};  // ...and rarity is the multiplier

/* THE FORGE (items AND shards). Three relics is not the whole price — the fire
   has to be paid for. This is what stops the forge being a free ratchet that
   quietly turns every common you ever find into a relic. */
/* THE FORGE. The last step — three LEGENDARIES into a MYTHIC — costs 500, the
   price of a legendary outright. Ascending to the apex should never be the cheap
   route to it. */
const FORGE_FEE = {1:40, 2:90, 3:200, 4:500};

/* ==========================================================================
   THE ALCHEMY TABLE (crops)
   The farm's half of the Town. Everything here is bought with what you GREW.

   Every brew costs MORE the next time you buy it (x1.8). You cannot stand at
   the table and convert a good harvest into an unbeatable build — the second
   Elixir of Wrath costs 81 crops, the third 146, and the farm simply does not
   pay that fast. One or two brews a run is the intended shape.
   ========================================================================= */
const BREWS=[
  {id:'DRAUGHT', n:'HEALING DRAUGHT', c:14, col:'#6adc6a',
   d:'A potion for your belt. Q drinks it.',
   can:()=>potions<POTION_MAX,
   why:'YOUR BELT IS FULL',
   go:()=>{ potions++; }},

  {id:'IRON', n:'ELIXIR OF IRON', c:40, col:'#9adcff',
   d:'+1 ARMOR, for the rest of the run.',
   go:()=>{ elix.armor+=1; }},

  {id:'WRATH', n:'ELIXIR OF WRATH', c:45, col:'#ff7040',
   d:'+12% DAMAGE, for the rest of the run.',
   go:()=>{ elix.dmg+=0.12; }},

  {id:'HASTE', n:'ELIXIR OF HASTE', c:40, col:'#c8a0e8',
   d:'+8% SPEED, for the rest of the run.',
   go:()=>{ elix.spd+=0.08; }},

  {id:'VINE', n:'BLOOD OF THE VINE', c:95, col:'#c0304a',
   d:'+1 HEART CONTAINER. Permanent. Expensive.',
   go:()=>{ elix.maxHp+=2; recalcStats(); P.hp=Math.min(stats.maxHp,P.hp+2); }},
];
const BREW_CLIMB = 1.8;                    // each one you brew makes the next dearer

let brewed={};                             // how many of each you have already made
let elix={armor:0, dmg:0, spd:0, maxHp:0}; // what the elixirs have given you

const brewCost = b => Math.round(b.c * Math.pow(BREW_CLIMB, brewed[b.id]||0));

function brew(k){
  const b=BREWS[k];
  if(!b) return;
  if(b.can && !b.can()){ say(b.why); shake=120; return; }
  const cost=brewCost(b);
  if(crops<cost){ say(`NOT ENOUGH CROPS — ${cost} NEEDED. GO AND FARM.`); shake=140; return; }
  crops-=cost;
  brewed[b.id]=(brewed[b.id]||0)+1;
  b.go();
  recalcStats();
  shake=150; flash=90;
  say(`BREWED: ${b.n}`);
  setTimeout(saveRun,0);
}

let market=null;                 // {day, stock:[{i,tier,sold}]}
let townTab=0;                   // 0 MARKET · 1 FORGE · 2 ANVIL
let townSel=0;                   // the highlighted row
let forgePick=[];                // indices into inv, chosen to melt

/* the price you actually pay: TRADE VALUE is finally worth something */
const priceOf = tier => Math.max(5, Math.round(PRICE[tier] * (1 - clamp(stats.tradeVal,0,.6))));

/* THE STOCK. Four slots, and the fourth is ALWAYS a Tier 4 — the shop is the
   only reliable way to see one, which is what makes shards worth carrying. */
function rollMarket(){
  const pick=t=>{ const p=POOL[t]; return ITEMS.indexOf(p[Math.floor(Math.random()*p.length)]); };
  market={ day, stock:[
    {i:pick(1), tier:1, sold:false},
    {i:pick(2), tier:2, sold:false},
    {i:pick(Math.random()<.5?2:3), tier:0, sold:false},
    /* THE APEX SLOT. A LEGENDARY every day — and one day in four, a MYTHIC
       instead. The shop is the only place you can COUNT on seeing the top of the
       game, but it will take your whole run to buy it. */
    {i:pick(Math.random()<0.25 ? 5 : 4), tier:0, sold:false},
  ]};
  /* the third slot's tier follows whatever it actually rolled */
  market.stock.forEach(s=>{ if(!s.tier) s.tier=ITEMS[s.i].t; });
}
function ensureMarket(){ if(!market || market.day!==day) rollMarket(); }

function toTown(){ ensureMarket(); mode=S.TOWN; townTab=0; townSel=0; forgePick=[]; }

/* ---- THE MARKET ---- */
function buy(k){
  ensureMarket();
  const s=market.stock[k];
  if(!s || s.sold) return;
  if(inv.length>=INV_SLOTS){ say('YOUR BAG IS FULL'); shake=140; return; }
  const cost=priceOf(s.tier);
  if(shards<cost){ say(`NOT ENOUGH SHARDS — ${cost} NEEDED`); shake=140; return; }
  shards-=cost;
  const it=ITEMS[s.i];
  inv.push(it); lvls.push(1);
  s.sold=true;
  if(it.e.shards) shards += Math.round(it.e.shards*(1+stats.shardGain));
  recalcStats();
  if(it.e.heal) P.hp = it.e.heal>=99 ? stats.maxHp : Math.min(stats.maxHp, P.hp+Math.ceil(it.e.heal));
  if(it.e.maxHp>0) P.hp=Math.min(stats.maxHp,P.hp+it.e.maxHp);
  RUN.relics++;
  say(`BOUGHT: ${it.n}`);
  setTimeout(saveRun,0);
}

/* ---- THE FORGE: three of a tier, one of the next ---- */
function forgeTierOf(){
  if(forgePick.length!==3) return 0;
  const t=inv[forgePick[0]].t;
  return forgePick.every(i=>inv[i] && inv[i].t===t) ? t : 0;
}
function canForge(){ const t=forgeTierOf(); return t>=1 && t<=4; }
function forge(){
  const t=forgeTierOf();
  if(!t){ say('THE FORGE TAKES THREE OF ONE TIER'); shake=140; return; }
  if(t>=5){ say('THERE IS NOTHING ABOVE A MYTHIC'); shake=140; return; }
  /* THE FIRE IS NOT FREE. Without a fee the forge is a ratchet: every common you
     ever pick up quietly becomes a relic, and the tier system stops meaning
     anything. */
  const fee=FORGE_FEE[t];
  if(shards<fee){ say(`THE FIRE COSTS ${fee} SHARDS`); shake=140; return; }
  shards-=fee;
  /* remove the three, highest index first so the earlier ones do not shift */
  const gone=[...forgePick].sort((a,b)=>b-a);
  for(const i of gone){ inv.splice(i,1); lvls.splice(i,1); }
  const up=POOL[t+1];
  const it=up[Math.floor(Math.random()*up.length)];
  inv.push(it); lvls.push(1);
  forgePick=[]; townSel=0;
  recalcStats();
  if(it.e.maxHp>0) P.hp=Math.min(stats.maxHp,P.hp+it.e.maxHp);
  shake=200; flash=120;
  say(`FORGED: ${it.n} (${TIER_N[t+1]})`);
  setTimeout(saveRun,0);
}
function togglePick(i){
  if(i<0||i>=inv.length) return;
  const at=forgePick.indexOf(i);
  if(at>=0) forgePick.splice(at,1);
  else if(forgePick.length<3) forgePick.push(i);
  else { say('THREE IS THE FORGE\'S APPETITE'); shake=100; }
}

/* ---- THE ANVIL: crops make a relic stronger ---- */
function anvilCost(i){
  const L=lvlOf(i);
  if(L>=MAX_LVL || !inv[i]) return 0;
  return Math.round(ANVIL_BASE[L+1] * ANVIL_TIER[inv[i].t]);
}
/* what it costs to take an item all the way, from wherever it is now */
function anvilTotal(i){
  if(!inv[i]) return 0;
  let sum=0;
  for(let L=lvlOf(i); L<MAX_LVL; L++) sum += Math.round(ANVIL_BASE[L+1]*ANVIL_TIER[inv[i].t]);
  return sum;
}
function upgrade(i){
  if(i<0||i>=inv.length) return;
  const L=lvlOf(i);
  if(L>=MAX_LVL){ say('IT WILL TAKE NO MORE'); shake=100; return; }
  const cost=anvilCost(i);
  /* SHARDS. The anvil is Hell's work, paid for with Hell's money. */
  if(shards<cost){ say(`NOT ENOUGH SHARDS — ${cost} NEEDED`); shake=140; return; }
  shards-=cost;
  lvls[i]=L+1;
  recalcStats();
  if(inv[i].e.maxHp>0) P.hp=Math.min(stats.maxHp, P.hp+Math.round(inv[i].e.maxHp*0.33));
  shake=160; flash=90;
  say(`${inv[i].n} \u2192 LEVEL ${L+1}`);
  setTimeout(saveRun,0);
}

/* ==========================================================================
   THE RUN LEDGER
   Everything the run did, so that when it ends you can be shown what it WAS —
   not just handed a menu. A death screen that says nothing is a death screen
   nobody presses NEW RUN from.
   ========================================================================= */
const RUN = {
  ms:0,            // the clock: from the moment you first step into the Hall
  running:false,   // ...until Hades falls. Not the farm, not the title.
  housesCleared:0, // THE GAUNTLET: how many heirs have already gone the distance
  kills:0, elites:0, bosses:0, rooms:0,
  hits:0,          // half-hearts taken
  potions:0,       // drunk
  relics:0,        // picked up
  shardsEarned:0, cropsEarned:0,
  returns:0,       // times you crawled back out of your own grave
  deepest:1,       // the furthest segment reached, even if you came back
};

function resetRun(){
  Object.assign(RUN,{ms:0,running:false,housesCleared:0,kills:0,elites:0,bosses:0,
    rooms:0,hits:0,potions:0,relics:0,shardsEarned:0,cropsEarned:0,returns:0,deepest:1});
}

/* The clock ticks in REAL time — hit-stop and slow motion are cosmetic, and a
   speedrun timer that you could slow down by getting hit would be a lie. */
function tickClock(realMs){
  if(RUN.running && mode!==S.PAUSE) RUN.ms += realMs;
}

function fmtTime(ms){
  const t=Math.max(0,Math.floor(ms/1000));
  const h=Math.floor(t/3600), m=Math.floor(t%3600/60), s=t%60;
  const cs=Math.floor((ms%1000)/10);
  return (h?String(h)+':':'') + String(m).padStart(h?2:1,'0') + ':' +
         String(s).padStart(2,'0') + '.' + String(cs).padStart(2,'0');
}

/* ==========================================================================
   THE META — what survives your death
   A separate key from the run save, because the whole point is that it is NOT
   wiped when the run is. This is the first thing in the game that makes losing
   productive, and it is the seed the Town will grow out of.
   ========================================================================= */
const META_KEY='hellfarmers_meta_v1';
const META = {
  unlockedReturn:false,   // THE RETURN: die once per run and wake on the farm
  runs:0, deaths:0, wins:0,
  bossesFelled:0,         // lifetime
  best:{},                // fastest completed run PER MODE, ms
  bestTime:0,             // (legacy: the old single best, kept so old saves load)
  deepest:0,              // furthest segment ever reached
  gauntletsWon:0,
};

async function loadMeta(){
  try{
    const raw=await Store.get(META_KEY);
    if(raw) Object.assign(META, JSON.parse(raw));
  }catch(e){ /* first boot, or storage is unavailable — the defaults stand */ }
}
async function saveMeta(){
  try{ await Store.set(META_KEY, JSON.stringify(META)); }catch(e){}
}

/* THE RETURN is EARNED, not given: fell your first boss and Hell learns your
   name. From then on, every run, you get one crawl back out of the grave. */
function checkUnlocks(){
  if(!META.unlockedReturn && META.bossesFelled>=1){
    META.unlockedReturn=true;
    say('UNLOCKED — THE RETURN: you may now die once, and wake on your farm');
    saveMeta();
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

const SAVE_KEY='hellfarmers_run_v1';
const Store=(()=>{
  const host = (typeof window!=='undefined' && window.storage && window.storage.get) ? 'host' : null;
  let local=null;
  if(!host){
    try{ localStorage.setItem('__t','1'); localStorage.removeItem('__t'); local='local'; }
    catch(e){ local=null; }
  }
  const mem={};
  const kind = host||local||'memory';
  return {
    kind,
    async get(k){
      try{
        if(kind==='host'){ const r=await window.storage.get(k); return r?r.value:null; }
        if(kind==='local') return localStorage.getItem(k);
        return mem[k]??null;
      }catch(e){ return null; }
    },
    async set(k,v){
      try{
        if(kind==='host') await window.storage.set(k,v);
        else if(kind==='local') localStorage.setItem(k,v);
        else mem[k]=v;
        return true;
      }catch(e){ return false; }
    },
    async del(k){
      try{
        if(kind==='host') await window.storage.delete(k);
        else if(kind==='local') localStorage.removeItem(k);
        else delete mem[k];
      }catch(e){}
    },
  };
})();

/* Items are saved by INDEX, not by name — so renaming an item (e.g. flipping
   USE_SAFE_NAMES) doesn't invalidate anybody's save. */
function serialise(){
  return JSON.stringify({
    v:1, seg, room, day, tree, pts, spent, crops, seedBank, shards, runSeed,
    curses:[...curses], roomKind, pending, potions, hpUpgrades, hero:hero.id,
    run:RUN, mode2:MODE, gaunt,      // the ledger, the clock, the mode, the roster
    hp:P.hp, revivesUsed, roomsCleared,
    inv: inv.map(it=>ITEMS.indexOf(it)), lvls, market, brewed, elix,
    plots: plots.map(p=>p.tilled ? [1, p.crop?p.crop.stage:-1, p.crop&&p.crop.wet?1:0] : 0),
    /* When you save FROM the pause menu, `mode` is PAUSE — so ask where the
       pause came from, or you'd save a boss fight as if you were on the farm. */
    at: (m=>m===S.BOSS?'boss':m===S.HALL?'hall':'farm')(mode===S.PAUSE?pausedFrom:mode),
  });
}

function deserialise(raw){
  const d=JSON.parse(raw);
  if(!d||d.v!==1) throw new Error('bad save');
  seg=d.seg; room=d.room; day=d.day; tree=d.tree; pts=d.pts;
  runSeed = (d.runSeed!==undefined) ? d.runSeed : ((Math.random()*0x7fffffff)|0);
  curses.clear(); (d.curses||[]).forEach(c=>curses.add(c));
  roomKind = d.roomKind||'COMBAT'; pending = d.pending||null;
  potions = d.potions||0;
  hpUpgrades = d.hpUpgrades||0;
  hero = CHARS.find(h=>h.id===d.hero) || CHARS[0];
  rage=0; rageT=0; swipes=[]; sowerT=0;
  resetRun();
  if(d.run) Object.assign(RUN, d.run);
  RUN.running = RUN.ms>0;           // if the clock had started, it starts again
  MODE = d.mode2 || 'HARDCORE';
  gaunt = d.gaunt || null;
  spent=d.spent; crops=d.crops; seedBank=d.seedBank; shards=d.shards;
  revivesUsed=d.revivesUsed||0; roomsCleared=d.roomsCleared||0;
  inv=d.inv.map(i=>ITEMS[i]).filter(Boolean);
  lvls=(d.lvls||[]).slice(0,inv.length);
  while(lvls.length<inv.length) lvls.push(1);    // an old save has no levels: everything is 1
  market=d.market||null;
  brewed=d.brewed||{};
  elix=Object.assign({armor:0,dmg:0,spd:0,maxHp:0}, d.elix||{});
  plots=d.plots.map(v=>{
    if(!v) return {tilled:false,crop:null};
    const [t,stage,wet]=v;
    return {tilled:!!t, crop: stage>=0 ? {stage, wet:!!wet, grow:0} : null};
  });
  recalcStats();
  P.hp=clamp(d.hp,1,stats.maxHp);
  P.dashN=dashMax(); P.dashQ=[];
  /* You always resume at the START of the room you were in. Rooms are
     seeded by index, so it regenerates identically — no need to persist
     enemy positions, and no way to save-scum mid-fight. */
  if(d.at==='boss') enterBoss();
  else if(d.at==='hall') enterHall();
  else enterFarm();
  saveMsg='RUN RESTORED — DAY '+day;
}

let saveMsg='', saveFlash=0, hasSave=false;

async function saveRun(){
  /* Only ever write from a live run. Autosaves are queued on a timer, so one
     could otherwise land AFTER you've already exited to the title and clobber
     a good save with a menu-state snapshot. */
  if(mode!==S.FARM && mode!==S.HALL && mode!==S.BOSS && mode!==S.SKILL && mode!==S.PAUSE) return;
  const okd=await Store.set(SAVE_KEY, serialise());
  if(okd){ hasSave=true; saveFlash=1100; }
}
async function clearSave(){ await Store.del(SAVE_KEY); hasSave=false; }

/* ==========================================================================
   OPENING CINEMATIC
   Three phases: The Betrayal · The Prophecy · The Reality.
   Rendered in-engine at the game's own resolution, so it is pixel-perfect with it.

   VIDEO_INTRO: set to a filename (e.g. 'intro.mp4') to play a video instead.
   Your DaVinci render is 960x960 square — it will pillarbox badly on a 2:1
   frame, so re-export at 1280x640 (a clean 2x of the game res) before using it.
   ========================================================================== */
const VIDEO_INTRO = null;      // e.g. 'intro.mp4'

const CINE=[
 /* ---- PHASE 1 · THE BETRAYAL ---- */
 {p:1, dur:5200, t:"One thousand years ago, seven gods betrayed Hades."},
 {p:1, dur:5600, t:"They stripped him of his crown and locked him within his own domain."},
 /* ---- PHASE 2 · THE PROPHECY ---- */
 {p:2, dur:5400, t:"Before the gates closed, a prophecy was carved in stone:"},
 {p:2, dur:6000, t:"One day, one of those seven gods will return to Hell. Not as a prisoner, but as a ruler."},
 {p:2, dur:6200, t:"They will raise the greatest army existence has ever seen, conquer Mount Olympus, and claim absolute dominion over all gods."},
 /* ---- PHASE 3 · THE REALITY ---- */
 {p:3, dur:3200, t:"That day is not today."},
 {p:3, dur:5000, t:"Right now, Hell is a wasteland. There are no heroes, no alliances, and no mercy."},
 {p:3, dur:5400, t:"If you want to survive, you rely on your own hands. You till the ash. You grow your own food. You defend your plot."},
 {p:3, dur:4200, t:"Trust no one. Welcome to Hell."},
];
let cineI=0, cineT=0, cinePT=0;

function startIntro(){ mode=S.INTRO; cineI=0; cineT=0; cinePT=0; }
function cineNext(){
  cineT=0;
  if(CINE[cineI] && CINE[cineI+1] && CINE[cineI+1].p!==CINE[cineI].p) cinePT=0;
  cineI++;
  if(cineI>=CINE.length){ mode=S.TITLE; }
}
function cineSkip(){ mode=S.TITLE; }

/* ---- INIT ------------------------------------------------------------- */
/* NEW RUN now means: pick HOW you want to play, and then who you are.
   beginRun() is the old newGame — the part that actually builds a run. */
function newGame(){ toModeSelect(); }

let modeSel=0;
function toModeSelect(){ mode=S.MODE; modeSel=0; gaunt=null; }

const modeCard=i=>({ X:36, Y:64+i*66, W:VIEW_W-72, H:58 });

function modeHit(mx,my){
  for(let i=0;i<MODES.length;i++){
    const r=modeCard(i);
    if(mx>=r.X&&mx<r.X+r.W&&my>=r.Y&&my<r.Y+r.H) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function pickMode(i){
  const M=MODES[clamp(i,0,MODES.length-1)];
  if(!M.need()){ shake=140; say('LOCKED — '+M.why); return; }
  MODE=M.id;
  if(M.id==='GAUNTLET'){
    /* No choosing your champion here. The Gauntlet picks, and it picks all of
       them, in an order you do not control. */
    rollGauntlet();
    hero=CHARS.find(h=>h.id===gaunt.order[0]) || CHARS[0];
    const keep=MODE, g=gaunt;
    beginRun();
    MODE=keep; gaunt=g;
    say(`THE GAUNTLET — ${gaunt.order.map(id=>CHARS.find(h=>h.id===id).fam).join(' \u2192 ')}`);
    return;
  }
  toSelect();
}

/* ---- BETWEEN HOUSES (the Gauntlet's roster) ----
   THIS WAS CALLED drawHouse(), AND SO IS THE FARM'S COTTAGE. Two function
   declarations with the same name: the later one wins, silently, so every call
   to drawHouse() — including the Gauntlet's hand-over screen — was drawing a
   little farm building. The test passed because the function existed and threw
   no error. It was drawing the wrong thing, correctly. */
function drawRoster(){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'rgba(8,6,10,.9)');
  const done=gaunt?gaunt.idx:0, total=gaunt?gaunt.order.length:7;
  g.textAlign='center';
  g.fillStyle=C.gold; g.font='18px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.textBaseline='top';
  g.fillText('HADES FALLS AGAIN', VIEW_W/2, 20);
  txtC(`${done} OF ${total} HOUSES HAVE GONE THE DISTANCE`, VIEW_W/2, 44, C.dim, 8);
  g.fillStyle=C.bone; g.font='20px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText(fmtTime(RUN.ms), VIEW_W/2, 60);
  txtC('THE CLOCK RUNS ON', VIEW_W/2, 84, '#5a4a5e', 7);
  g.textAlign='left';

  /* the roster: who has gone, who is next, who is still waiting */
  if(gaunt){
    gaunt.order.forEach((id,i)=>{
      const h=CHARS.find(c=>c.id===id);
      const X=14+i*88, Y=104;
      const state = i<gaunt.idx ? 'done' : i===gaunt.idx ? 'next' : 'wait';
      px(X,Y,82,58, state==='next'?'#241c26':'#100c14');
      px(X,Y, state==='next'?3:1, 58, state==='done'?'#3a5a3a' : state==='next'?h.col : '#2a2430');
      g.globalAlpha = state==='wait' ? .35 : state==='done' ? .5 : 1;
      drawSprite(h.id, 0, SPR_ROW.front, X+41-SPR_W/2, Y+4);
      g.globalAlpha=1;
      g.textAlign='center';
      txtC(h.fam, X+41, Y+40, state==='next'?h.col2:h.col, 8);
      txtC(state==='done'?'\u2713 FELLED HADES' : state==='next'?'\u25b6 YOU, NOW' : 'WAITING',
           X+41, Y+50, state==='done'?'#6adc6a' : state==='next'?C.bone : '#4a3f4a', 6);
      g.textAlign='left';
    });
  }

  g.textAlign='center';
  const nx=CHARS.find(c=>c.id===(gaunt?gaunt.order[gaunt.idx]:hero.id));
  txtC(`${nx.fam} STEPS UP WITH NOTHING — NO RELICS, NO SKILLS, NO HEARTS.`,
       VIEW_W/2, 176, '#c8a0e8', 8);
  txtC('PRESS ENTER OR CLICK TO BEGIN', VIEW_W/2, 192, C.dim, 8);
  g.textAlign='left';
}

/* ==========================================================================
   THE TOWN SCREEN
   ========================================================================= */
const TOWN_TABS=[
  {n:'MARKET',  c:C.gold,    sub:'\u25c6 SHARDS \u2014 relics, bought outright. the RELIC slot is here every day.'},
  {n:'FORGE',   c:'#ff8a5a', sub:'\u25c6 SHARDS + THREE RELICS OF ONE TIER \u2014 melt them, get one of the tier above.'},
  {n:'ANVIL',   c:'#9adcff', sub:'\u25c6 SHARDS \u2014 sharpen a relic you already own. four levels. it is not cheap.'},
  {n:'ALCHEMY', c:'#6adc6a', sub:'\u2663 CROPS \u2014 what you GREW becomes potions and elixirs. the farm keeps you alive.'},
];
const tabBox=i=>({X:14+i*154, Y:8, W:148, H:26});
const rowBox=i=>({X:14, Y:74+i*26, W:SCREEN_W-28, H:24});

function townRows(){
  if(townTab===0){ ensureMarket(); return market.stock.length; }
  if(townTab===3) return BREWS.length;
  return inv.length;
}
function townHit(mx,my){
  for(let i=0;i<TOWN_TABS.length;i++){ const b=tabBox(i);
    if(mx>=b.X&&mx<b.X+b.W&&my>=b.Y&&my<b.Y+b.H) return {tab:i}; }
  for(let i=0;i<townRows();i++){ const r=rowBox(i);
    if(mx>=r.X&&mx<r.X+r.W&&my>=r.Y&&my<r.Y+r.H) return {row:i}; }
  return null;
}
function townAct(i){
  if(townTab===0) buy(i);
  else if(townTab===1) togglePick(i);
  else if(townTab===2) upgrade(i);
  else brew(i);
}

function drawTown(){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'#0d0a10');

  /* your purse, always visible — every stall here wants something from it */
  txt(`\u25c6 ${shards} SHARDS`, SCREEN_W-190, 14, C.gold, 9);
  txt(`\u2663 ${crops} CROPS`,  SCREEN_W-92,  14, C.crop, 9);

  TOWN_TABS.forEach((t,i)=>{
    const b=tabBox(i), on=i===townTab;
