          if(s.pierce>0) s.pierce--; else s.life=0;
        }
      }
      for(const f of foes){
        if(f.hp<=0||s.life<=0) continue;
        if(dst(s,f)>s.r+f.r||s.hitList.includes(f)) continue;
        hurtEnemy(f,s,s.dmg); shake=50;
        s.hitList.push(f);
        if(s.pierce>0) s.pierce--; else { s.life=0; break; }   // pierce = extra enemies
      }
      for(const m of mobs){
        if(m.ally||m.hp<=0||s.life<=0) continue;
        if(dst(s,m)>s.r+m.r||s.hitList.includes(m)) continue;
        hurtEnemy(m,s,s.dmg); s.hitList.push(m);
        if(s.pierce>0) s.pierce--; else { s.life=0; break; }
      }
    } else {
      if(s.kind==='deceit'){
        s.fadeT=(s.fadeT||0)+ms;
        if(s.fadeT>700&&!s.blinked){ s.blinked=1;
          s.x=P.x+rand(60,-60); s.y=P.y+rand(60,-60);
          const a2=ang(s,P), sp=Math.hypot(s.vx,s.vy);
          s.vx=Math.cos(a2)*sp; s.vy=Math.sin(a2)*sp; }
      }
      if(dst(s,P)<s.r+P.r && P.dashT<=0 && P.iframe<=0){
        if(s.kind==='invert'){ P.invert=2000; s.life=0; }
        else if(s.kind==='ice'){ P.frozen=1500; damage(1); s.life=0; }
        else if(s.kind==='coin'){ P.slowT=1400; P.slowV=.55; if(boss)boss.hp-=1; s.life=0; }
        else if(s.kind==='eye'){ P.slowT=1600; P.slowV=.4; s.life=0; }
        else {
          /* THE DAMAGE MODEL, fixed.
             This used to read damage(boss ? 2 : ...) — meaning EVERY projectile
             on screen hit for a full heart the moment a boss was alive, right
             down to a Beelzebub fly's popgun. At 3 hearts that is three
             mistakes and you are dead, which is why the bosses were killing a
             competent bot 13 times out of 21.
             Now: BULLETS CHIP (1). Bodies, beams and telegraphed slams HURT (2).
             Elites still hit for a full heart — that's their whole identity. */

          /* MINERVA (Athena): "Holding the attack button blocks incoming
             projectiles from the front." The shield is up while you're FIRING,
             so you're trading your own rate of fire for a wall you have to aim. */
          if(heroIs('MINERVA') && P.firing){
            const toShot=Math.atan2(s.y-(P.y-8), s.x-P.x);
            const aim=Math.atan2(P.aimY-(P.y-8), P.aimX-P.x);
            const da=Math.abs(((toShot-aim+Math.PI)%6.283)-Math.PI);
            if(da < 1.05){                          // ~60 degrees of shield
              s.life=0; shake=Math.max(shake,30);
              for(let i=0;i<3;i++) burst(s.x,s.y,1,'#c8c8d8');
              continue;
            }
          }

          const base = s.elite ? 2 : (s.dmg||1);
          /* Boss bullets bite harder the deeper you are. Elites already hit for
             a full heart; they scale too, but half as fast. */
          const scale = boss ? dangerTier() : (s.elite ? Math.ceil(dangerTier()/2) : 1);
          damage(base * scale);
          if(!s.pierce)s.life=0;
        }
      }
      if(s.kind==='creep'){ s.ct=(s.ct||0)-ms;
        if(s.ct<=0){ s.ct=120; blight(s.x,s.y,10,2600,'slow'); } }
      if(s.kind==='mortar'&&s.life<3600){ blight(s.x,s.y,16,3000,'fire'); s.life=0; }
    }
  }
  shots=shots.filter(s=>s.life>0);

  for(const f of fx){
    f.life-=ms;
    if(f.grav){ f.vy += f.grav*dt; f.vx *= Math.pow(f.drag||1, dt*60); }
    f.x+=f.vx*dt; f.y+=f.vy*dt;
  }
  fx=fx.filter(f=>f.life>0);
  for(const r of rings) r.life-=ms;
  rings=rings.filter(r=>r.life>0);

  /* ---- ground items: hover to inspect, E to take ---- */
  hoverItem=null;
  for(const gi of ground){
    gi.t+=ms;
    gi.vy=(gi.vy||0)+180*dt; gi.y+=gi.vy*dt;
    if(gi.y>P.y+40) { gi.y=Math.min(gi.y,VIEW_H-24); gi.vy=0; }
    if(gi.vy>0&&hitsWall(gi.x,gi.y+6,4)){ gi.vy=0; }
    if(dst(gi,P)<20) hoverItem=gi;             // walking over it shows the card
  }
}


/* --------------------------------------------------------------------------
   DROPS — the pickup magnet, hearts, shards, potions, items
   -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
function tickDrops(dt, ms){
  /* ---- drops ---- */
  /* MERCER (Hermes): "the hook pulls pickups directly to you from across the
     room." Everyone else gets the ordinary little magnet. */
  const mag  = heroIs('MERCER') ? 14+stats.magnet+280 : 14+stats.magnet;
  const pull = heroIs('MERCER') ? 250 : 90;
  for(const d of drops){
    d.t+=ms;
    if(dst(d,P)<mag&&dst(d,P)>13){             // pickup magnet
      const a=ang(d,P); d.x+=Math.cos(a)*pull*dt; d.y+=Math.sin(a)*pull*dt;
    }
    if(dst(d,P)<14){
      if(d.kind==='heart'){ d.dead=1; P.hp=Math.min(stats.maxHp,P.hp+1); }
      if(d.kind==='shard'){
        d.dead=1; shards+=Math.round(20*(1+stats.shardGain));
        /* THE GREED TRAP is a HALL-room mechanic: grab the loose shards and the
           doors seal until you clear the ambush. It must NOT fire in a boss room.
           A boss reward drops shards too, and the reopen logic only runs in
           S.HALL — so a boss-room seal never lifts. That is a softlock a real
           player can hit, not just the bot: kill Behemoth, walk over the spoils,
           and the exit locks behind you forever. */
        if(!lockedIn && mode===S.HALL && cond && cond.id==='GREED'){
          lockedIn=true; doorOpen=false; shake=300;
          say('GREED — THE DOORS SEAL');
          const sc2=1+globalRoom()/60;
          for(let k=0;k<3;k++){ const [x,y]=freeSpot(); foes.push(makeElite(
            ['grub','imp','husk'][k], x, y, sc2*0.7)); }
        }
      }
      else if(d.kind==='potion'){
        if(potions<POTION_MAX){ potions++; d.dead=1;
          say(`POTION TAKEN (${potions}/${POTION_MAX}) — PRESS Q TO DRINK`); }
      }
      else if(d.kind==='seed'){ seedBank++; d.dead=1; }
      else if(d.kind==='chest'){ d.dead=1; crops+=3; seedBank+=3; say('TREASURE: +3 CROP +3 SEED'); }
      else if(d.kind==='mimic'){ d.dead=1; P.hp=Math.max(1,P.hp-1); crops+=5;
              say('MIMIC! -1 HEART, +5 CROP'); shake=280; }
    }
  }
  drops=drops.filter(d=>!d.dead);
}


/* ==========================================================================
   THE FRAME
   update() was 604 lines — one function holding the player, the enemies, the
   boss, every projectile, every hazard and every pickup. It is the function
   every future bug will live in, so it is now a running order rather than a
   wall: the phases below run in a fixed sequence, and each one is small
   enough to hold in your head.
   ========================================================================= */
function update(dt){
  /* ---- HIT-STOP, before anything else reads the clock ---- */
  const realMs = dt*1000;
  tickClock(realMs);           // the run clock is REAL time: hit-stop cannot pad it
  if(hitStop>0){
    hitStop-=realMs;
    /* The world is frozen — but the SCREEN is not. Shake still decays and the
       renderer still runs, so a freeze looks like a held breath, not a crash. */
    shake=Math.max(0,shake-realMs);
    if(mode!==S.PAUSE) return;
  }
  if(slowMo>0){ slowMo-=realMs; dt*=slowRate; }

  const ms=dt*1000; FMS=ms;
  now+=ms; shake=Math.max(0,shake-ms); msgT=Math.max(0,msgT-ms);
  Music.setContext(musicContext());        // crossfades when the mood changes

  /* ---- ANTI-FREEZE ---- */
  if(mode===S.FARM||mode===S.HALL||mode===S.BOSS){
    /* A NaN position is an instant, permanent freeze: every hitsWall() call
       against NaN returns true, so nothing can move. Catch it immediately. */
    if(!isFinite(P.x)||!isFinite(P.y)){ P.x=VIEW_W/2; P.y=VIEW_H/2; unstick(true); }
    /* Boxed in on all four sides -> you are stuck by definition, whatever put
       you there. Give it a quarter second (knockback, dashes, a boss shove can
       all clip you briefly) and then pull you out. */
    if(boxedIn()){ stuckT+=ms; if(stuckT>250) unstick(true); }
    else stuckT=0;
  }
  if(mode===S.INTRO){
    cineT+=ms; cinePT+=ms;
    const beat=CINE[cineI];
    if(beat && cineT>=beat.dur) cineNext();      // auto-advance; SPACE to hurry
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.TITLE||mode===S.PAUSE||mode===S.SELECT||mode===S.MODE||mode===S.HOUSE||mode===S.TOWN
     ||mode===S.CODEX) return;   // the world stops
  if(mode===S.DEAD||mode===S.WIN||mode===S.SKILL) return;

  /* tag===boss means "the context that scheduled this is still current".
     In a Hall room boss is null AND the tag is null, so room-level callbacks
     (barrel chains) fire. Stale volleys from a dead boss still get dropped. */
  for(const c of sched){ c.t-=ms; if(c.t<=0){ if(c.tag===boss) c.fn(); c.dead=1; } }
  sched=sched.filter(c=>!c.dead);

  tickPlayer(dt, ms);
  tickFoes(dt, ms);
  tickFamiliars(dt, ms);
  tickBoss(dt, ms);
  tickHazards(dt, ms);
  tickShots(dt, ms);
  tickDrops(dt, ms);

  /* ---- clear + exit ---- */
  if(mode===S.HALL&&!doorOpen&&foes.length===0&&!boss){
    doorOpen=true; roomsCleared++;
    /* The room is cleared — now CHOOSE. The last room of a segment leads only
       to the Boss Gate, so there's nothing to pick. */
    if(room < ROOMS_PER_SEG) rollDoors(); else doors=[];
    if(lockedIn) say('THE DOORS RELENT');

    /* PAY OUT what the door promised. */
    const cx0=VIEW_W/2, cy0=VIEW_H/2;
    if(roomKind==='ELITE'){
      dropItem(rollItem(3), cx0, cy0);            // guaranteed Tier-3
      say('THE GAUNTLET YIELDS');
    }
    else if(roomKind==='TRIAL'){
      shards += Math.round(30*(1+stats.shardGain));
      if(Math.random()<.55) dropItem(rollItem(), cx0, cy0);
      say('TRIAL PASSED — DOUBLE SHARDS');
    }
    else if(roomKind==='CURSED'){
      dropItem(rollItem(4), cx0, cy0);            // Tier-4
      shards += Math.round(40*(1+stats.shardGain));
      crops += 6;
      say('THE CURSED DOOR PAYS');
    }
    /* THE CHAIN BREAKS ON CLEAR.
       The leash is 96px. The exit is 288px away. Without this, a TETHERED room
       is literally impossible to leave: you kill everything, the door opens,
       and the chain holds you in the middle of the room forever. */
    if(tether){ tether=null; shake=Math.max(shake,200); say('THE CHAIN SNAPS'); }
    if(stats.healClear) P.hp=Math.min(stats.maxHp,P.hp+stats.healClear);
    if(DYN.has('livingbattery')) P.dashN=dashMax();
  }
  if((mode===S.HALL||mode===S.BOSS)&&doorOpen&&P.x>VIEW_W-TILE-4){
    if(mode===S.HALL && doors.length){
      const d=doorAt(P.y);
      if(!d){ P.x=VIEW_W-TILE-5; }        // between doors: you have to pick one
      else {
        pending=d.id;
        if(d.id==='CURSED') applyCurse();        // the price is paid on entry
        nextRoom();
      }
    } else nextRoom();
  }
}

function phaseOf(b){
  if(b.def.gate) return b.def.gate(b);
  const f=b.hp/b.maxHp, ps=b.def.phases;
  for(let i=0;i<ps.length;i++) if(f>ps[i].min) return i;
  return ps.length-1;
}

/* ============================ ATTACK LIBRARY ============================
   Every boss below is assembled from these. Add a verb here, not in a boss. */

function shot(x,y,a,spd,o={}){
  /* This used to silently DROP `friendly`, `hitList` and `elite`. Two
     consequences, both live:
       - any friendly projectile made through shot() came out HOSTILE
       - elite ranged attacks passed elite:1 and it was thrown away, so they
         have been hitting for 1 instead of 2 this entire time
     Whitelisting fields is how that happens. Carry them. */
  shots.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*spd,vy:Math.sin(a)*spd,
    r:o.r||4,dmg:o.dmg||1,life:o.life||4000,col:o.col||C.hostile,
    homing:o.homing||0,pierce:o.pierce||0,bounce:o.bounce||0,
    split:o.split||0,spin:o.spin||0,curve:o.curve||0,
    friendly:o.friendly||0, hitList:o.hitList||[],
    elite:o.elite||0, wisp:o.wisp||0, shred:o.shred||0,
    kind:o.kind||'',fade:o.fade||0,hp:o.hp||0,a});
}
/* n shots evenly around a circle */
function radial(x,y,n,spd,off=0,o={}){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++) shot(x,y,off+i/n*6.283,spd,o);
}
/* cone aimed at the player */
function aimed(x,y,n,spread,spd,o={}){
  const base=ang({x,y},P);
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const t=n===1?0:(i/(n-1)-.5);
    shot(x,y,base+t*spread,spd,o);
  }
}
/* a horizontal wall of shots with exactly one gap — Charon, Hades Edict */
function gapWave(fromLeft,gapY,spd,o={}){
  const x=fromLeft?8:VIEW_W-8;
  const a=fromLeft?0:Math.PI;
  for(let y=24;y<VIEW_H-16;y+=13){
    if(Math.abs(y-gapY)<22) continue;          // the survivable slot
    shot(x,y,a,spd,{r:5,...o});
  }
}
/* telegraphed circle -> callback. The 1-second read the doc keeps asking for. */
function tel(x,y,r,dur,cb,col){ tels.push({x,y,r,t:0,dur,cb,col:col||C.blood}); }
/* rotating / sweeping beam. Telegraph, then it's live. */
function beam(x,y,a,o={}){
  beams.push({x,y,a,len:o.len||900,w:o.w||5,
    tel:o.tel!==undefined?o.tel:600, live:o.live||1200,t:0,
    rot:o.rot||0, follow:o.follow||null, col:o.col||C.blood, dmg:o.dmg||1});
}
/* ground fluid — the doc's "creep", renamed blight */
function blight(x,y,r,dur,kind='slow'){ blights.push({x,y,r,t:0,dur,kind}); }
function mob(type,x,y,hp,o={}){
  mobs.push({type,x,y,r:o.r||7,hp,hit:0,cool:rand(1200,300),t:0,
             speed:o.speed||34,burrow:0,...o});
}
function burst(x,y,n,col){ for(let i=0;i<n;i++)
  fx.push({x,y,vx:rand(90,-90),vy:rand(90,-90),life:400,max:400,size:2,col:col||C.blood}); }

/* ==========================================================================
   DEATH JUICE
   Enemies used to simply VANISH. No pop, no gibs, no pause — the most common
   event in the game had no punctuation at all.
   ========================================================================= */
let rings=[];                    // expanding shockwave rings

function ring(x,y,r,col,ms=280,w=2){
  rings.push({x,y,r0:4,r1:r,life:ms,max:ms,col,w});
}

/* Chunky bits that fly, tumble and FALL. Not the old weightless confetti. */
function gib(x,y,n,col,power=1){
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=rand(6.283), sp=rand(190,50)*power;
    fx.push({
      x, y,
      vx:Math.cos(a)*sp, vy:Math.sin(a)*sp - rand(70,10),   // thrown up and out
      life:rand(760,420), max:760,
      size: Math.random()<.35 ? 3 : 2,
      col,
      grav: 320,                                            // it comes back down
      drag: 0.86,
    });
  }
}

/* The punctuation on a death. */
function deathFx(e, isLast){
  const col = e.col || C.blood;
  const big = e.elite ? 1.5 : 1;

  gib(e.x, e.y, e.elite?22:12, col, big);
  gib(e.x, e.y, e.elite?8:4, C.bone, big*0.7);              // bone shrapnel
  ring(e.x, e.y, (e.r||8)*(e.elite?4:2.6), e.elite?C.gold:col, 260, e.elite?3:2);

  if(e.elite){ stopHit(90); shake=Math.max(shake,220); }
  else       { stopHit(55); shake=Math.max(shake,90);  }

  /* THE LAST KILL OF A ROOM IS AN EVENT.
     Time stretches, the screen kicks, and a white ring goes out. You feel the
     room end instead of just noticing the door open. */
  if(isLast){
    stopHit(110);
    slowTime(620, 0.32);
    shake=Math.max(shake,300);
    ring(e.x, e.y, 90, C.bone, 480, 3);
    ring(e.x, e.y, 150, col, 620, 2);
    gib(e.x, e.y, 26, col, 1.6);
  }
}

/* ============================== ARENA =================================== */
function baseArena(o={}){
  solid=new Uint8Array(COLS*ROWS);
  for(let y=0;y<ROWS;y++)for(let x=0;x<COLS;x++)
    if(x===0||y===0||x===COLS-1||y===ROWS-1) solid[y*COLS+x]=1;
  arena={hazard:null,shrink:0,dark:0,storm:0,current:0,...o};
}
/* Cyclops: a maze of indestructible blocks */
function mazeArena(){
  baseArena();
  for(let y=3;y<ROWS-3;y+=3)
    for(let x=5;x<COLS-4;x+=4)
      if((x+y)%3) { solid[y*COLS+x]=2; solid[y*COLS+x+1]=2; }
}
/* Charon: a narrow raft over a river of souls */
function raftArena(){
  baseArena({hazard:(x,y)=> (y<6*TILE||y>14*TILE)?'souls':null});
}
/* Leviathan: bottom half is an abyssal chasm */
function chasmArena(){
  baseArena({hazard:(x,y)=> y>11*TILE?'chasm':null});
}
/* Hades P3: arena shrinks to a central platform, perimeter floods */
function riverArena(){
  baseArena({hazard:(x,y)=>{
    const inX=x>13*TILE&&x<27*TILE, inY=y>5*TILE&&y<15*TILE;
    return (inX&&inY)?null:'river';
  }});
}

/* ==========================================================================
   BOSS DEFINITIONS — 20 + Hades
   phases: evaluated top-down; first one whose `min` <= hpFrac is active.
   enter() fires once on transition. tick() runs every frame.
   b.cd is the universal attack cooldown; b.t is ms alive; b.s is scratch state.
   ========================================================================== */
const BOSSDEF=[

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- 1 ---- */
{name:'CERBERUS', hp:120, w:64,h:64, col:'#7a1f34', arena:baseArena,
 desc:'Three heads. Left spews slowing creep, right fires sonic rings, centre homes.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2; b.y=60; b.s.head=0; },
 phases:[
  {min:.6, name:'CHAINED', enter(b){b.speed=0;},
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     if(b.cd>0)return;
     b.s.head=(b.s.head+1)%3;
     if(b.s.head===0){                                   // LEFT: arc of creep
       b.cd=1500;
       for(let i=0;i<7;i++) shot(b.x-22,b.y+10, 1.05+ (i-3)*0.16, 60,
         {col:C.sludge,r:5,kind:'creep'});
     } else if(b.s.head===1){                            // RIGHT: sonic rings
       b.cd=1100;
       for(let i=0;i<3;i++) after(i*130,()=>radial(b.x+22,b.y+10,10,150,i*.3,
         {r:3,col:C.bone,life:1800}));
     } else {                                            // CENTRE: homing stream
       b.cd=1300;
       for(let i=0;i<5;i++) after(i*90,()=>aimed(b.x,b.y+14,1,0,90,
         {homing:2.2,col:C.blood,r:4}));
     }
   }},
  {min:.2, name:'UNCHAINED', enter(b){ b.speed=0; b.s.leap=0; shake=300; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     b.s.leap-=ms;
     if(b.s.leap<=0){                                    // leap -> 12-shot burst on land
       b.s.leap=1900;
       const tx=clamp(P.x+rand(60,-60),40,VIEW_W-40), ty=clamp(P.y+rand(40,-40),40,VIEW_H-40);
       tel(tx,ty,34,650,()=>{ if(!boss)return;
         boss.x=tx; boss.y=ty; shake=200;
         radial(tx,ty,12,105,rand(6.28),{r:4});
         aimed(tx,ty,5,1.1,80,{r:5,col:C.bone});         // the bite cone
       });
     }
   }},
  {min:0, name:'MADDENED', enter(b){ b.speed=52; shake=400; b.w=48;b.h=48; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     const a=ang(b,P);                                   // chase, trailing permanent creep
     b.x+=Math.cos(a)*b.speed*dt; b.y+=Math.sin(a)*b.speed*dt;
     every(b,'trail',90,()=>blight(b.x,b.y,13,6000,'slow'));
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=520; radial(b.x,b.y,9,rand(120,80),rand(6.28),{r:4}); }
   }},
 ]},

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- 2 ---- */
{name:'CHARON', hp:110, w:48,h:64, col:'#4a5a6a', arena:raftArena,
 desc:'Ferryman. Soul-waves sweep the raft; one gap. Then he capsizes it.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W-90; b.y=VIEW_H/2; b.s.gap=160; },
 phases:[
  {min:.5, name:'THE RAFT', enter(b){b.speed=14;},
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     b.y+=Math.sin(b.t/900)*22*dt;
     if(b.cd>0)return;
     if(b.s.k=!b.s.k){                                   // oar: horizontal shockwave
       b.cd=1300;
       for(let i=0;i<4;i++) shot(b.x,b.y-20+i*14,Math.PI,120,{r:6,col:C.bone});
     } else {                                            // soul wave with a moving gap
       b.cd=2400;
       b.s.gap=clamp(P.y+rand(50,-50),50,VIEW_H-60);
       gapWave(true,b.s.gap,72,{col:'#c8d8e8'});
     }
   }},
  /* ==================== CAPSIZED ====================
     THE BUG THIS PHASE HAD: `arena.hazard = () => 'water'` — a function that
     ignores its coordinates and returns water for EVERY TILE. Water does half a
     heart every 600ms. So the whole arena did half a heart every 600ms, forever,
     with nowhere to stand. That is not a hard phase, it is arithmetic: one full
     heart every 1.2 seconds until you are dead. It was unsurvivable, and it was
     unsurvivable by accident.

     What the phase was always MEANT to be is written in the boss's own
     description: "Then he capsizes it." The raft does not vanish — it BREAKS.
     The wreckage is what you stand on. That wreckage is now real: three planks
     of the shattered raft, drifting on the current, and they are the only dry
     ground left in the room.

     The pressure is intact — arguably better. You are not dodging in the open
     any more, you are island-hopping while a ferryman surfaces underneath you,
     and the islands are MOVING. */
  {min:0, name:'CAPSIZED',
   enter(b){
     shake=400; raftArena(); b.s.dive=0;

     /* The raft comes apart into three pieces. One of them is under YOU at the
        moment it flips — you do not get dumped into the water for a mechanic
        you were given no warning of. */
     b.s.planks=[
       {x:clamp(P.x,70,VIEW_W-70), y:clamp(P.y,60,VIEW_H-60), w:72, h:52, vx: 16, vy: 9},
       {x:150, y: 90, w:64, h:46, vx:-13, vy:-11},
       {x:470, y:230, w:64, h:46, vx: 11, vy: 13},
     ];
     arena.planks=b.s.planks;

     /* THE HAZARD NOW READS ITS COORDINATES. Standing on wreckage is dry. */
     arena.hazard=(x,y)=> plankAt(x,y) ? null : 'water';
     say('THE RAFT BREAKS — STAND ON THE WRECKAGE');
   },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     /* the wreckage drifts, and bounces off the walls of the room */
     for(const p of b.s.planks){
       p.x+=p.vx*dt; p.y+=p.vy*dt;
       const hw=p.w/2+TILE, hh=p.h/2+TILE;
       if(p.x<hw){ p.x=hw; p.vx=Math.abs(p.vx); }
       if(p.x>VIEW_W-hw){ p.x=VIEW_W-hw; p.vx=-Math.abs(p.vx); }
       if(p.y<hh){ p.y=hh; p.vy=Math.abs(p.vy); }
       if(p.y>VIEW_H-hh){ p.y=VIEW_H-hh; p.vy=-Math.abs(p.vy); }
     }

     /* Swimming is slow. STANDING IS NOT. The old code slowed you to 55% every
        frame regardless — so even if there had been dry ground, you would have
        waded across it. */
     if(hazardAt(P.x,P.y)==='water') slowTo(.55);

     b.s.dive-=ms;
     if(b.s.dive<=0){                                     // dive, resurface under player
       b.s.dive=2300; b.s.hidden=800;
       const tx=clamp(P.x+rand(40,-40),40,VIEW_W-40), ty=clamp(P.y+rand(40,-40),40,VIEW_H-40);
       tel(tx,ty,30,800,()=>{ if(!boss)return;
         boss.x=tx; boss.y=ty; shake=180;
         for(let i=0;i<14;i++) shot(tx,ty,i/14*6.283,70,
           {homing:1.1,col:C.purple,r:4,life:3000});      // tracking spirits
       },C.purple);
     }
     if(b.s.hidden>0) b.s.hidden-=ms;
   }},
 ]},

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- 3 ---- */
/* The Furies' body was 32x32 — a target smaller than the distance it travelled
   during a bullet's flight time. At ~98px/s of orbit and a 0.57s flight from
   typical range, even a perfectly-led shot arrives where the boss no longer is.
   A dodging bot took a boss from 100% to 86% in SEVENTY-FIVE SECONDS. That is
   not difficulty, it is a geometry problem. Bigger body, slower orbit. */
{name:'THE FURIES', hp:130, tough:0.60, w:44,h:44, col:'#9a2a4a', arena:baseArena,
 desc:'Three sisters. Dive-bomb crosses, then a linked brimstone tether.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2; b.y=70;
   b.s.sis=[{x:200,y:70,a:0},{x:320,y:60,a:2.1},{x:440,y:70,a:4.2}]; },
 phases:[
  {min:.65, name:'FORMATION', enter(b){b.speed=0; b.y=VIEW_H/2;},
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     /* THEY HOVERED AT THE TOP OF THE ARENA (y~90) and orbited. The player,
        fighting from below, landed 39 shots out of ~160 fired — a 25% hit rate.
        Boss HP is derived assuming you connect ~82% of the time, so the fight
        stretched to three minutes and the bot simply ran out of clock.
        The sisters come down and sweep the ROOM now. Closer, hittable, and
        considerably more frightening. */
     b.s.sis.forEach((s,i)=>{ s.a+=dt*.45;
       s.x=VIEW_W/2+Math.cos(s.a+i*2.1)*150;
       s.y=VIEW_H/2+Math.sin(s.a+i*2.1)*70; });
     /* THE HITBOX WAS STALE.
        `b.x=s.x; b.y=s.y` used to sit BELOW the `if(b.cd>0) return`, so the
        boss's damageable body only moved once every 1.6 SECONDS while the three
        sisters were drawn orbiting continuously. You were shooting the sprite
        and hitting empty air — the real hitbox was lingering wherever a sister
        had been up to a second and a half ago. The body tracks the lead sister
        every frame now, which is what the player has been aiming at all along. */
     b.x=b.s.sis[0].x; b.y=b.s.sis[0].y;
     if(b.cd>0)return;
     b.cd=1600;
     const s=b.s.sis[Math.floor(rand(3))];               // sequential dive -> cross line
     const tx=P.x, ty=P.y;
     tel(tx,ty,20,1000,()=>{ if(!boss)return;            // 1-second laceration read
       /* This loop ran 12 times and never used `i` — it stacked 12 identical
          copies of the same five shots, so the "cross" was really SIXTY
          overlapping projectiles dropped on your head every 1.6 seconds. It
          killed a dodging bot three times out of three at segment 3.
          It was always meant to be a cross. Now it is one: four arms, three
          shots deep, expanding outward. Twelve shots, not sixty. */
       shot(tx,ty,0,0,{r:3,col:C.blood,life:900});        // the mark itself
       for(let i=1;i<=3;i++){
         const sp=90+i*45;
         shot(tx,ty, 0,     sp,{r:3});  shot(tx,ty, Math.PI, sp,{r:3});
         shot(tx,ty, 1.57,  sp,{r:3});  shot(tx,ty,-1.57,    sp,{r:3});
       }
       shake=140;
     });
   }},
  {min:.25, name:'WRATH TETHER', enter(b){ b.s.sis.pop(); b.s.rot=0; b.vuln=1.3; shake=250; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     /* Slowed from 0.9 to 0.55. A 32x32 sister orbiting at 135px/s couldn't be
        hit at all once the tether stopped killing you outright — the fight went
        from a 3-for-3 slaughter straight to a three-minute stalemate. */
     b.s.rot+=dt*.55;
     const [a,c]=b.s.sis;
     a.x=VIEW_W/2+Math.cos(b.s.rot)*150; a.y=VIEW_H/2+Math.sin(b.s.rot)*90;
     c.x=VIEW_W/2-Math.cos(b.s.rot)*150; c.y=VIEW_H/2-Math.sin(b.s.rot)*90;
     b.x=a.x;b.y=a.y;
     /* THE TETHER NEEDS A DOWNBEAT.
        It was refreshed every 90ms with tel:0 — a permanently live blade
        sweeping the entire arena with no telegraph and no gap. The bot died to
        it at exactly 64% boss HP, every single time, because there was no
        moment at which crossing the line was survivable.
        Now it CHARGES (you can see it coming) and it BREATHES. The gap between
        pulses is the fight. */
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=1250;
       beams.push({x:a.x,y:a.y,a:ang(a,c),len:dst(a,c),w:4,
                   tel:420, live:620, t:0, rot:0, col:C.ember, dmg:1});
       if(Math.random()<.5) shot(c.x,c.y,rand(6.28),rand(110,60),{r:4});
     }
   }},
  {min:0, name:'GROUNDED', enter(b){ b.s.sis=[b.s.sis[0]]; b.y=VIEW_H/2; b.x=VIEW_W/2; shake=300; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     const a=ang(b,P);                                    // wind push
     pushField(-Math.cos(a)*54,-Math.sin(a)*54);
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=700; radial(b.x,b.y,16,130,rand(6.28),{r:3,col:C.bone}); }
   }},
 ]},

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- 4 ---- */
{name:'MINOTAUR', hp:110, w:64,h:64, col:'#8a3a2a', arena:baseArena,
 desc:'Wall-to-wall charges. Crash = 2s stun window. Then the pillar spin.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2; b.y=VIEW_H/2; b.s.stun=0; b.s.dash=null; },
 phases:[
  {min:.5, name:'THE CHARGE',
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     if(b.s.stun>0){ b.s.stun-=ms; b.vuln=2.6;            // stunned = DPS window
       if(b.s.stun<=0) b.vuln=1; return; }
     if(!b.s.dash){
       if(b.cd>0)return;
       b.cd=900;
       // lock to a cardinal — telegraph, then commit
       const dx=P.x-b.x, dy=P.y-b.y;
       b.s.dash=Math.abs(dx)>Math.abs(dy)?{x:Math.sign(dx),y:0}:{x:0,y:Math.sign(dy)};
       b.s.wind=420;
     }
     if(b.s.wind>0){ b.s.wind-=ms; return; }
     b.x+=b.s.dash.x*300*dt; b.y+=b.s.dash.y*300*dt;
     if(b.x<48||b.x>VIEW_W-48||b.y<48||b.y>VIEW_H-48){   // CRASH
       b.x=clamp(b.x,48,VIEW_W-48); b.y=clamp(b.y,48,VIEW_H-48);
       b.s.dash=null; b.s.stun=2000; b.vuln=2; shake=340;
       for(let i=0;i<8;i++){                              // rocky debris
         const dx2=rand(VIEW_W-40,40), dy2=rand(VIEW_H-40,40);
         tel(dx2,dy2,14,900,()=>radial(dx2,dy2,4,90,rand(6.28),{r:3,col:C.rockLip}));
       }
     }
   }},
  {min:0, name:'THE PILLAR', enter(b){ b.s.spin=0; b.deflect=1; b.s.guard=0; shake=300; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     /* PERMANENT DEFLECT IS A TRAP.
        b.deflect=1 for the whole phase meant every shot you fired came back at
        you as a hostile bullet — 69% of the damage that killed the bot was its
        OWN. You could neither hurt him nor stop hurting yourself.
        Now the guard comes up and drops: ~1.5s spinning (shots bounce), then
        ~1.4s open (hit him). Same fantasy, but it has a rhythm you can read. */
     b.s.guard=(b.s.guard||0)+ms;
     if(b.s.guard>1500 && b.deflect){ b.deflect=0; b.s.guard=0; b.vuln=1.3; }
     else if(b.s.guard>1400 && !b.deflect){ b.deflect=1; b.s.guard=0; b.vuln=1; shake=120; }
     b.s.spin+=dt*(b.deflect?3.4:1.6);
     const a=ang(b,P);                                    // drifts at you while spinning
     b.x+=Math.cos(a)*40*dt; b.y+=Math.sin(a)*40*dt;
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=110;                               // outward spiral of splinters
       shot(b.x,b.y,b.s.spin,140,{r:4,col:C.bone});
       shot(b.x,b.y,b.s.spin+3.14,140,{r:4,col:C.bone});
     }
   }},
 ]},

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- 5 ---- */
{name:'CYCLOPS', hp:150, tough:0.8, w:64,h:64, col:'#6a5a3a', arena:mazeArena,
 desc:'Maze arena. Boulders shatter on walls. Then he blinds himself and breaks it.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W-100; b.y=VIEW_H/2; },
 phases:[
  {min:.45, name:'THE SLAM', enter(b){b.speed=16;},

     const a=ang(b,P); b.x+=Math.cos(a)*b.speed*dt; b.y+=Math.sin(a)*b.speed*dt;
     if(b.cd>0)return; b.cd=2000;
     tel(b.x+Math.cos(a)*50,b.y+Math.sin(a)*50,44,700,()=>{ if(!boss)return;
       shake=280;
       for(let i=0;i<3;i++)                               // exploding boulders
         shot(b.x,b.y,a+(i-1)*.35,95,{r:7,col:C.rockLip,split:8,bounce:0});
       aimed(b.x,b.y,7,1.4,110,{r:4,col:C.rock});         // the cone
     });
   }},
  {min:0, name:'BLIND FURY', enter(b){ shake=420; b.speed=44; b.s.brk=0; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     const a=ang(b,P);
     b.x+=Math.cos(a)*b.speed*dt; b.y+=Math.sin(a)*b.speed*dt;
     // he breaks the maze as he stumbles — arena mutates live
     const cx=Math.floor(b.x/TILE), cy=Math.floor(b.y/TILE);
     for(let dy=-1;dy<=1;dy++)for(let dx=-1;dx<=1;dx++){
       const i=(cy+dy)*COLS+(cx+dx);
       if(solid[i]===2){ solid[i]=0; burst((cx+dx)*TILE+8,(cy+dy)*TILE+8,4,C.rockLip); shake=90; }
     }
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=2600;                              // the sweeping socket laser
       beam(b.x,b.y,ang(b,P),{rot:1.5,live:2000,tel:700,w:7,col:C.blood,len:900});
     }
     every(b,'shock',700,()=>radial(b.x,b.y,8,60,b.t*.002,{r:3,col:C.rock,life:1400}));
   }},
 ]},

{name:'AZAZEL', hp:135, w:64,h:64, col:'#5a4a3a', arena:()=>baseArena({storm:1}),
 desc:'Sandstorm drains you. Stand in the blood pools to live.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2;b.y=VIEW_H/2; b.s.pool=0; b.s.tick=0; },
 phases:[
  {min:.4, name:'THE STORM', enter(b){ arena.storm=1; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     b.s.tick-=ms;
     if(b.s.tick<=0){ b.s.tick=2600; if(!inHealPool()) damage(1,'STORM'); } // environmental drain
     b.s.pool-=ms;
     if(b.s.pool<=0){ b.s.pool=4000;
       blights=blights.filter(x=>x.kind!=='heal');
       for(let i=0;i<2;i++) blight(rand(VIEW_W-90,90),rand(VIEW_H-70,70),30,4200,'heal');
     }
     const a=ang(b,P); b.x+=Math.cos(a)*30*dt; b.y+=Math.sin(a)*30*dt;
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=1400; aimed(b.x,b.y,9,1.9,85,{r:4,col:'#1a1a1a'}); } // black brimstone arc
   }},
  {min:0, name:'THE SLAM', enter(b){ arena.storm=2; shake=350; b.x=VIEW_W/2;b.y=VIEW_H/2; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     b.s.tick-=ms;
     if(b.s.tick<=0){ b.s.tick=1900; if(!inHealPool()) damage(1,'STORM'); }
     b.s.pool-=ms;
     if(b.s.pool<=0){ b.s.pool=2400;                       // pools move faster
       blights=blights.filter(x=>x.kind!=='heal');
       blight(rand(VIEW_W-90,90),rand(VIEW_H-70,70),26,2600,'heal');
     }

{name:'LEVIATHAN', hp:160, w:64,h:64, col:'#2a4a6a', arena:chasmArena,
 desc:'Tidal waves push you up. Then the maw opens and pulls you in.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2;b.y=VIEW_H-70; },
 phases:[
  {min:.5, name:'THE BREACH',
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     b.x=VIEW_W/2+Math.sin(b.t/1300)*160;
     if(b.cd>0)return; b.cd=2200;
     if(b.s.k=!b.s.k){
       tel(b.x,b.y-30,50,700,()=>{ shake=300;              // tail slam -> tidal push
         impulse(0,-260);
         /* A 24-shot wall at 26px spacing, WHILE an impulse throws you upward
            into it. There was no gap to aim for. Now the wave breaks around a
            readable gap, and the wall is thinner. */
         const gap = 60 + Math.floor(rand(4))*130;         // a channel to swim for
         for(let x=20;x<VIEW_W;x+=36){
           if(Math.abs(x-gap)<44) continue;
           shot(x,VIEW_H-40,-1.5708,110,{r:5,col:C.waterLit});
         }
       },C.waterLit);
     } else {
       shot(b.x,b.y-40,ang(b,P),42,{r:9,col:'#6ad8e8',split:12,hp:3,kind:'orb'}); // splitting orb
     }
   }},
  {min:0, name:'THE GATE', enter(b){ b.x=VIEW_W-70; b.y=VIEW_H/2; b.nocontact=1; shake=400; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     /* DOUBLE JEOPARDY.
        The maw drags you toward it, and the maw's body deals contact damage —
        so being pulled in (the mechanic working as designed) ground the player
        to death against its face. 62% of all damage taken in this fight was
        boss contact. Being pulled should be POSITIONAL pressure.
        Now the maw spits you back out instead of chewing on you. */
     const a=ang(P,b);                                     // suction (capped: always escapable)
     pushField(Math.cos(a)*58,Math.sin(a)*58);
     if(Math.abs(P.x-b.x)<b.w/2+4 && Math.abs(P.y-b.y)<b.h/2+4){
       const out=ang(b,P);
       P.x+=Math.cos(out)*7; P.y+=Math.sin(out)*7;         // shoved clear of the teeth
       impulse(Math.cos(out)*300, Math.sin(out)*300);
     }
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=2400;                               // alternating sweeping eye lasers
       beam(b.x,b.y-14,Math.PI,{rot:.55,live:1600,tel:600,w:6,col:C.blood,len:900});
       beam(b.x,b.y+14,Math.PI,{rot:-.55,live:1600,tel:600,w:6,col:C.blood,len:900});
     }
   }},
 ]},

{name:'HELLHOUND', hp:120, w:64,h:48, col:'#1a1a22', arena:baseArena,
 desc:'Runs laps, then lunges. The trail burns.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=80;b.y=60; b.s.lap=0; },
 phases:[
  {min:.5, name:'THE HUNT',
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     /* A LUNGE HAS TO HAVE A RECOVERY.
        This dog lapped the arena at ~287px/s without ever stopping, so a shot
        could never lead it. It took 87 seconds to kill while barely landing a
        hit on the player. Now it overshoots the lunge and has to skid to a
        halt — and while it's panting, it's yours. */
     if(b.s.pant>0){ b.s.pant-=ms; b.vuln=1.4; return; }      // SPENT — hit him
     b.vuln=1;
     if(b.s.lungeT>0){ b.s.lungeT-=ms; b.x+=b.s.lv.x*dt; b.y+=b.s.lv.y*dt;
       blight(b.x,b.y,13,2600,'fire');
       if(b.s.lungeT<=0){ b.s.pant=1100; shake=180; }         // skid, then pant
       return; }
     b.s.lap+=dt*0.85;                                        // slower laps
     b.x=VIEW_W/2+Math.cos(b.s.lap)*250; b.y=VIEW_H/2+Math.sin(b.s.lap)*120;
     /* "The trail burns" — it said so on the tin, and it didn't. With a punish
        window added, the dog dropped to 3% of your health bar: no longer
        unkillable, now simply harmless. It burns as it runs. */
     every(b,'trail',180,()=>blight(b.x,b.y,11,1800,'fire'));
     every(b,'bay',1000,()=>aimed(b.x,b.y,3,.55,150,{r:4,col:C.fire}));
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=2300;
       const a=ang(b,P);
       tel(b.x,b.y,20,450,()=>{ if(!boss)return;
         boss.s.lv={x:Math.cos(a)*330,y:Math.sin(a)*330}; boss.s.lungeT=680;
         aimed(boss.x,boss.y,5,.9,140,{r:4,col:C.fire});
       },C.fire);
     }
   }},
  {min:0, name:'BLAZING SPECTER', enter(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2;b.y=VIEW_H/2; shake=360;
     mob('pup',VIEW_W/2-60,VIEW_H/2,4,{speed:64,r:6});
     mob('pup',VIEW_W/2+60,VIEW_H/2,4,{speed:64,r:6}); },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=90;                                   // rotating cross of fire
       const a=b.t*.0016;
       for(let i=0;i<4;i++) shot(b.x,b.y,a+i*1.5708,120,{r:4,col:C.fire,life:2200});
     }

       });
     }
   }},
  {min:0, name:'THE ANCHOR', enter(b){ b.s.ax=VIEW_W/2; b.s.ay=VIEW_H/2; b.s.sw=0; b.s.rest=0; shake=380; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     /* THE PUNISH WINDOW.
        He used to swing forever at ~176px/s — faster than you can lead a shot.
        Measured: a competent bot could not kill him in three minutes while
        taking almost no damage. That isn't difficulty, it's a stalemate.
        Now he swings in bursts and then hangs off the club, panting. That gap
        is where the fight is won. */
     if(b.s.rest>0){                                           // SPENT — hit him
       b.s.rest-=ms; b.vuln=1.35;                              // and it hurts more
       b.x=b.s.ax+Math.cos(b.s.sw)*110; b.y=b.s.ay+Math.sin(b.s.sw)*80;
       return;
     }
     b.vuln=1;
     b.s.sw+=dt*1.15;                                          // slower swing
     b.s.arc=(b.s.arc||0)+dt*1.15;
     b.x=b.s.ax+Math.cos(b.s.sw)*110; b.y=b.s.ay+Math.sin(b.s.sw)*80;
     if(b.s.arc > 6.283){ b.s.arc=0; b.s.rest=1500; shake=200; }  // one full lap, then rest
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=140;
       radial(b.x,b.y,3,105,b.s.sw,{r:4,col:C.fire});
       beams.push({x:b.s.ax,y:b.s.ay,a:b.s.sw,len:140,w:6,tel:0,live:140,t:0,rot:0,
                   col:C.rockLip,dmg:1});                       // the club itself
     }
   }},
 ]},

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- 17 ---- */
/* GORGON WAS TANKIER THAN HADES.
   `tough` is a per-boss HP multiplier, and every other boss in the game uses it
   to bring HP DOWN — 0.6, 0.68, 0.78, 0.8, 0.85. Gorgon was the only one above
   1.0, at 1.55, which pushed its derived HP to 241: MORE THAN THE FINAL BOSS
   (201) and 70% above the next-highest ordinary boss. It measured at 77s to kill
   against a ~40s average, and cost 14 hearts doing it — the longest, most
   punishing fight in the game, sitting at segment 17.
   1.0 puts it at ~155 HP: still the toughest of the ordinary bosses, and still
   below Hades, which is where the penultimate fight belongs. */
{name:'GORGON', hp:185, tough:1.0, w:48,h:64, col:'#3a7a5a', arena:baseArena,
 desc:'When the beam charges, FACE AWAY or be rooted.',
 spawn(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2;b.y=50; },
 phases:[
  {min:.5, name:'PETRIFY',
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     b.x=VIEW_W/2+Math.sin(b.t/1400)*200;                     // slides the back wall
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=3000; b.s.gaze=1400;                   // TELL: look away
       tel(b.x,b.y,26,1400,()=>{ if(!boss)return;
         boss.s.gaze=0;
         // facing check: are you looking at her?
         const toB=ang(P,boss);
         const look=Math.atan2(P.dy,P.dx);
         const diff=Math.abs(((toB-look+Math.PI)%6.283)-Math.PI);
         if(diff<1.2 && !god){ P.frozen=2000; shake=200; }     // rooted
         radial(boss.x,boss.y,10,110,0,{r:4,col:C.toxic});
       },C.toxic);
     }
     if(b.s.gaze>0) b.s.gaze-=ms;
     every(b,'hair',420,()=>shot(b.x,b.y+10,rand(6.28),rand(90,50),{r:3,col:C.toxic})); // snake hair
   }},
  {min:0, name:'SHED SKIN', enter(b){ b.x=VIEW_W/2;b.y=VIEW_H/2; shake=320; },
   tick(b,dt,ms){
     if(b.cd<=0){ b.cd=2200;
       const ox=b.x,oy=b.y;
       tel(ox,oy,24,700,()=>radial(ox,oy,12,130,0,{r:4,col:C.toxic}),C.toxic); // explosive decoy
       b.x=rand(VIEW_W-70,70); b.y=rand(VIEW_H-60,60); burst(b.x,b.y,6,C.toxic);
       for(let i=0;i<3;i++) after(i*180,()=>
         shot(boss.x,boss.y,ang(boss,P),75,{homing:1.8,col:C.toxic,r:5,kind:'venom'}));
     }
   }},
 ]},

  {min:0, name:'THE MASKS', enter(b){ shake=380; b.s.masks=[0,2.09,4.18]; b.w=32;b.h=32; },

/* ============================ RENDER =================================== */
const back=document.createElement('canvas');
back.width=SCREEN_W; back.height=SCREEN_H;    // the canvas is the SCREEN, not the arena
const g=back.getContext('2d'); g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
const screen=document.getElementById('screen');
const sc=screen.getContext('2d');
/* ==========================================================================
   FITTING 640x320 TO A REAL SCREEN

   The base resolution is chosen, not inherited: 640 divides EXACTLY into every
   common display width — 1920 = 3x, 2560 = 4x, 3840 = 6x. Whole numbers, every
   time. That matters more than it sounds, because a fractional scale (2.7x) puts
   source pixels on half-positions: they come out as uneven blocks that shimmer
   as you walk. Integer scaling is the entire reason this looks crisp.

   TWO BUGS LIVED HERE.

   1. The old code reserved 40px of width and 100px of height for the page
      margins BEFORE flooring. On a 1080p monitor that dropped the scale from
      3x to 2x — the game rendered at 1280x640 and left a THIRD OF THE SCREEN
      EMPTY. The reserve was costing a whole integer step on the single most
      common display there is.

   2. devicePixelRatio was never handled at all. On a HiDPI screen — a Mac, or
      Windows at 150% scaling — one CSS pixel is not one device pixel. The
      canvas got a clean integer scale in CSS space and was then stretched by
      1.5x or 2x on the way to the actual glass. At dpr 1.5 that is a fractional
      blow-up of already-scaled art: uneven pixels, exactly what integer scaling
      exists to prevent.

   So: measure in DEVICE pixels, floor there, and hand the browser a canvas whose
   backing store is an exact integer multiple of 640x320. The CSS size is then
   whatever makes that land 1:1 on the glass.
   ========================================================================= */
/* NO RESERVE. Any pixels held back here get FLOORED away and cost a whole
   integer step — that is the exact bug that was capping 1080p at 2x before, and
   reserving 72px for the legend would have re-introduced it on the taller base
   (1008/360 = 2.8 -> 2x instead of 3x). The legend simply sits below the fold
   and the page scrolls; the game takes the pixels.
   In FULLSCREEN (press F) innerHeight is the whole panel, so 1080p lands on
   exactly 3x = 1920x1080: the entire screen, pixel for pixel. */
const VREserve = 0;
let scale=2;                      // the integer scale, in DEVICE pixels

function fit(){
  /* Guard it the way the Store adapter does — `window` is not a given in every
     host, and fit() runs at load, so an unguarded reference takes the whole
     game down before the first frame. */
  const dpr = Math.max(1, (typeof window!=='undefined' && window.devicePixelRatio) || 1);

  /* how many DEVICE pixels do we actually have to play with? */
  const availW = innerWidth * dpr;
  const availH = (innerHeight - VREserve) * dpr;

  scale = Math.max(1, Math.floor(Math.min(availW/SCREEN_W, availH/SCREEN_H)));

  /* the backing store is an exact integer multiple — this is the pixel grid */
  screen.width  = SCREEN_W*scale;
  screen.height = SCREEN_H*scale;

  /* ...and the CSS size lays those device pixels down 1:1 on the glass */
  screen.style.width  = (SCREEN_W*scale/dpr)+'px';
  screen.style.height = (SCREEN_H*scale/dpr)+'px';

  sc.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
}
addEventListener('resize',fit); fit();

/* F — fullscreen. At 3x on a 1080p panel the game is 1920x960 and the browser
   chrome is the only thing standing between you and the whole screen. */
function toggleFullscreen(){
  try{
    if(!document.fullscreenElement){
      if(document.documentElement.requestFullscreen) document.documentElement.requestFullscreen();
    } else if(document.exitFullscreen){
      document.exitFullscreen();
    }
  }catch(e){ /* the browser said no; nothing is broken by that */ }
}

const px=(x,y,w,h,c)=>{g.fillStyle=c;g.fillRect(x|0,y|0,w,h);};
const cir=(x,y,r,c)=>{g.fillStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.arc(x|0,y|0,r,0,7);g.fill();};
const txt=(t,x,y,c,s=9)=>{g.fillStyle=c;g.font=s+'px ui-monospace,monospace';g.textBaseline='top';g.fillText(t,x,y);};
/* centred text — the select screen leans on it */
const txtC=(t,x,y,c,s=9)=>{g.fillStyle=c;g.font=s+'px ui-monospace,monospace';g.textBaseline='top';
  const al=g.textAlign; g.textAlign='center'; g.fillText(t,x,y); g.textAlign=al;};

/* ---- ARENA BACKGROUNDS (embedded, one per boss; decoded into <img>s) ---- */
const BG_IMG={};
(function(){
  for(const name in BOSS_BG){
    const im=new Image(); im.__ready=false;
    im.onload=()=>{ im.__ready=true; };
    im.src=BOSS_BG[name];
    BG_IMG[name]=im;
  }
})();
/* backdrop for the active fight, or null (regular rooms / farm / still decoding) */
function arenaBgImage(){
  if(!boss) return null;                 // only during a boss encounter
  const im=BG_IMG[boss.def.name];
  return (im && im.__ready) ? im : null;
}

function drawGround(){
  const B = (mode===S.HALL) ? biome : null;
  const F=B?B.f:C.floor, F2=B?B.f2:C.floorAlt, W=B?B.w:C.wall,
        WL=B?B.wl:C.wallLip, RK=B?B.r:C.rock, RL=B?B.rl:C.rockLip;
  const BG=arenaBgImage();
  if(BG){ g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false; g.drawImage(BG,0,0,VIEW_W,VIEW_H); }  // arena backdrop
  for(let y=0;y<ROWS;y++)for(let x=0;x<COLS;x++){
    const X=x*TILE,Y=y*TILE,s=solid[y*COLS+x];
    if(s===1){ px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,W); px(X,Y+TILE-3,TILE,3,WL); }
    else if(s===2){ px(X+1,Y+1,TILE-2,TILE-2,RK); px(X+1,Y+1,TILE-2,3,RL); }
    else if(s===3){                                  // VINE — cover. Stops shots.
      px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,(x+y)%2?F:F2);
      px(X+2,Y+1,2,14,'#3c5c30'); px(X+7,Y,2,16,'#4a6e38'); px(X+12,Y+2,2,13,'#3c5c30');
      px(X+4,Y+4,3,2,'#6a9a48'); px(X+9,Y+9,3,2,'#6a9a48'); px(X+1,Y+11,3,2,'#6a9a48');
    }
    else if(s===4){                                  // PIT — you fall, bullets don't
      px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,'#07050a');
      px(X,Y,TILE,2,'#1a1420');
    }
    else if(s===5){                                  // HELL-FUEL BARREL
      px(X+2,Y+2,12,13,'#6a3a1a');
      px(X+2,Y+2,12,2,'#9a5a2a');
      px(X+2,Y+7,12,2,'#3a1e0e');
      px(X+6,Y+4,4,4,C.ember);
      px(X+7,Y-1,2,3,C.sulfur);                      // the fuse
    }
    else {
      const h=hazardAt(X+8,Y+8);
      const plank = h===null ? plankAt(X+8,Y+8) : null;
      if(plank){
        /* THE WRECKAGE. Dry ground has to LOOK like dry ground — an invisible
           safe tile is the same as no safe tile. */
        px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,'#5a4028');
        px(X,Y,TILE,2,'#7a5a3a');                       // the lit top edge
        if((x+y)%2) px(X+2,Y+6,TILE-4,1,'#42301e');     // grain
        px(X,Y+TILE-1,TILE,1,'#2e2016');
        /* foam where the wreck meets the water */
        if(!plankAt(X+8, Y+8+TILE)) px(X,Y+TILE-2,TILE,2,'#8aa8c0');
      }
      else if(h==='water'||h==='river'){ px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,C.water);
        if((x+y+Math.floor(now/300))%4===0)px(X+3,Y+6,6,2,C.waterLit); }
      else if(h==='souls'){ px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,'#1a2438');
        if((x*3+y+Math.floor(now/400))%5===0)px(X+5,Y+5,3,3,'#7a8ab8'); }
      else if(h==='chasm'){ px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,'#0a1420'); }
      else if(mode===S.FARM){ if(!BG){ px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,C.ash); px(X,Y,TILE,2,C.ashTop); } }
      else if(!BG) px(X,Y,TILE,TILE,(x+y)%2?F:F2);
    }
  }
  if(mode!==S.HALL) return;
  /* --- THE DOORS. The whole point is that you can READ them before you commit. */
  if(doorOpen && doors.length){
    const near=doorAt(P.y);
    for(const d of doors){
      const X=(COLS-1)*TILE, Y=d.lane*TILE;
      const hot = near===d;
      /* the doorway itself */
      px(X-2,Y-8,TILE+2,TILE*2+16,'#000');
      px(X-1,Y-7,TILE,TILE*2+14, hot?'#241a24':'#120d14');
      /* frame, lit in the door's colour */
      px(X-3,Y-9,2,TILE*2+18, d.c);
      px(X-3,Y-9,TILE+4,2, d.c);
      px(X-3,Y+TILE*2+7,TILE+4,2, d.c);
      /* a pulse when you're standing in it */
      if(hot){ g.globalAlpha=.25+.15*Math.sin(now/180);
        px(X-14,Y-9,TILE+14,TILE*2+18,d.c); g.globalAlpha=1; }

      /* the icon */
      const ix=X+3, iy=Y+TILE-4;
      if(d.icon==='sword'){ px(ix+3,iy-8,2,12,'#c8c0b4'); px(ix,iy+2,8,2,'#8a7a6a'); }
      else if(d.icon==='skull'){ px(ix+1,iy-7,7,7,'#e8e0d0'); px(ix+2,iy-5,2,2,'#000'); px(ix+5,iy-5,2,2,'#000'); px(ix+2,iy+1,5,2,'#e8e0d0'); }
      else if(d.icon==='chest'){ px(ix,iy-6,9,8,'#8a6a2a'); px(ix,iy-3,9,2,'#e0b93c'); px(ix+4,iy-2,2,3,'#e0b93c'); }
      else if(d.icon==='rune'){ px(ix+3,iy-8,2,11,d.c); px(ix,iy-5,9,2,d.c); px(ix,iy-1,9,2,d.c); }
      else if(d.icon==='curse'){ px(ix+1,iy-8,7,3,d.c); px(ix+3,iy-5,3,8,d.c); px(ix,iy-2,9,2,d.c); }

      /* the label — the actual decision, in words */
      const lw=126, lx=X-lw-16, ly=Y-6;
      g.globalAlpha = hot?1:.72;
      px(lx,ly,lw,40,'#0b080d');
      px(lx,ly,2,40,d.c);
      txt(d.n, lx+7, ly+4, d.c, 9);
      txt(d.risk,   lx+7, ly+16, '#b8607a', 7);
      txt(d.reward, lx+7, ly+26, C.crop, 7);
      g.globalAlpha=1;
    }
    if(!near){
      g.textAlign='center';
      g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
      g.fillText('STEP INTO A DOOR TO CHOOSE', VIEW_W/2, VIEW_H-30);
      g.textAlign='left';
    }
  }

  /* GORE's sword arcs — the swing you actually see */
  for(const w of swipes){
    const k=w.t/w.dur;
    g.globalAlpha=(1-k)*0.9;
    const spread=1.15;
    const steps=9;
    for(let i=0;i<=steps;i++){
      const a=w.a-spread + (i/steps)*spread*2;
      const rr=w.reach*(0.55+0.45*k);
      const x=w.x+Math.cos(a)*rr, y=w.y+Math.sin(a)*rr;
      px(x-2,y-2,4,4, rageT>0?'#ff9060':'#e8d0c0');
    }
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }

  /* the tether: a chain from the pillar to your ribs */
  if(tether){
    px(tether.x-6,tether.y-6,12,12,RL);
    px(tether.x-4,tether.y-4,8,8,RK);
    const n=Math.floor(dst(P,tether)/7);
    for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
      const u=i/n;
      px(tether.x+(P.x-tether.x)*u-1, (tether.y+(P.y-14-tether.y)*u)-1, 3,2,
         i%2?'#7a6a5a':'#a8967e');
    }
  }
  /* sawblades */
  for(const w of saws){
    g.strokeStyle='#3a342c'; g.lineWidth=1;
    g.beginPath();
    if(w.horiz){ g.moveTo(w.a-w.span,w.b); g.lineTo(w.a+w.span,w.b); }
    else { g.moveTo(w.b,w.a-w.span); g.lineTo(w.b,w.a+w.span); }
    g.stroke();
    const sp=now/40;
    cir(w.x,w.y,w.r,'#8a7e6e');
    for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
      const a=sp+i*.785;
      px(w.x+Math.cos(a)*w.r-1, w.y+Math.sin(a)*w.r-1, 3,3,'#c8bcae');
    }
    cir(w.x,w.y,3,'#4a423a');
  }
}

function drawPlots(){
  for(let y=0;y<FARM_H;y++)for(let x=0;x<FARM_W;x++){
    const p=plots[y*FARM_W+x];
    const X=(FARM_X+x)*TILE, Y=(FARM_Y+y)*TILE;
    if(!p.tilled) continue;
    px(X,Y,TILE,TILE, p.crop&&p.crop.wet?C.soilWet:C.soil);
    for(let f=2;f<TILE;f+=4) px(X+1,Y+f,TILE-2,1,C.furrow);
    if(!p.crop) continue;
    const s=p.crop.stage;
    if(s===0) px(X+7,Y+11,2,2,C.stem);
    else if(s===1){px(X+7,Y+8,2,5,C.stem);px(X+5,Y+8,2,2,C.crop);px(X+9,Y+8,2,2,C.crop);}
    else if(s===2){px(X+7,Y+4,2,9,C.stem);px(X+4,Y+6,3,2,C.crop);px(X+9,Y+8,3,2,C.crop);}
    else{ px(X+7,Y+5,2,8,C.stem);px(X+4,Y+9,3,2,C.crop);px(X+9,Y+9,3,2,C.crop);
      const pl=Math.sin(now/240+x)>0?C.sulfur:C.ember;
      px(X+5,Y+2,6,5,C.ember); px(X+6,Y+3,4,3,pl); }
  }
  /* tile highlight under the cursor, if it's a reachable plot */
  const cx=Math.floor(P.aimX/TILE), cy=Math.floor(P.aimY/TILE);
  if(plotAt(cx,cy)>=0){
    const reach=Math.hypot(P.aimX-P.x,P.aimY-P.y)<=46;
    g.strokeStyle=reach?C.sulfur:'#5a4450'; g.lineWidth=1;
    g.strokeRect(cx*TILE+.5,cy*TILE+.5,TILE-1,TILE-1);
  }
}

function drawHouse(){
  px(2*TILE,2*TILE,5*TILE,3*TILE,'#2a1620');
  px(2*TILE,2*TILE,5*TILE,4,C.blood);
  px(4*TILE,3*TILE+6,TILE,TILE+10,'#120a10');
  txt('HOME',2*TILE+10,TILE+4,C.dim,8);
}

/* THE TOWN GATE — west. An exit the player will never find if it is not drawn,
   and the whole economy hangs off it. Deliberately warm: a lit doorway, wheat
   colours, the opposite of the portal's cold ring on the far side of the farm. */
function drawTownGate(){
  const X=0, Y=10*TILE-34, W=TILE*2, H=68;
  px(X, Y, W, H, '#2a2018');
  px(X, Y, W, 3, '#6a5a3a');
  px(X, Y+H-3, W, 3, '#6a5a3a');
  px(X+W-4, Y, 4, H, '#4a3a26');
  /* the doorway itself, lit from inside */
  const g2=6+Math.sin(now/420)*2;
  px(X+4, Y+14, W-12, 40, '#c8a05a');
  px(X+4, Y+14+g2, W-12, 40-g2*2, '#f0d090');
  /* a lantern */
  px(X+W-9, Y+8, 5, 5, '#ffd070');
  const near = mode===S.FARM && P.x<TILE*5 && Math.abs(P.y-10*TILE)<TILE*3.4;
  g.textAlign='center';
  txtC('TOWN', X+W/2+10, Y-13, near?C.gold:'#7a6a4a', 8);
  if(near) txtC('\u2190 WALK IN', X+W/2+22, Y+H+4, C.bone, 7);
  g.textAlign='left';
}

function drawPortal(){
  const X=(COLS-2)*TILE, Y=8*TILE;
  px(X-2,Y-4,TILE+4,TILE*4+8,'#1a0f14');
  px(X-2,Y-4,3,TILE*4+8,C.ember);
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){ const o=Math.sin(now/300+i*1.2)*3;
    px(X+4,Y+4+i*12+o,7,7, i%2?C.sulfur:C.ember); }
  txt('HALL OF MIRRORS',X-104,Y-18,C.sulfur,8);
  txt('>>',X-18,Y+28,C.ember,10);
}

function drawDoor(){
  const dy=(ROWS/2-1)*TILE, dx=(COLS-1)*TILE;
  if(doorOpen){ px(dx,dy,TILE,TILE*2,'#1a0f14'); px(dx,dy,3,TILE*2,C.ember); px(dx+6,dy+12,4,8,C.sulfur); }
  else { px(dx,dy,TILE,TILE*2,'#2a1620'); px(dx+4,dy+14,8,4,C.blood); }
}

function drawPlayer(){
  /* dash afterimages — Phantom Step reads as a real thing */
  for(const t of P.dashTrail){
    g.globalAlpha=t.life/220*.4;
    px(t.x-5,t.y-24,10,22,C.purple);
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  if(P.iframe>0 && P.dashT<=0 && Math.floor(now/70)%2) return;
  const x=Math.round(P.x)-CHAR/2;
  const b=Math.round(P.y)-CHAR+(Math.floor(P.anim)%2?1:0);
  px(x+10,b+29,12,2,'rgba(0,0,0,.4)');            // the shadow, for everyone

  /* ---- HAND-DRAWN SPRITE, if this bloodline has one ---- */
  if(hasSheet(hero.id)){
    const row=SPR_ROW[facingOf(P.aimX, P.aimY, P.x, P.y)];
    /* Moving -> the walk cycle. Standing -> the idle breath.
       P.anim is exactly this signal already: it is reset to 0 the moment you
       stop and climbs while you move, so the walk reads at the speed you
       actually travel — a Hermes heir with +30% speed visibly hurries. */
    const moving = P.anim > 0;
    let col;
    if(P.atkT>0){
      /* ATTACKING beats walking beats standing. Drive, then settle. */
      const k=P.atkT/Math.max(1,P.atkMax);        // 1 at the moment of the blow -> 0
      col = k>0.45 ? SPR_STRIKE : SPR_WINDUP;
    }
    else if(moving) col = 2 + (Math.floor(P.anim) % 4);   // 4 walk frames
    else            col = Math.floor(now/420) % 2;        // 2 idle frames, a slow breath
    const tint = P.frozen>0 ? C.ice
               : P.invert>0 ? C.purple
               : null;
    /* RECOIL. A gun kicks you BACK; a broadsword drags you THROUGH. Two
       characters, two opposite shoves, from the same three lines. */
    let ox=0, oy=0;
    if(P.atkT>0){
      const k=P.atkT/Math.max(1,P.atkMax);
      const a=Math.atan2(P.aimY-(P.y-8), P.aimX-P.x);
      const push = heroIs('GORE') ? 5*k : -2.5*k;
      ox=Math.cos(a)*push; oy=Math.sin(a)*push*0.55;
    }
    if(drawSprite(hero.id, col, row,
                  Math.round(P.x+ox)-SPR_W/2, Math.round(P.y+oy)-SPR_H, tint)){
      drawHeldTool();                              // the weapon still tracks the cursor
      return;
    }
  }

  /* ---- otherwise, the procedural body ---- */
  px(x+11,b+18,10,11, P.frozen>0?C.ice : P.invert>0?C.purple : C.cloak);
  px(x+11,b+18,10,2,C.cloakLit);
  px(x+12,b+8,8,10,C.skin);
  px(x+9,b+4,3,6,C.bone); px(x+20,b+4,3,6,C.bone);
  px(x+11,b+6,10,3,C.cloakLit);
  px(x+13,b+12,2,2,C.ember); px(x+18,b+12,2,2,C.ember);
  drawHeldTool();
}

/* The weapon / farm tool, pointing wherever the cursor is. Shared by the sprite
   path and the procedural path, so they can never drift apart. */
function drawHeldTool(){
  /* the weapon/tool points at the cursor */
  const a=Math.atan2(P.aimY-(P.y-8), P.aimX-P.x);
  const hx=P.x+Math.cos(a)*11, hy=(P.y-14)+Math.sin(a)*8;

  /* THE HAMMER IS GONE TOO.
     A brown stick with a crossbar on the end, bolted to your hand and swinging
     round with the cursor. It was the same stand-in idea as the white weapon
     block: something to hold, from back when the player had nothing of their own.
     Every heir now carries their real tool in the art, and the HUD band names the
     selected one outright ("TOOL GRAIN SCYTHE (1-4)"), so the little mallet was
     telling you nothing you could not already see — while covering up the art
     that was telling you properly. */
  if(mode===S.FARM){
    if(hasSheet(hero.id)) return;              // she has her own tools
    /* the procedural body still has nothing to hold */
    px(hx-1,hy-3,2,8,'#6b4a32');
    px(hx-3,hy-5,6,2, P.tool===3?'#4aa8d8' : P.tool===4?C.bone : C.rockLip);
    return;
  }

  /* THE WHITE BLOCK IS GONE.
     That bone-coloured rectangle was a stand-in WEAPON, drawn back when the
     player was six coloured boxes and had no weapon of their own. Every heir now
     carries their own — Freya's scepter, Gore's broadsword, Minerva's spear —
     drawn into the art, in their own palette. Painting a white brick on top of a
     hand-drawn weapon just covers it up.
     What survives is the muzzle spark: the one thing the sprite CAN'T show,
     because it happens at the tip of a weapon that's pointing wherever your
     mouse is. And it only appears at the moment you actually fire. */
  if(hasSheet(hero.id)){
    if(P.atkT>0 && !heroIs('GORE')){                 // Gore has no muzzle — he swings
      const k=P.atkT/Math.max(1,P.atkMax);
      const tx=hx+Math.cos(a)*4, ty=hy+Math.sin(a)*4;
      g.globalAlpha=k;
      px(tx-1, ty-1, 3, 3, C.sulfur);
      if(k>0.6) px(tx+Math.cos(a)*2-1, ty+Math.sin(a)*2-1, 2, 2, C.bone);
      g.globalAlpha=1;
    }
    return;
  }

  /* the procedural body has no weapon of its own, so it still needs one drawn */
  px(hx-2,hy-2,5,4,C.bone);
  px(hx+Math.cos(a)*3-1,hy+Math.sin(a)*3-1,3,3,C.sulfur);
}

/* ==========================================================================
   THE RUN SUMMARY
   You used to die and get a menu. Two lines of text and two buttons. Nothing
   about what the run WAS — how far, how long, what you built, what you killed,
   what it cost. That screen is where a player decides whether to press NEW RUN,
   and it was saying nothing.
   ========================================================================= */
function drawSummary(won){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'rgba(8,6,10,.86)');
  g.textBaseline='top';

  /* --- the verdict --- */
  g.textAlign='center';
  const G = modeIs('GAUNTLET');
  if(won){
    g.fillStyle=C.sulfur; g.font='20px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText(G ? 'THE GAUNTLET IS RUN' : 'HELL IS TILLED', VIEW_W/2, 14);
    g.fillStyle=C.crop; g.font='9px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText(G ? 'ALL SEVEN HOUSES. NO DEATHS. NOTHING CARRIED.'
                 : 'HADES HAS FALLEN. THE THRONE IS YOURS.', VIEW_W/2, 38);
  } else {
    g.fillStyle=C.blood; g.font='20px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText(G ? 'THE GAUNTLET IS BROKEN' : 'HARVESTED', VIEW_W/2, 14);
    g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='9px ui-monospace,monospace';
    if(G) g.fillText(`${hero.fam} FELL IN SEGMENT ${seg} — AND TOOK ALL SEVEN DOWN WITH THEM`, VIEW_W/2, 38);
    else  g.fillText(`${hero.fam} OF THE HOUSE OF ${hero.god} — CUT DOWN IN SEGMENT ${seg}`, VIEW_W/2, 38);
  }

  /* --- THE CLOCK, big, because it is the number people chase --- */
  g.fillStyle = won ? C.bone : '#7a6a80';
  g.font='24px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText(fmtTime(RUN.ms), VIEW_W/2, 54);
  const best=(META.best||{})[MODE];
  if(won && best && RUN.ms<=best){
    g.fillStyle=C.gold; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText('\u2605 NEW BEST — '+modeDef().n, VIEW_W/2, 80);
  } else if(best){
    g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText(modeDef().n+' best  '+fmtTime(best), VIEW_W/2, 80);
  } else {
    g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
    g.fillText(modeDef().n, VIEW_W/2, 80);
  }
  g.textAlign='left';

  /* --- what the run WAS: two honest columns --- */
  const L=54, R=VIEW_W/2+24, Y=98, LH=11;
  const left=[
    G ? ['HOUSES', `${RUN.housesCleared}/7 CLEARED`]
      : ['DEPTH',  `SEGMENT ${RUN.deepest}/20` + (won?'  \u2014 CLEARED':'')],
    ['BOSSES',   `${RUN.bosses} FELLED`],
    ['ROOMS',    `${RUN.rooms}`],
    ['KILLS',    `${RUN.kills}` + (RUN.elites?`  (${RUN.elites} ELITE)`:'')],
    ['DAYS',     `${day}`],
  ];
  const right=[
    ['RELICS',   `${inv.length} CARRIED`],
    ['BANKED',   `${crops} CROP \u00b7 ${shards} SHARD`],
    ['BLOOD',    `${(RUN.hits/2).toFixed(1)} HEARTS LOST`],
    ['POTIONS',  `${RUN.potions} DRUNK`],
    ['CURSES',   curses.size ? [...curses].map(c=>c.replace('GREEDY','MISER')).join(', ') : 'NONE'],
  ];
  const row=(pairs,x)=>pairs.forEach(([k,v],i)=>{
    txt(k, x, Y+i*LH, '#5a4a5e', 7);
    txt(v, x+58, Y+i*LH, C.bone, 8);
  });
  row(left,L); row(right,R);

  /* --- THE RETURN: say plainly that it happened --- */
  g.textAlign='center';
  if(RUN.returns){
    txtC(`\u2020 YOU CRAWLED BACK ${RUN.returns} TIME${RUN.returns===1?'':'S'}. IT COST YOU THE CLOCK.`,
         VIEW_W/2, Y+5*LH+4, '#c0304a', 7);
  } else if(!won && modeIs('HARDCORE') && META.unlockedReturn){
    txtC('ONE LIFE. YOU CHOSE THIS.', VIEW_W/2, Y+5*LH+4, '#c0304a', 7);
  } else if(!won && !META.unlockedReturn){
    txtC('FELL A BOSS AND HELL WILL LEARN YOUR NAME.', VIEW_W/2, Y+5*LH+4, C.dim, 7);
  }
  g.textAlign='left';

  /* --- the build that got you there --- */
  const shown=inv.slice(0,7).map(it=>itemName(it));
  if(shown.length){
    txt('THE BUILD', L, Y+5*LH+16, '#5a4a5e', 7);
    let x=L, y=Y+5*LH+26;
    shown.forEach(n=>{
      const w=n.length*4.4+10;
      if(x+w > VIEW_W-L){ x=L; y+=10; }
      px(x, y-1, w, 9, '#181220');
      txt(n, x+4, y, C.crop, 7);
      x+=w+4;
    });
    if(inv.length>7) txt(`+${inv.length-7} more`, x+2, y, C.dim, 7);
  }
}

function drawFoe(f){
  const c=f.hit?C.bone:(f.stun>0?C.ice:f.dotT>0?'#9fd35a':C[f.type]);
  const x=Math.round(f.x), y=Math.round(f.y);

  if(f.elite){
    /* Elites must read as DANGEROUS in one glance: bigger body, affix-coloured
       aura, a crown, and a health bar. You should never eat a full heart from
       something you mistook for a grub. */
    const ac=AFFIX[f.affix].c;
    g.globalAlpha=.22+.10*Math.sin(now/240);
    cir(x,y,f.r+7,ac); g.globalAlpha=1;
    g.strokeStyle=ac; g.lineWidth=1;
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(x,y,f.r+4,0,7); g.stroke();

    const s2=1.5;                                   // the body, scaled up
    if(f.type==='grub'){ cir(x,y,8*s2,c); px(x-5,y-5,3,3,C.blood); px(x+3,y-5,3,3,C.blood); }
    else if(f.type==='imp'){ px(x-10,y-12,21,21,c);
      px(x-13,y-17,4,7,C.bone); px(x+9,y-17,4,7,C.bone);
      px(x-6,y-6,4,4,C.sulfur); px(x+3,y-6,4,4,C.sulfur); }
    else { const tell=f.dashT>0||f.cool<300;
      px(x-12,y-13,24,25,tell?C.bone:c);
      px(x-7,y-7,4,4,C.blood); px(x+4,y-7,4,4,C.blood); }

    /* crown */
    for(let i=0;i<3;i++) px(x-7+i*6,y-f.r-10+(i===1?-2:0),3,5,ac);
    px(x-8,y-f.r-5,17,2,ac);

    /* health bar + affix tag */
    const w=30;
    px(x-w/2-1,y-f.r-19,w+2,4,'#000');
    px(x-w/2,y-f.r-18,Math.max(0,f.hp/f.maxHp)*w,2,ac);
    if(f.armor>0 && !f.shredded) px(x+w/2+2,y-f.r-19,3,4,'#8aa0c8');
    txt(f.affix,x-f.affix.length*2.4,y-f.r-29,ac,7);
    return;
  }

  if(f.type==='grub'){ cir(x,y,8,c); px(x-3,y-3,2,2,C.blood); px(x+2,y-3,2,2,C.blood); }
  else if(f.type==='imp'){ px(x-7,y-8,14,14,c);
    px(x-9,y-12,3,5,C.bone); px(x+6,y-12,3,5,C.bone);
    px(x-4,y-4,3,3,C.sulfur); px(x+2,y-4,3,3,C.sulfur); }
  else { const tell=f.dashT>0||f.cool<300;
    px(x-8,y-9,16,17,tell?C.bone:c);
    px(x-5,y-5,3,3,C.blood); px(x+3,y-5,3,3,C.blood); }
}
