/* ---- BOSS SPRITE RUNTIME (decodes the embedded art above into <img>s) ---- */
const BOSS_IMG={};
(function(){
  for(const name in BOSS_ART){
    BOSS_IMG[name]={};
    const ph=BOSS_ART[name];
    for(const k in ph){
      const im=new Image();
      im.__id=name+':'+k; im.__ready=false;
      im.onload=()=>{ im.__ready=true; };
      im.src=ph[k];
      BOSS_IMG[name][+k]=im;
    }
  }
})();
/* current boss+phase sprite, or null if none supplied / still decoding */
function bossArt(b){
  const set=BOSS_IMG[b.def.name]; if(!set) return null;
  const im=set[b.phase];
  return (im && im.__ready) ? im : null;
}
/* cached solid-colour silhouette of a sprite, for hit-flash / shield tints */
const _tintCache=new Map();
function tintedSprite(img,tint){
  const key=img.__id+'|'+tint;
  let cv=_tintCache.get(key);
  if(!cv){
    cv=document.createElement('canvas');
    cv.width=img.naturalWidth||img.width; cv.height=img.naturalHeight||img.height;
    const tg=cv.getContext('2d'); tg.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
    tg.drawImage(img,0,0);
    tg.globalCompositeOperation='source-in';
    tg.fillStyle=tint; tg.fillRect(0,0,cv.width,cv.height);
    _tintCache.set(key,cv);
  }
  return cv;
}
/* blit a boss sprite into arena space, nearest-neighbour, with optional tint */
function drawBossSprite(img,x,y,w,h,tint){
  g.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  g.drawImage(img, x|0, y|0, w, h);
  if(tint){
    const prev=g.globalAlpha;
    g.globalAlpha=prev*(tint===C.bone?0.85:0.5);
    g.drawImage(tintedSprite(img,tint), x|0, y|0, w, h);
    g.globalAlpha=prev;
  }
}

function drawBoss(){
  const b=boss;
  if(b.hidden) return;
  let alpha = b.invis>0 ? .12 : b.ghost ? .45 : 1;
  g.globalAlpha=alpha;
  const x=b.x-b.w/2, y=b.y-b.h/2;
  px(x+6,y+b.h-4,b.w-12,3,'rgba(0,0,0,.4)');            // grounding shadow (kept)
  const art=bossArt(b);
  if(art){                                              // hand-drawn sprite
    drawBossSprite(art, x, y, b.w, b.h, b.hit?C.bone:(b.shield?C.toxic:null));
  } else {                                              // fallback block-art
    const c=b.hit?C.bone:(b.shield?'#3a4a2a':b.col);
    px(x+4,y+8,b.w-8,b.h-14,c);
    px(x+2,y+2,8,14,C.bone); px(x+b.w-10,y+2,8,14,C.bone);
    const ec=b.shield?C.toxic:C.sulfur;
    px(x+b.w*.28,y+b.h*.42,7,6,ec); px(x+b.w*.58,y+b.h*.42,7,6,ec);
  }
  g.globalAlpha=1;

  if(b.def.name==='CERBERUS'&&b.phase<2){
    px(x-6,y+10,16,16, b.s.head===0?C.bone:'#5a1a2a');
    px(x+b.w-10,y+10,16,16, b.s.head===1?C.bone:'#5a1a2a');
  }
  if(b.s.sis) b.s.sis.forEach(s=>{ px(s.x-10,s.y-10,20,20,'#9a2a4a');
    px(s.x-14,s.y-6,5,10,C.bone); px(s.x+9,s.y-6,5,10,C.bone); });
  if(b.s.o){ px(b.s.o.x-10,b.s.o.y-14,20,26,'#6a3a8a');
    px(b.s.o.x-6,b.s.o.y-6,4,4,C.sulfur); px(b.s.o.x+2,b.s.o.y-6,4,4,C.sulfur); }
  if(b.s.decoys) b.s.decoys.forEach(d=>{ g.globalAlpha=.35;
    px(d.x-12,d.y-16,24,32,'#8a8a7a'); g.globalAlpha=1; });
  if(b.s.masks) b.s.masks.forEach((m,i)=>{
    const mx=VIEW_W/2+Math.cos(m)*150, my=VIEW_H/2+Math.sin(m)*85;
    px(mx-9,my-9,18,18,[C.blood,'#c88ae8',C.bone][i]);
    px(mx-5,my-3,3,3,'#000'); px(mx+2,my-3,3,3,'#000'); });
  if(b.s.ax!==undefined) px(b.s.ax-3,b.s.ay-3,6,6,C.rockLip);

  /* boss bar */
  const w=280, bx=(VIEW_W-w)/2;
  px(bx-1,VIEW_H-22,w+2,9,'#000');
  px(bx,VIEW_H-21,(b.hp/b.maxHp)*w,7, b.shield?'#4a5a2a':C.blood);
  txt(`${seg} · ${b.name}`,bx,VIEW_H-33,C.bone,9);
  txt(b.phName,bx+w-b.phName.length*5.4,VIEW_H-33,C.sulfur,9);
  if(b.shield) txt('SHIELDED — KILL THE FLIES',bx+62,VIEW_H-45,C.toxic,8);
  if(b.vuln>1) txt('STUNNED',bx+118,VIEW_H-45,C.sulfur,8);
  if(b.s.gaze>0) txt('!! LOOK AWAY !!',VIEW_W/2-42,44,C.toxic,10);
}

function drawCursor(){
  const x=Math.round(P.aimX), y=Math.round(P.aimY);
  const c = mode===S.FARM ? C.crop : C.sulfur;
  px(x-5,y,4,1,c); px(x+2,y,4,1,c);
  px(x,y-5,1,4,c); px(x,y+2,1,4,c);
  px(x,y,1,1,c);
}

/* Items on the floor: a tier-coloured SQUARE for now — swap the art later. */
function drawGroundItems(){
  for(const gi of ground){
    const c=TIER_COL[gi.it.t];
    const bob=Math.sin(gi.t/300)*1.5;
    const x=Math.round(gi.x), y=Math.round(gi.y+bob);
    /* glow pad */
    g.globalAlpha=.20+.10*Math.sin(gi.t/240);
    cir(x,y+6,13,c); g.globalAlpha=1;
    /* the square */
    px(x-7,y-7,14,14,'#0d0910');
    px(x-6,y-6,12,12,c);
    px(x-6,y-6,12,3,'#ffffff28');
    /* tier pips */
    for(let i=0;i<gi.it.t;i++) px(x-6+i*3,y+8,2,2,c);
  }
}

/* Walk over an item and its card appears. */
function drawItemCard(gi){
  const it=gi.it, name=itemName(it);
  const W=232, lines=wrap(it.d,34);
  const H=34+lines.length*11;
  let X=clamp(gi.x-W/2, 6, VIEW_W-W-6);
  let Y=gi.y-H-18; if(Y<6) Y=gi.y+22;
  px(X-1,Y-1,W+2,H+2,TIER_COL[it.t]);
  px(X,Y,W,H,'#0d0910');
  txt(name,X+8,Y+6,TIER_COL[it.t],9);
  txt('★'.repeat(it.t)+'  '+TIER_NAME[it.t],X+8,Y+18,C.dim,8);
  lines.forEach((l,i)=>txt(l,X+8,Y+31+i*11,C.bone,8));
  txt('[E] TAKE',X+W-52,Y+6,C.sulfur,8);
}
function wrap(t,n){
  const w=t.split(' '), out=[]; let cur='';
  for(const word of w){
    if((cur+' '+word).trim().length>n){ out.push(cur.trim()); cur=word; }
    else cur+=' '+word;
  }
  if(cur.trim()) out.push(cur.trim());
  return out;
}

/* 15 slots. Right-click a slot to drop it. */
function drawInventory(){
  const SW=18, X0=VIEW_W-INV_SLOTS*SW-8, Y0=VIEW_H-24;
  for(let i=0;i<INV_SLOTS;i++){
    const X=X0+i*SW;
    px(X,Y0,SW-2,16,'#15101a');
    px(X,Y0,SW-2,1,'#2a2030');
    const it=inv[i];
    if(it){
      px(X+2,Y0+2,SW-6,12,TIER_COL[it.t]);
      px(X+2,Y0+2,SW-6,2,'#ffffff30');
    }
  }
  txt(`INV ${inv.length}/${INV_SLOTS}`,X0,Y0-11,C.dim,8);
  /* hovering a filled slot with the cursor shows its card */
  const i=invSlotAt(P.aimX,P.aimY);
  if(i>=0&&inv[i]){
    const it=inv[i], name=itemName(it);
    const lines=wrap(it.d,34), W=232, H=34+lines.length*11;
    const X=clamp(X0+i*SW-W/2,6,VIEW_W-W-6), Y=Y0-H-8;
    px(X-1,Y-1,W+2,H+2,TIER_COL[it.t]);
    px(X,Y,W,H,'#0d0910');
    txt(name,X+8,Y+6,TIER_COL[it.t],9);
    txt('★'.repeat(it.t)+'  '+TIER_NAME[it.t],X+8,Y+18,C.dim,8);
    lines.forEach((l,k)=>txt(l,X+8,Y+31+k*11,C.bone,8));
    txt('RMB DROP',X+W-56,Y+6,C.blood,8);
  }
}
function invSlotAt(mx,my){
  const SW=18, X0=VIEW_W-INV_SLOTS*SW-8, Y0=VIEW_H-24;
  if(my<Y0||my>Y0+16) return -1;
  const i=Math.floor((mx-X0)/SW);
  return (i>=0&&i<INV_SLOTS)?i:-1;
}

function drawHUD(){
  /* ======================================================================
     THE HUD BAND — 640x40, beneath the arena.
     It used to be painted straight onto the play area: hearts, shards, skill
     tree and tool all stacked down the left edge, over the floor you were
     fighting on. Two of the lines even overlapped each other (the room label
     and your family name were both at y=28).
     16:9 hands us 40 free pixels. This is what they are for.
     ====================================================================== */
  const Y0=VIEW_H;                      // 320 — the top of the band
  px(0,Y0,SCREEN_W,HUD_H,'#0d0910');
  px(0,Y0,SCREEN_W,1,'#2a2030');        // a hairline, so the arena has an edge

  /* ---------- IN THE ARENA: only what describes the ROOM ---------- */
  if(mode===S.HALL){
    txt(biome.n, VIEW_W/2-biome.n.length*2.7, 6, C.dim, 8);
    if(cond.id!=='NONE'){
      const w=cond.n.length*7+16;
      px(VIEW_W/2-w/2,17,w,13,'#0d0910');
      px(VIEW_W/2-w/2,17,w,1,cond.c);
      txt(cond.n,VIEW_W/2-cond.n.length*3.4,20,cond.c,9);
      txt(cond.d,VIEW_W/2-cond.d.length*2.1,33,C.dim,8);
    }
  }

  /* ---------- LEFT: your body. Health, dashes, potions. ---------- */
  const hearts=Math.ceil(stats.maxHp/2);
  for(let i=0;i<hearts;i++){
    const X=6+i*10, filled=clamp(P.hp-i*2,0,2);
    px(X,Y0+5,8,7,'#3a2028');
    if(filled>=1){ px(X,Y0+5,filled===2?8:4,7,C.blood); px(X+1,Y0+6,2,2,'#ff8a9a'); }
  }
  if(shieldUp) px(6+hearts*10+2, Y0+5, 6, 6, '#9adcff');

  const mx2=dashMax();
  for(let i=0;i<mx2;i++){
    const X=6+i*16, Y=Y0+15;
    px(X,Y,13,4,'#2a2030');
    if(i<P.dashN) px(X,Y,13,4,C.purple);
    else{
      const q=P.dashQ.slice().sort((a,b)=>a-b)[i-P.dashN];
      if(q!==undefined) px(X,Y,Math.round(13*(1-q/dashRecharge())),4,'#4a3a6a');
    }
  }
  for(let i=0;i<POTION_MAX;i++){
    const X=6+mx2*16+8+i*11, Y=Y0+13;
    if(i<potions){ px(X+2,Y,5,2,'#c8b8a0'); px(X+1,Y+2,7,7,'#2a5a2a'); px(X+2,Y+5,5,3,'#6adc6a'); }
    else         { px(X+1,Y+2,7,7,'#1a1a1a'); px(X+2,Y+3,5,5,'#0e0c10'); }
  }
  if(potions>0) txt('Q', 6+mx2*16+8+POTION_MAX*11+2, Y0+15, C.dim, 7);

  txt(`CROP ${crops}`,   6,  Y0+26, C.crop, 8);
  txt(`SEED ${seedBank}`, 62, Y0+26, '#7a9a5a', 8);
  txt(`\u25c6 ${shards}`, 122, Y0+26, C.gold, 8);

  /* ---------- MIDDLE: who you are, and where. ---------- */
  const MID=178;
  const label = mode===S.FARM ? `FARM \u00b7 DAY ${day}`
    : mode===S.BOSS ? `BOSS GATE ${seg}/20`
    : `HALL ${globalRoom()}/${TOTAL_ROOMS} \u00b7 ${roomType}`;
  txt(label, MID, Y0+5, C.sulfur, 8);
  if(mode===S.HALL && roomKind!=='COMBAT'){
    const RK=DOORS.find(d=>d.id===roomKind);
    if(RK) txt(RK.n, MID+label.length*5+8, Y0+5, RK.c, 7);
  }

  txt(hero.fam, MID, Y0+16, hero.col, 8);
  txt(`${tree} \u00b7 SP ${pts}`, MID+hero.fam.length*5+8, Y0+16, TREES[tree].c, 8);
  if(revives>0) txt(`\u2665${revives}`, MID+hero.fam.length*5+8+58, Y0+16, '#9adcff', 7);

  if(heroIs('GORE')){
    const bw=52;
    px(MID, Y0+28, bw, 5, '#1a1016');
    if(rageT>0){ px(MID,Y0+28,bw,5,'#ff7040');
      txt('RAGE '+(rageT/1000).toFixed(1)+'s', MID+bw+4, Y0+27, '#ff9060', 7); }
    else px(MID, Y0+28, Math.round(bw*rage/RAGE_MAX), 5, '#d03a2a');
  } else if(mode===S.FARM){
    txt(`${toolName(P.tool)} (1-4)`, MID, Y0+27, P.tool===4?hero.col:C.bone, 7);
    txt(hero.crop, MID+80, Y0+27, C.crop, 7);
  } else if(curses.size){
    /* your curses, still always visible — you chose them, you live with them */
    let cx2=MID;
    for(const id of curses){
      const c=CURSES.find(x=>x.id===id);
      const n='\u2020'+c.n.replace('CURSE OF ','').slice(0,7);
      txt(n, cx2, Y0+27, '#c0304a', 7);
      cx2 += n.length*4.6+6;
      if(cx2>420) break;
    }
  }

  /* ---------- RIGHT: the run. Clock, depth, rules. ---------- */
  const rt=(t,y,c,sz=8)=> txt(t, SCREEN_W-8-t.length*(sz*0.56), y, c, sz);

  if(RUN.ms>0) rt(fmtTime(RUN.ms), Y0+4, RUN.running?C.bone:C.gold, 10);
  rt(`SEG ${seg}/20`, Y0+17, C.dim, 8);

  if(modeIs('GAUNTLET') && gaunt)
    rt(`HOUSE ${gaunt.idx+1}/${gaunt.order.length} \u00b7 NO DEATHS`, Y0+28, modeDef().c, 7);
  else if(modeIs('HARDCORE'))
    rt('\u2020 ONE LIFE', Y0+28, '#c0304a', 7);
  else if(modeIs('LONGWAY'))
    rt(RUN.returns ? `\u2020 DIED ${RUN.returns}\u00d7` : '\u2020 THE LONG WAY', Y0+28, '#6ae0a8', 7);

  /* now playing — small, and out of the way */
  const nm=Music.name;
  txt((Music.on?'\u266a ':'\u2715 ')+nm, 430, Y0+5, Music.on?'#4a3f4a':'#3a2c38', 7);
  txt(`${scale}x`, 430, Y0+16, '#3a3040', 7);

  /* ---------- transient, over the arena ---------- */
  if(msgT>0){ g.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,msgT/500);
    px(0,VIEW_H-16,VIEW_W,12,C.panel); txt(msg,10,VIEW_H-14,C.sulfur); g.globalAlpha=1; }

  if(saveFlash>0){ saveFlash-=FMS;
    g.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,saveFlash/400);
    txt('SAVED', 430, Y0+27, C.crop, 8);       // in the band, not floating over the floor
    g.globalAlpha=1; }

  if(DIAG){
    const L=[
      'FPS '+fps+'   mode '+(Object.keys(S).find(k=>S[k]===mode)||mode),
      'screen '+SCREEN_W+'x'+SCREEN_H+' @'+scale+'x   arena '+VIEW_W+'x'+VIEW_H,
      'room '+globalRoom()+'/'+TOTAL_ROOMS+'  seg '+seg+'  day '+day,
      'cond '+(cond?cond.id:'-')+'   biome '+(biome?biome.n:'-'),
      'enemy set '+(eset?eset.n:'-')+'   foes '+foes.length+'  elites '+eliteCount,
      'player '+P.x.toFixed(0)+','+P.y.toFixed(0)+'   tile '+Math.floor(P.x/TILE)+','+Math.floor(P.y/TILE),
      'in wall: '+(hitsWall(P.x,P.y,P.r)?'YES':'no')+'   boxed in: '+(boxedIn()?'YES':'no'),
      'door '+(doorOpen?'OPEN':'shut')+'   tether '+(tether?'ON':'off')+'   unstuck x'+unstuckCount,
      'music '+Music.source+' — '+Music.name,
      'storage '+Store.kind+'   seed '+runSeed+'   mode '+MODE,
      lastErr? ('LAST ERROR: '+lastErr) : 'no errors',
    ];
    px(4,44,258,10+L.length*10,'rgba(6,4,8,.86)');
    px(4,44,2,10+L.length*10,C.sulfur);
    L.forEach((l,i)=>txt(l,10,50+i*10, i===10&&lastErr?C.blood:C.dim,8));
  }
}

const SKILL_CLOSE={X:VIEW_W-92, Y:12, W:76, H:20};

function drawSkill(){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,C.panel);
  const T=TREES[tree];
  txt(`DEITY OF ${tree} — THE SKILL WEB`,20,16,T.c,12);
  txt(`${pts} POINTS UNSPENT · RULE OF 20 · CLICK A NODE`,20,34,C.dim,8);
  /* a real, clickable close button — not just "press K" */
  const hov = P.aimX>=SKILL_CLOSE.X && P.aimX<SKILL_CLOSE.X+SKILL_CLOSE.W &&
              P.aimY>=SKILL_CLOSE.Y && P.aimY<SKILL_CLOSE.Y+SKILL_CLOSE.H;
  px(SKILL_CLOSE.X,SKILL_CLOSE.Y,SKILL_CLOSE.W,SKILL_CLOSE.H, hov?'#3a2430':'#1a1420');
  px(SKILL_CLOSE.X,SKILL_CLOSE.Y,2,SKILL_CLOSE.H, hov?C.sulfur:'#3a2c38');
  txt('CLOSE',SKILL_CLOSE.X+22,SKILL_CLOSE.Y+6, hov?C.bone:C.dim,9);
  ['SLOT 1','SLOT 2','SLOT 3'].forEach((s,i)=>txt(s,30+i*196,46,C.dim,8));
  for(let r=0;r<5;r++)for(let c=0;c<3;c++){
    const [name]=T.rows[r][c];
    const X=30+c*196, Y=56+r*48, own=spent[r][c];
    const locked = r>0 && !(spent[r-1][0]||spent[r-1][1]||spent[r-1][2]);
    px(X,Y,176,36, own?T.c:locked?'#1a141a':'#241c26');
    px(X+1,Y+1,174,34, own?'#1a1016':'#15101a');
    txt(name,X+10,Y+8, own?T.c:locked?'#3a2c38':C.bone,9);
    txt(own?'TAKEN':locked?'LOCKED':'1 SP',X+10,Y+20, own?T.c:C.dim,8);
  }
}

/* ============================ CINEMATIC ART ============================= */

/* PHASE 1 — THE BETRAYAL
   Seven divine constellations drag a crowned dark god down into the abyss. */
function cinePhase1(t){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'#07060b');
  /* starfield */
  for(let i=0;i<90;i++){
    const x=(i*137)%VIEW_W, y=(i*73)%(VIEW_H-70);
    const tw=.3+.7*Math.abs(Math.sin(t/700+i));
    g.globalAlpha=tw*.6; px(x,y,1,1,'#cdd6f0'); g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  /* the abyss opens from below and swallows him */
  const open=clamp((t-1200)/6000,0,1);
  const ay=VIEW_H-20-open*74;
  for(let y=ay;y<VIEW_H;y++){
    const k=(y-ay)/(VIEW_H-ay);
    px(0,y,VIEW_W,1, k>.66?'#ff8a2f' : k>.33?'#c8321e' : '#5a1414');
  }
  for(let i=0;i<26;i++){
    const x=(i*53+Math.sin(t/400+i)*12)%VIEW_W;
    const y=ay-((t/9+i*30)%60);
    if(y<ay&&y>ay-56){ g.globalAlpha=.5; px(x,y,2,2,i%2?C.ember:C.sulfur); g.globalAlpha=1; }
  }

  /* HADES — crowned, dragged down as the abyss climbs */
  const sink=open*66;
  const hx=VIEW_W/2, hy=150+sink;
  const dim=clamp(1-open*.75,.25,1);
  g.globalAlpha=dim;
  px(hx-26,hy-26,52,74,'#120d1a');                 // body
  px(hx-20,hy-44,40,22,'#120d1a');                 // head
  px(hx-14,hy-36,8,6,C.ember); px(hx+6,hy-36,8,6,C.ember);   // eyes
  g.globalAlpha=1;
  /* the crown: rises, then is torn away */
  const steal=clamp((t-3600)/2600,0,1);
  const cy=hy-52-steal*82, calp=1-steal;
  g.globalAlpha=calp;
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++) px(hx-20+i*9,cy-(i===2?6:0),6,10,C.sulfur);
  px(hx-22,cy+8,46,4,C.sulfur);
  g.globalAlpha=1;

  /* the seven — constellations, each anchored by a chain */
  for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
    const a=-Math.PI + (i+.5)/7*Math.PI;
    const gx=VIEW_W/2+Math.cos(a)*216, gy=104+Math.sin(a)*62;
    const app=clamp((t-300-i*260)/900,0,1);
    if(app<=0) continue;
    g.globalAlpha=app;
    /* constellation body */
    const pts=[[0,-9],[-6,-2],[6,-2],[-4,7],[4,7]];
    g.strokeStyle='#8fa8dc'; g.lineWidth=1;
    g.beginPath();
    pts.forEach((q,k)=>{ const X=gx+q[0],Y=gy+q[1];
      if(k===0)g.moveTo(X,Y); else g.lineTo(X,Y); });
    g.closePath(); g.stroke();
    pts.forEach(q=>px(gx+q[0]-1,gy+q[1]-1,2,2,'#dfe8ff'));
    /* the chain, drawn link by link */
    const grab=clamp((t-1800-i*140)/1400,0,1);
    if(grab>0){
      const tx=hx+Math.cos(a)*22, ty=hy+Math.sin(a)*10;
      const links=Math.floor(grab*13);
      for(let L=0;L<links;L++){
        const u=L/13;
        const X=gx+(tx-gx)*u, Y=gy+(ty-gy)*u;
        px(X-1,Y-1,3,2, L%2?'#7a6a5a':'#a8967e');
      }
    }
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
}

/* PHASE 2 — THE PROPHECY
   A crumbling obsidian tablet. The camera pans. The inscription burns gold. */
function cinePhase2(t){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'#0a0810');
  const pan=Math.min(t/12000,1)*90;                // slow rightward pan
  g.save(); g.translate(-pan,0);

  px(-20,26,VIEW_W+130,232,'#191320');             // the slab
  px(-20,26,VIEW_W+130,4,'#2e2438');
  px(-20,254,VIEW_W+130,4,'#0a0810');
  for(let i=0;i<16;i++){                            // cracks
    const x=30+i*46, y=40+((i*37)%180);
    px(x,y,1,26+(i%3)*12,'#0d0a12');
    px(x+1,y+12,10+(i%4)*6,1,'#0d0a12');
  }

  const glow=.55+.45*Math.sin(t/420);
  /* glyph rows, revealing left to right */
  const rows=[[46,54],[46,72],[46,90],[46,222],[46,240]];
  rows.forEach(([x,y],r)=>{
    const rev=clamp((t-500-r*380)/1500,0,1);
    for(let i=0;i<28;i++){
      if(i/28>rev) break;
      g.globalAlpha=glow*(.5+.5*Math.sin(t/300+i));
      px(x+i*24, y, 3+((i+r)%3)*4, 3, C.sulfur);
      if((i+r)%4===0) px(x+i*24, y+4, 3, 4, C.sulfur);
      g.globalAlpha=1;
    }
  });

  /* the relief: one figure astride a burning underworld and a shattered Olympus */
  const rel=clamp((t-3000)/3000,0,1);
  g.globalAlpha=rel;
  const cx=VIEW_W/2+40, base=196;
  /* burning underworld (left) */
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++) px(cx-104+i*10, base-8-i*3, 8, 30+i*3, '#3a1a1a');
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++) px(cx-104+i*10, base-10-i*3, 8, 3, C.ember);
  /* shattered Olympus (right) */
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++) px(cx+16+i*10, base-32+i*3, 8, 54-i*3, '#2a2438');
  px(cx+30,base-40,6,26,'#4a4360'); px(cx+58,base-34,6,20,'#4a4360');
  px(cx+44,base-46,30,3,'#4a4360');
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++) px(cx+22+i*13, base-52-i*4, 4, 4, '#2a2438');   // rubble
  /* the figure */
  px(cx-8, base-84, 16, 44, '#0d0a12');
  px(cx-12, base-98, 24, 16, '#0d0a12');
  px(cx-16, base-104, 6, 10, C.sulfur); px(cx+10, base-104, 6, 10, C.sulfur);  // horns/crown
  g.globalAlpha=rel*glow;
  px(cx-9, base-92, 5, 4, C.ember); px(cx+4, base-92, 5, 4, C.ember);
  g.globalAlpha=1;
  g.restore();
}

/* PHASE 3 — THE REALITY
   Cut. No music sting. Just dirt, a pitchfork, and things in the dark. */
function cinePhase3(t){
  /* hard cut to a sour, sulfurous dusk */
  for(let y=0;y<190;y++){
    const k=y/190;
    px(0,y,VIEW_W,1, k>.72?'#5a3a2a' : k>.4?'#3e2a26' : '#241a22');
  }
  /* distant prowlers on the ridge — the whole point of the fortified plot */
  const ridge=186;
  px(0,ridge-6,VIEW_W,8,'#1a1218');
  for(let i=0;i<5;i++){
    const x=(40+i*128 + Math.sin(t/2200+i*2)*26)%(VIEW_W+60)-30;
    const bob=Math.sin(t/380+i)*1.5;
    g.globalAlpha=.75;
    px(x,ridge-16+bob,11,12,'#120d12');
    px(x-2,ridge-21+bob,3,5,'#120d12'); px(x+10,ridge-21+bob,3,5,'#120d12');
    px(x+2,ridge-13+bob,2,2,C.blood);  px(x+7,ridge-13+bob,2,2,C.blood);
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  /* the ash field */
  px(0,ridge,VIEW_W,VIEW_H-ridge,C.ash);
  px(0,ridge,VIEW_W,3,C.ashTop);
  for(let i=0;i<70;i++){
    const x=(i*97)%VIEW_W, y=ridge+8+((i*41)%120);
    px(x,y,2,1,'#5b4842');
  }
  /* the fortified plot: fence, then rows of mutated crops */
  for(let i=0;i<VIEW_W;i+=22) px(i,ridge+8,3,14,'#3a2c26');
  px(0,ridge+10,VIEW_W,2,'#3a2c26');
  for(let r=0;r<3;r++) for(let i=0;i<12;i++){
    const x=44+i*46, y=ridge+34+r*30;
    px(x-10,y,22,10,C.soil);
    px(x,y-8,2,9,C.stem);
    const pulse=Math.sin(t/300+i+r)>0?C.sulfur:C.ember;
    px(x-3,y-12,7,5,C.ember); px(x-2,y-11,4,3,pulse);
  }
  /* THE PITCHFORK, driven into the dirt — the hero shot */
  const drive=clamp((t-400)/700,0,1);
  const fx2=140, fy2=ridge+44 - (1-drive)*30;
  px(fx2-1, fy2-58, 3, 60, '#6b4a32');                 // haft
  px(fx2-9, fy2-64, 19, 4, '#7a6a5a');                 // crossbar
  px(fx2-8, fy2-74, 3, 12, '#8a7a6a');                 // tines
  px(fx2-1, fy2-76, 3, 14, '#8a7a6a');
  px(fx2+6, fy2-74, 3, 12, '#8a7a6a');
  for(let i=0;i<9;i++) px(fx2-6+((i*7)%16), fy2-56+((i*11)%50), 2, 2, '#4a3830');  // rust
  if(drive>=1 && t<1400){ g.globalAlpha=(1400-t)/500;
    for(let i=0;i<8;i++) px(fx2-12+i*3, fy2+2+((i*5)%6), 2, 1, C.ashTop);
    g.globalAlpha=1; }
  /* the skeletal hand still on the haft */
  px(fx2-6, fy2-44, 5, 3, '#cfc4ae'); px(fx2+2, fy2-44, 5, 3, '#cfc4ae');
  px(fx2-7, fy2-41, 15, 4, '#cfc4ae');

  /* the farmer, wiping ash from their brow */
  const px0=196, py0=ridge+46;
  const wipe = t>1600 && t<3400;
  px(px0-8, py0-2, 16, 4, 'rgba(0,0,0,.4)');
  px(px0-6, py0-20, 12, 19, C.cloak);
  px(px0-6, py0-20, 12, 3, C.cloakLit);
  px(px0-5, py0-32, 10, 12, C.skin);
  px(px0-8, py0-38, 4, 7, C.bone); px(px0+4, py0-38, 4, 7, C.bone);
  px(px0-6, py0-35, 12, 3, C.cloakLit);
  px(px0-4, py0-28, 2, 2, C.ember); px(px0+2, py0-28, 2, 2, C.ember);
  if(wipe){ px(px0-9, py0-33, 8, 3, C.skin); px(px0-10, py0-30, 3, 5, C.skin); }  // forearm
  else    { px(px0+5, py0-19, 3, 9, C.skin); }

  /* falling ash, always */
  for(let i=0;i<44;i++){
    const x=(i*67 + Math.sin(t/1000+i)*8)%VIEW_W;
    const y=((i*53) + t*0.028)%VIEW_H;
    g.globalAlpha=.30; px(x,y,1,2,'#8a7a76'); g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
}

function drawCinematic(){
  const beat=CINE[cineI];
  if(!beat){ mode=S.TITLE; return; }
  if(beat.p===1) cinePhase1(cinePT);
  else if(beat.p===2) cinePhase2(cinePT);
  else cinePhase3(cinePT);

  /* letterbox */
  px(0,0,VIEW_W,18,'#000');
  px(0,VIEW_H-56,VIEW_W,56,'#000');

  /* narrator: typed out, one character at a time */
  const chars=Math.floor(cineT/17);
  const shown=beat.t.slice(0,chars);
  const done=chars>=beat.t.length;
  const lines=wrap(shown,74);
  g.font='10px ui-monospace,monospace'; g.textBaseline='top';
  lines.slice(-3).forEach((l,i)=>{
    g.fillStyle=C.bone; g.fillText(l, 22, VIEW_H-46+i*13);
  });
  if(done && Math.floor(now/450)%2){
    g.fillStyle=C.ember;
    g.fillText('>', VIEW_W-24, VIEW_H-20);
  }
  g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('CLICK to advance', 22, 5);
  /* a real SKIP button you can click */
  const hov = P.aimX>VIEW_W-70 && P.aimY<18;
  px(VIEW_W-70,0,70,18, hov?'#2a1620':'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
  g.fillStyle=hov?C.sulfur:C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('SKIP  >>', VIEW_W-56, 5);
  const ph=['','THE BETRAYAL','THE PROPHECY','THE REALITY'][beat.p];
  g.textAlign='right'; g.fillStyle=C.sulfur;
  g.fillText(ph, VIEW_W-22, 5); g.textAlign='left';
}

/* The frozen world stays visible behind the menu — you can see what you're
   going back to, and where the boss's bullets are. */
function drawPause(){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'rgba(8,6,10,.72)');

  g.textAlign='center'; g.textBaseline='top';
  g.fillStyle=C.ember; g.font='16px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('PAUSED',VIEW_W/2,58);

  const where = pausedFrom===S.BOSS ? `BOSS GATE ${seg}/20 — ${boss?boss.name:''}`
              : pausedFrom===S.HALL ? `HALL ${globalRoom()}/${TOTAL_ROOMS}`
              : `THE FARM — DAY ${day}`;
  g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText(where,VIEW_W/2,80);
  g.fillText(`SEG ${seg}/20 · ${crops} CROP · ${shards} SHARDS · ${inv.length}/15 ITEMS`,
             VIEW_W/2,92);

  drawMenu(PAUSE_MENU);

  g.textAlign='center';
  g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('CLICK to pick   ·   ESC to resume', VIEW_W/2, VIEW_H-24);
  const st = Store.kind==='memory'
    ? 'NO STORAGE HERE — SAVING WILL NOT PERSIST. Download the file and run it locally.'
    : 'autosaves every room · storage: '+Store.kind;
  g.fillStyle = Store.kind==='memory'?C.blood:'#463c46';
  g.fillText(st, VIEW_W/2, VIEW_H-13);
  g.textAlign='left';
}

function drawTitle(){
  px(0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,'#0c090e');
  for(let i=0;i<40;i++){                                  // drifting embers
    const x=(i*79+now*.02)%VIEW_W, y=(i*47+Math.sin(now/900+i)*30+VIEW_H)%VIEW_H;
    g.globalAlpha=.15+.15*Math.sin(now/500+i);
    px(x,y,2,2,i%3?C.ember:C.sulfur); g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  g.textAlign='center'; g.textBaseline='top';
  g.fillStyle=C.ember; g.font='26px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('HELL FARMERS',VIEW_W/2,58);
  g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='9px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('100 rooms · 20 bosses · 195 relics',VIEW_W/2,88);
  g.fillStyle=C.blood; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('TRUST NO ONE',VIEW_W/2,104);

  drawMenu(titleMenu());

  g.textAlign='center';
  g.fillStyle=C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText('M mute   ·   -/= volume   ·   P next track', VIEW_W/2, VIEW_H-40);
  g.fillStyle=Store.kind==='memory'?C.blood:C.dim; g.font='8px ui-monospace,monospace';
  g.fillText(
    Store.kind==='memory'
      ? 'NO STORAGE IN THIS ENVIRONMENT — PROGRESS WILL NOT PERSIST'
      : 'autosaves every room · storage: '+Store.kind,
    VIEW_W/2, VIEW_H-26);
  g.textAlign='left';
}

/* Blit the back buffer to the glass. Five of the early-return screens each had
   their own copy of this, drawCursor and all. */
function present(){
  sc.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;
  sc.drawImage(back,0,0,SCREEN_W,SCREEN_H,0,0,SCREEN_W*scale,SCREEN_H*scale);
}

function render(){
  if(mode===S.INTRO){
    drawCinematic();
    drawCursor();
    present();
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.CODEX){
    drawCodex();
    drawCursor();
    present();
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.TOWN){
    drawTown();
    drawCursor();
    present();
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.MODE){
    drawModeSelect();
    drawCursor();
    present();
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.HOUSE){
    drawRoster();
    drawCursor();
    present();
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.SELECT){
    drawSelect();
    drawCursor();
    present();
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.TITLE){
    drawTitle();
    drawCursor();
    present();
    return;
  }
  /* Paused: render the world exactly as it was, then lay the menu over it. */
  const paused = (mode===S.PAUSE);
  if(paused) mode=pausedFrom;
  const sx=shake>0?(Math.random()-.5)*(shake/40):0, sy=shake>0?(Math.random()-.5)*(shake/40):0;
  g.save(); g.translate(sx|0,sy|0);

  drawGround();
  if(mode===S.FARM){ drawPlots(); drawHouse(); drawPortal(); drawTownGate(); }
  else drawDoor();

  for(const b of blights){
    g.globalAlpha=clamp(1-b.t/b.dur,0,1)*.55;
    cir(b.x,b.y,b.r, b.kind==='fire'?C.fire : b.kind==='heal'?C.blood
                   : b.kind==='gold'?C.gold : '#4a6a2a');
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  for(const t of tels){
    const p=t.t/t.dur;
    g.globalAlpha=.25+.4*Math.abs(Math.sin(t.t/70));
    g.strokeStyle=t.col; g.lineWidth=2;
    if(t.r>0){ g.beginPath(); g.arc(t.x,t.y,t.r*(.35+p*.65),0,7); g.stroke(); }
    else { g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(t.x,0); g.lineTo(t.x,VIEW_H);
           g.moveTo(0,t.y); g.lineTo(VIEW_W,t.y); g.stroke(); }
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  for(const bm of beams){
    const charging=bm.t<bm.tel;
    g.globalAlpha=charging?.3+.3*Math.abs(Math.sin(bm.t/60)):1;
    g.strokeStyle=bm.col; g.lineWidth=charging?1:bm.w;
    g.beginPath(); g.moveTo(bm.x,bm.y);
    g.lineTo(bm.x+Math.cos(bm.a)*bm.len, bm.y+Math.sin(bm.a)*bm.len);
    g.stroke(); g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  for(const d of drops){
    const yy=d.y+Math.sin(d.t/200)*2;
    if(d.kind==='seed') cir(d.x,yy,3,C.crop);
    else if(d.kind==='shard'){ px(d.x-3,yy-4,6,8,C.gold); px(d.x-2,yy-3,2,3,'#fff2a8'); }
    else if(d.kind==='potion'){                       // a little green flask
      const gl=.6+.4*Math.sin(now/220);
      g.globalAlpha=gl*.35; cir(d.x,yy-2,8,'#6adc6a'); g.globalAlpha=1;
      px(d.x-3,yy-7,6,3,'#c8b8a0');                   // cork
      px(d.x-4,yy-4,8,8,'#2a5a2a');                   // glass
      px(d.x-3,yy-1,6,4,'#6adc6a');                   // liquid
      px(d.x-2,yy-3,2,2,'#c8ffc8');                   // shine
    }
    else if(d.kind==='heart'){ px(d.x-4,yy-3,8,6,C.blood); px(d.x-3,yy-4,2,2,C.blood);
      px(d.x+1,yy-4,2,2,C.blood); px(d.x-3,yy-2,2,2,'#ff8a9a'); }
    else { px(d.x-9,yy-7,18,14,'#8a6a2a'); px(d.x-9,yy-2,18,3,C.sulfur);
      if(d.kind==='mimic'){ px(d.x-6,yy-4,3,3,C.blood); px(d.x+3,yy-4,3,3,C.blood); } }
  }
  for(const m of mobs){
    const c=m.hit?C.bone:
      m.type==='fly'?'#3a4a1a':m.type==='pup'?'#2a2a3a':m.type==='ghoul'?'#6a5a4a':
      m.type==='debris'?C.rockLip:m.type==='monolith'?'#4a3a5a':
      m.type==='preta'?'#8a8a6a':C.blood;
    if(m.type==='monolith'){ px(m.x-8,m.y-14,16,26,c); px(m.x-8,m.y-14,16,3,'#8a7ab8'); }
    else cir(m.x,m.y,m.r,c);
  }
  drawGroundItems();
  for(const f of foes) drawFoe(f);
  if(boss) drawBoss();
  drawPlayer();
  for(const w of wisps){                               // orbiting familiars
    cir(w.x,w.y,4,'#9adcff');
    cir(w.x,w.y,2,'#e8f8ff');
  }
  if(shieldUp){ g.globalAlpha=.35+.15*Math.sin(now/200);
    g.strokeStyle='#9adcff'; g.lineWidth=1;
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(P.x,P.y-14,16,0,7); g.stroke(); g.globalAlpha=1; }
  for(const s of shots) cir(s.x,s.y,s.r,s.col);
  for(const f of fx){
    g.globalAlpha=Math.min(1, f.life/(f.max||400));
    const sz=f.size||2;
    px(f.x,f.y,sz,sz,f.col);
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  /* shockwave rings — the POP */
  for(const r of rings){
    const k=1-(r.life/r.max);
    const rad=r.r0+(r.r1-r.r0)*(1-Math.pow(1-k,3));    // fast out, then ease
    g.globalAlpha=(1-k)*0.9;
    g.strokeStyle=r.col; g.lineWidth=r.w||2;
    g.beginPath(); g.arc(r.x,r.y,rad,0,6.283); g.stroke();
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }
  g.restore();

  if(arena&&arena.dark>0){
    /* SHADOW SWAP: a tiny personal glow, and a big flash when you fire.
       You navigate by muzzle light. */
    const rad = 34 + (flash>0 ? 170*(flash/140) : 0);
    const gr=g.createRadialGradient(P.x,P.y,rad*0.25,P.x,P.y,rad);
    gr.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    gr.addColorStop(1,`rgba(0,0,0,${arena.dark})`);
    g.fillStyle=gr; g.fillRect(0,0,VIEW_W,VIEW_H);
  }
  if(arena&&arena.storm){
    g.globalAlpha=.10*arena.storm;
    for(let i=0;i<70;i++){
      const x=(i*97+now*(.25*arena.storm))%VIEW_W;
      const y=(i*53+Math.sin(now/500+i)*20)%VIEW_H;
      px(x,y,6,1,C.sand);
    }
    g.globalAlpha=1;
  }

  drawHUD();
  drawInventory();
  if(hoverItem && mode!==S.SKILL) drawItemCard(hoverItem);
  if(!paused) drawCursor();
  if(mode===S.SKILL) drawSkill();
  if(mode===S.DEAD){
    drawSummary(false);          // it draws its own backdrop
    drawMenu(DEAD_MENU);
  }
  if(mode===S.WIN){
    drawSummary(true);
    drawMenu(WIN_MENU);
  }
  if(paused){ mode=S.PAUSE; drawPause(); }
  if(currentMenu()) drawCursor();

  present();
}

/* ---- INPUT ------------------------------------------------------------ */
const K=new Set();
const norm=e=>e.key.length===1?e.key.toLowerCase():e.key;
let prevMode=S.FARM;

addEventListener('blur',()=>{ K.clear(); P.firing=false; });
addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
  if(e.key===' '||e.key.startsWith('Arrow')) e.preventDefault();
  Music.unlock();                           // browsers require a gesture first
  const k=norm(e);
  /* music keys work everywhere INCLUDING the pause menu — except 'p', which
     the menu reuses, so it's handled inside the pause block below */
  if(k==='m'){ const on=Music.toggle(); say(on?'MUSIC ON':'MUSIC OFF'); }
  if(k==='-'||k==='_'){ say('VOLUME '+Math.round(Music.louder(-.1)*100)); }
  if(k==='='||k==='+'){ say('VOLUME '+Math.round(Music.louder(+.1)*100)); }
  if(k==='p' && mode!==S.PAUSE){ Music.next(); say('NEXT TRACK'); }
  if(mode===S.INTRO){
    if(k===' '||k==='Enter') cineNext();
    if(k==='Escape'||k==='s') cineSkip();
    K.add(k); return;
  }
  /* ---- THE COMPENDIUM ---- */
  if(mode===S.CODEX){
    const list=codexList();
    if(k==='w'||k==='ArrowUp')    codexSel=Math.max(0, codexSel-1);
    if(k==='s'||k==='ArrowDown')  codexSel=Math.min(list.length-1, codexSel+1);
    if(k==='a'||k==='ArrowLeft')  codexSel=Math.max(0, codexSel-CODEX_ROWS);
    if(k==='d'||k==='ArrowRight') codexSel=Math.min(list.length-1, codexSel+CODEX_ROWS);
    if('012345'.includes(k)){ codexTier=+k; codexSel=0; codexScroll=0; }
    if(k==='Escape'||k==='q'||k==='Q'){ mode=codexFrom; menuSel=0; }
    return;
  }

  /* ---- THE TOWN ---- */
  if(mode===S.TOWN){
    const rows=townRows();
    if('1234'.includes(k)) { townTab=+k-1; townSel=0; forgePick=[]; }
    if(k==='w'||k==='ArrowUp')   townSel=rows? (townSel+rows-1)%rows : 0;
    if(k==='s'||k==='ArrowDown') townSel=rows? (townSel+1)%rows : 0;
    if(k==='Enter'||k===' '){
      if(townTab===1 && canForge() && forgePick.length===3) forge();
      else if(rows) townAct(townSel);
    }
    if(k==='f'||k==='F'){ if(townTab===1) forge(); }   // F also forges, since the stall says so
    if(k==='Escape'||k==='q'||k==='Q'){ mode=S.FARM; placePlayerSafely(TILE*3.4, 10*TILE); }
    return;
  }

  /* ---- HOW WILL YOU GO DOWN? ---- */
  if(mode===S.MODE){
    if(k==='w'||k==='ArrowUp')   modeSel=(modeSel+MODES.length-1)%MODES.length;
    if(k==='s'||k==='ArrowDown') modeSel=(modeSel+1)%MODES.length;
    if(k==='Enter'||k===' ')     pickMode(modeSel);
    if(k==='Escape')             toTitle();
    return;
  }

  /* ---- BETWEEN HOUSES ---- */
  if(mode===S.HOUSE){
    if(k==='Enter'||k===' ') nextHouse();
    return;
  }

  /* ---- CHARACTER SELECT ---- */
  if(mode===S.SELECT){
    if(k==='a'||k==='ArrowLeft')  selIdx=(selIdx+CHARS.length-1)%CHARS.length;
    if(k==='d'||k==='ArrowRight') selIdx=(selIdx+1)%CHARS.length;
    if(k==='Enter'||k===' ')      pickHero(selIdx);
    if(k==='Escape')              toTitle();
    return;
  }

  /* ---- ANY MENU SCREEN: title, pause, death, win ---- */
  const menu=currentMenu();
  if(menu){
    if(k==='w'||k==='ArrowUp')   menuSel=(menuSel+menu.length-1)%menu.length;
    if(k==='s'||k==='ArrowDown') menuSel=(menuSel+1)%menu.length;
    if(k==='Enter'||k===' ')     menu[menuSel].go();
    if(mode===S.PAUSE && (k==='Escape'||k==='p')) resumeGame();
    return;                                            // swallow everything else
  }
  if(k==='Escape' && canPause()){ pauseGame(); return; }
  if(k==='q'){ drinkPotion(); return; }           // drink a potion
  if(k==='u'){ unstick(true); return; }           // manual escape hatch, always available
  if(k==='F3'){ DIAG=!DIAG; return; }             // diagnostics overlay
  if(k==='r') newGame();
  if(k==='g') god=!god;
  if(k==='k'){
    if(mode===S.SKILL) mode=prevMode;
    else if(mode===S.FARM){ prevMode=mode; mode=S.SKILL; }
    else say('SKILL WEB ONLY AT THE FARM');
  }
  if(k==='f'||k==='F') toggleFullscreen();
  if('1234'.includes(k)) P.tool=+k;
  if(k==='e') pickUp();                                  // <-- E takes the item
  if(k==='Shift' && !K.has('Shift')) tryDash();
  if(k==='j'&&(mode===S.HALL||mode===S.BOSS)){ foes=[]; boss=null; doorOpen=true; }
  K.add(k);
});
addEventListener('keyup',e=>K.delete(norm(e)));

/* mouse: aim + fire */
function toWorld(e){
  /* Map the pointer through the canvas's CSS box, NOT through `scale`.
     clientX/Y arrive in CSS pixels; `scale` is now measured in DEVICE pixels
     (so that the pixel grid stays exact on HiDPI screens). On a dpr=2 display
     those two differ by a factor of two, and dividing by `scale` would have put
     every shot at half the distance from the crosshair. Going through
     rect.width is dpr-agnostic: it is correct at any scale, on any screen. */
  const r=screen.getBoundingClientRect();
  const sx = r.width  ? SCREEN_W / r.width  : 1;
  const sy = r.height ? SCREEN_H / r.height : 1;
  return { x: clamp((e.clientX-r.left)*sx, 0, SCREEN_W),
           y: clamp((e.clientY-r.top )*sy, 0, SCREEN_H) };
}
screen.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
  const w=toWorld(e); P.aimX=w.x; P.aimY=w.y;
  if(mode===S.CODEX){ const h=codexHit(w.x,w.y);
    if(h && h.row!==undefined && h.row<codexList().length) codexSel=h.row; return; }
  if(mode===S.TOWN){ const h=townHit(w.x,w.y); if(h && h.row!==undefined) townSel=h.row; return; }
  if(mode===S.MODE){ const i=modeHit(w.x,w.y); if(i>=0) modeSel=i; return; }
  if(mode===S.SELECT){ const h=selectHit(w.x,w.y); if(h>=0) selIdx=h; return; }
  const i=menuHit(w.x,w.y);          // works on title, pause, death and win alike
  if(i>=0) menuSel=i;
});
screen.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{
  e.preventDefault();                                    // <-- RMB drops from a slot
  const w=toWorld(e);
  const i=invSlotAt(w.x,w.y);
  if(i>=0) dropSlot(i);
});
screen.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
  Music.unlock();
  if(e.button!==0) return;
  const w=toWorld(e); P.aimX=w.x; P.aimY=w.y;

  /* the cinematic: click anywhere to advance, or hit SKIP */
  if(mode===S.INTRO){
    if(w.x>VIEW_W-70 && w.y<18) cineSkip(); else cineNext();
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.CODEX){
    const h=codexHit(w.x,w.y);
    if(!h) return;
    if(h.tier!==undefined){ codexTier=h.tier; codexSel=0; codexScroll=0; return; }
    if(h.row<codexList().length) codexSel=h.row;
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.TOWN){
    const h=townHit(w.x,w.y);
    if(!h) return;
    if(h.tab!==undefined){ townTab=h.tab; townSel=0; return; }
    townSel=h.row; townAct(h.row);
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.MODE){
    const i=modeHit(w.x,w.y);
    if(i>=0){ modeSel=i; pickMode(i); }
    return;
  }
  if(mode===S.HOUSE){ nextHouse(); return; }

  /* character select: click a bloodline to take it */
  if(mode===S.SELECT){
    const h=selectHit(w.x,w.y);
    if(h>=0){ selIdx=h; pickHero(h); }
    return;
  }
  /* any menu screen: click the row */
  const hit=menuHit(w.x,w.y);
  if(currentMenu()){
    if(hit>=0){ menuSel=hit; currentMenu()[hit].go(); }
    return;                                  // never fire a shot through a menu
  }
  if(mode===S.SKILL){
    if(w.x>=SKILL_CLOSE.X && w.x<SKILL_CLOSE.X+SKILL_CLOSE.W &&
       w.y>=SKILL_CLOSE.Y && w.y<SKILL_CLOSE.Y+SKILL_CLOSE.H){ mode=prevMode; return; }
    skillClick(w.x,w.y); return;
  }
  P.firing=true;
  P.cool=Math.min(P.cool,0);      // first shot is instant, not on the next tick
});
addEventListener('mouseup',e=>{ if(e.button===0) P.firing=false; });

function skillClick(mx,my){
  if(pts<=0) return;
  for(let r=0;r<5;r++)for(let c=0;c<3;c++){
    const X=30+c*196, Y=56+r*48;
    if(mx>=X&&mx<X+176&&my>=Y&&my<Y+36){
      const locked = r>0 && !(spent[r-1][0]||spent[r-1][1]||spent[r-1][2]);
      if(spent[r][c]||locked) return;
      spent[r][c]=1; pts--;
      const before=stats.maxHp;
      recalcStats();
      P.hp+=Math.max(0,stats.maxHp-before);          // new heart containers come full
      P.dashN=Math.min(dashMax(),P.dashN+ (stats.dashCh?1:0));
      say(`TAKEN: ${TREES[tree].rows[r][c][0]}`);
      return;
    }
  }
}

/* ---- BOOT ------------------------------------------------------------- */
/* Build a game state so every draw call has something to read, then roll the
   opening. If a run is already in progress we skip straight to the title —
   nobody wants the lore again on their fourth attempt.

   This MUST be beginRun(), not newGame(). newGame() now opens the character
   select, which deliberately does NOT build a world — so booting through it
   left foes/shots/drops undefined and the very first render threw. */
beginRun();
mode=S.INTRO; cineI=0; cineT=0; cinePT=0;
Store.get(SAVE_KEY).then(raw=>{
  hasSave=!!raw;
  if(hasSave && mode===S.INTRO) mode=S.TITLE;
});
/* Without this the meta is written and never read: THE RETURN would unlock, be
   saved, and then be forgotten on the next boot. */
loadMeta();

/* Optional: play a video instead of the in-engine opening. */
if(VIDEO_INTRO){
  mode=S.TITLE;
  const v=document.createElement('video');
  v.src=VIDEO_INTRO; v.autoplay=true; v.playsInline=true;
  Object.assign(v.style,{position:'fixed',inset:'0',width:'100%',height:'100%',
    objectFit:'contain',background:'#000',zIndex:'9999',cursor:'pointer'});
  const kill=()=>{ v.remove(); };
  v.onended=kill; v.onclick=kill;
  addEventListener('keydown',e=>{ if(e.key==='Escape'||e.key===' ') kill(); });
  document.body.appendChild(v);
}

let last=performance.now();
let errCount=0;
function frame(t){
  const dt=Math.min((t-last)/1000,.05); last=t;
  /* An uncaught throw inside update() or render() used to kill the RAF chain
     PERMANENTLY — the game would just stop, with no error visible to anyone
     not looking at devtools. That's indistinguishable from a freeze. Now a bad
     frame is survivable: log it, skip it, keep running. */
  fpsN++; fpsT+=dt; if(fpsT>=.5){ fps=Math.round(fpsN/fpsT); fpsN=0; fpsT=0; }
  try{ update(dt); }
  catch(e){ errCount++; lastErr='update: '+e.message; console.error('update() threw:',e);
            if(errCount<4) say('ENGINE HICCUP — RECOVERED (press F3)'); }
  try{ render(); }
  catch(e){ errCount++; lastErr='render: '+e.message; console.error('render() threw:',e); }
  requestAnimationFrame(frame);
}
requestAnimationFrame(frame);
</script>
</body>
</html>
